# Project Context

## 1. Project Overview
SwipeClean (display name "SwipeClean"; Android package `com.cognitix.swipeclean`, slug `swipeclean-free`) is an Expo/React Native mobile app for cleaning a device media library. The product direction is a mobile-first photo/video cleanup utility: users grant media-library access, review media with a swipe-based keep/delete flow, queue items for deletion, permanently delete selected items after confirmation, inspect storage stats, and compress large photos/videos to save space.

The app targets **both Android and iOS with feature parity** (scope change 2026-06-29; it was Android-first before). The repository now tracks BOTH generated native projects: `android/` (Kotlin, foreground-service permissions, background compression worker) and `ios/` (Swift AppDelegate, `SwipeClean.xcodeproj`/`.xcworkspace`, regenerated via `npx expo prebuild -p ios`; `ios/Pods/` and `ios/build/` are gitignored). Platform-specific features are built as local Expo native modules under `modules/` (Swift + Kotlin behind one JS API), each capability-probed at runtime so a platform where the module isn't built yet degrades gracefully instead of crashing.

There is no custom backend, database server, or authentication provider in the current codebase. State is local to the app and persisted on-device through AsyncStorage. Subscription validation is delegated to RevenueCat's hosted service and the native app-store billing SDKs.

## 2. Tech Stack
- Mobile framework: Expo SDK 54, React Native 0.81.5, React 19.1.0.
- Routing/navigation: Expo Router 6 with file-based routes under `app/`.
- Language/build: TypeScript 5.9 with strict mode, Metro, Babel preset Expo, React Native Reanimated Babel plugin.
- State management: Zustand 5 with `persist` middleware and `@react-native-async-storage/async-storage`.
- Media access/storage: `expo-media-library` for permissions, asset reads, asset creation, and deletion.
- Image rendering/cache: `expo-image`.
- Video playback: `expo-video`.
- Compression: `react-native-compressor` for image/video compression, video thumbnails, and file-size reads.
- Media-format conversion (Convert/"Studio", Pro): `expo-image-manipulator` (image jpg/png/webp + HEIC/GIF-frame decode), `react-native-compressor` (video→MP4/H.264), and three local Expo native modules — `SwipeCleanAudioEncode` (video→MP3/M4A/WAV; bundles **vendored LAME 3.100, LGPL**, compiled per-platform: Android CMake/NDK → `libswipecleanlame.so`, iOS C sources inside the pod), `SwipeCleanWebm` (Android-only video→WebM/VP8 via MediaCodec + EGL surface bridge + `MUXER_OUTPUT_WEBM`), `SwipeCleanGif` (iOS-only video→animated GIF via AVAssetImageGenerator + ImageIO). Plus the legacy `SwipeCleanAudioExtract` (M4A remux) kept as an m4a fallback. All are runtime-capability-probed; no FFmpeg anywhere.
- Background work/notifications: `react-native-background-actions` (RNBA) for the Android foreground service — a HARD SINGLETON shared by compression and the Smart Clean scan; `expo-notifications` for progress/completion/failure notifications.
- Localization (i18n): `i18next` + `react-i18next` + `expo-localization`. 10 languages (en, es, pt-BR, fr, de, it, id, hi, ar, ja); `en.json` is the canonical key source; parity enforced by `npm run i18n:check`.
- Smart Clean detection (Pro): perceptual hashing/blur pipeline via `expo-image-manipulator` + `jpeg-js` (downscale → decode → grayscale → dHash / variance-of-Laplacian); exact-duplicate MD5 via `expo-file-system` (legacy). Both are gated by a runtime native-capability probe (`requireOptionalNativeModule` from `expo-modules-core`) so absent native modules degrade to "not available" instead of crashing.
- Other Expo modules: `expo-haptics`, `expo-sharing`, `expo-file-system`, `expo-constants`, `expo-linking`.
- Security/app-lock: `expo-local-authentication` (Face ID/Touch ID/fingerprint) + `expo-secure-store` (Keystore-backed 4-digit passcode), both runtime-capability-probed by native-module name so they degrade safely until a native rebuild. Recurring reminder notifications use the existing `expo-notifications`.
- Gestures/animation: `react-native-gesture-handler`, React Native `Animated`, `react-native-reanimated`.
- UI/icons: React Native inline styles, `lucide-react-native`, `expo-linear-gradient`, `react-native-svg` (also used for the brand logo `AppLogo` and the adaptive launcher icon).
- Subscription/feature-gating: a Free-vs-Pro feature-access layer (`src/features/subscription/`) backed by RevenueCat (`react-native-purchases`) for Play Store / App Store / Test Store subscriptions. Pro is granted ONLY by an active subscription (`subscriptionStatus === "active"`) — the former `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO` build flag and the `debugProOverride` developer toggle were removed (2026-06-12).
- Native Android: generated Expo/React Native Android project with Kotlin `MainActivity`/`MainApplication`, Hermes, New Architecture enabled.
- Testing/quality gates: `tsc --noEmit` (typecheck), `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (locale key/placeholder parity), **Vitest** (`npm test`) unit tests for the pure modules (feature-access service, cleanup-report selectors, perceptual hash-utils), **ESLint 9** flat config (`npm run lint`, eslint-config-expo; the new React-Compiler rules are "warn", `rules-of-hooks` is an error), and a **Maestro** UI/E2E suite (`.maestro/`, run with `maestro test .maestro` against a device/emulator). Deeper review is still done by ad-hoc multi-agent passes.
- Ads: Google AdMob via `react-native-google-mobile-ads` (`src/features/ads/`) — banner + interstitial + **rewarded** (Free users watch one to unlock a single video compression), Pro-gated, TEST ads in dev / real ad units in release (rewarded uses a TEST-id placeholder until a real unit exists). A shared full-screen-ad cooldown (`full-screen-ad-gate.ts`) prevents back-to-back interstitial/rewarded ads.
- Third-party services: EAS project metadata is configured; RevenueCat is wired client-side for subscriptions and AdMob is wired for ads. Feedback and bug reporting open pre-addressed email drafts; analytics remains unimplemented.

## 3. Project Structure
- `app/`: Expo Router route files. Most route files are thin exports that mount screen components from `src/screens/`.
- `app/_layout.tsx`: Root layout. Sets up gesture root, status bar, stack navigation, photo-library sync, i18n preference sync, RevenueCat subscription sync, compression lifecycle/notifications/completion sheet, the global Pro upgrade sheet (`ProUpgradeSheet`), the global Smart Clean review sheet (`SmartCleanReviewSheet`, mounted ABOVE the tab navigator so it overlays the tab bar), the Smart Clean scan-resume sync + stop-notification listener.
- `app/(tabs)/`: Bottom tab shell. Four tabs: Swipe (`index`, `Layers` icon), Compress (`history`, `Archive` icon — formerly "Cleanup"), Stats (`stats`, `BarChart3`), and Premium/Smart Clean (`premium`; label+icon switch to "Smart Clean"/`Wand2` for Pro, "Premium"/`Star` for Free — same route either way).
- `src/screens/`: Feature screens for swiping, cleanup/compression (`history-screen`), stats, premium (Free upsell / Pro → Studio), settings, selected media, deletion review, media preview, largest photos, compression detail, compression media viewer, `smart-clean-screen`, the Pro `studio-screen` (swipeable, animated `[ Clean | Convert ]` pager), and the Convert flow (`convert-screen`, `convert-run-screen`, `convert-batch-run-screen`).
- `src/store/app-store.ts`: Main persisted application state (`swipeclean-free-store`) for permissions, selected filters, reviewed IDs, deletion queue, compressed results, deletion history, stats, settings, and core actions. `permanentlyDeleteMarked(ids, opts)` is the single audited deletion path (used by review-delete AND Smart Clean). Gallery asset metadata delegates to the media index store.
- `src/store/media-index-store.ts`: Persisted source-of-truth index for device media (`swipeclean-media-index-store`). Indexed assets by ID, sorted IDs, compressibility estimates, global savings summary, scan cursors/timestamps, and persisted `accessLevel` for full↔limited reconciliation. Uses a debounced custom `PersistStorage` + identity-stable merges so idle polls do no work.
- `src/store/subscription-store.ts` / `src/store/paywall-store.ts`: Subscription snapshot (`swipeclean-subscription-store`, RevenueCat billing state, plans, local Test Store cancel state) and the tiny non-persisted paywall open/close store.
- `src/store/cleanup-events-store.ts`: Append-only cleanup-events ledger (`swipeclean-cleanup-events-store`, EVENT_CAP 2000) powering Advanced Stats; pure selectors live in `src/features/advanced-stats/`.
- `src/features/compression/`: Compression module — persisted store (`swipeclean-compression-store`, debounced), queue, job types, selectors, deletion helpers. The single-item foreground flow lives in `src/screens/compress-run-screen.tsx` (see Feature History 2026-06-15). The batch engine and background-notification machinery were removed in the 2026-06-15 redesign. The current result screen links to `compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx` for a full-screen, zoomable Compressed/Original comparison; `components/compression-result-sheet.tsx` remains legacy code used only by older result-mode entry points. The Compress FILTER is a native form-sheet (`/compress-filter` → `src/components/compress-filter-sheet.tsx`) backed by `compress-filter.store.ts` (month + media type) and the shared `use-compressible-pool.ts` hook (one source for the grid + sheet month list).
- `src/features/subscription/`: Single source of truth for Free vs Pro - `feature-flags.ts` (FREE/PRO arrays + `FEATURE_TIER`; `compressAll` is Pro), pure `feature-access.service.ts` (`isProUser` = `subscriptionStatus === "active"` only — no build-flag or debug unlock), RevenueCat adapter (`revenuecat.service.ts`), `use-feature-access`/`use-ads-visibility` hooks.
- `src/features/ads/`: Google AdMob layer — `ad-config.ts` (banner/interstitial/rewarded unit IDs; test units when `__DEV__ || EXPO_PUBLIC_ADS_USE_TEST==="1"`, real per-platform units otherwise — see the release kill-switch caveat in §10), `interstitial.service.ts` (preload + Pro gate), `rewarded.service.ts` (`showForReward()` opt-in, used for free video compression), `full-screen-ad-gate.ts` (shared cooldown so interstitial + rewarded never stack), and `consent.service.ts` (GDPR/UMP `AdsConsent.gatherConsent()`, **fail-CLOSED** since 2026-07-13, run before SDK init). Banner renders via `src/components/ad-banner.tsx`; SDK init + ad preloads live in `app/_layout.tsx`. Free-video daily quota persists in `src/features/compression/free-video-quota.store.ts`.
- `src/features/smart-clean/`: Smart Clean (Pro). 8 real detectors in `detectors/` (tier0 large, tier1 screenshots + md5 duplicate photos, tier2 similar/blurry/duplicate-videos pixel pipeline, tier3 memes heuristic), with all tunable knobs centralized in `detectors/thresholds.ts`; `smart-clean.service.ts` (display order `SMART_CLEAN_DETECTORS` + cheap-first `SMART_CLEAN_SCAN_ORDER`); the PERSISTED runner `smart-clean-store.ts` (durable foreground-service scan + checkpoint/auto-resume); persisted per-asset `feature-cache-store.ts` (md5/dHash/blurVar/vHash, debounced, FIFO 8000); `native-capabilities.ts`/`file-system-capability.ts` (capability probes); `smart-clean-notifications.ts` (degraded-mode expo notification + Stop); `smart-clean-review-store.ts` + `components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` (virtualized review grid); `smart-clean-card.tsx`.
- `src/features/advanced-stats/`: Cleanup event types + pure O(n) report selectors (weekly/monthly/storage-trend/history) + the Pro `advanced-stats-section` and Free locked card.
- `src/features/convert/`: Pro media-format converter (the "Convert" half of the Studio tab). Native-import-free, **source-format-aware** target resolution (`convert-targets.ts`: `sourceFormat()` normalizes the file's extension with aliases jpeg/jpe/jfif→jpg, heif→heic, qt→mov; `getAvailableTargets` NEVER offers the source's own format back — no PNG→PNG/MP4→MP4. Targets: image→jpg/png/webp, video→mp4/webm/gif + audio mp3/m4a/wav; a GIF source gets still-image targets only). `ConvertCapabilities` is per-engine (`audioM4a`/`audioMp3`/`audioWav`/`webm`/`gif`), each flag set by a runtime native-module probe so a format chip appears only where its engine is compiled in. Persisted serial-FIFO job runner (`convert.store.ts`, key `swipeclean-convert-store`, debounced, format-agnostic), `convert.service.ts` (gallery-save for image/video — hard failure; **best-effort `saveAudioToLibrary`** puts audio in the Android media store so players can open it, iOS keeps sandbox+Share), pure selectors/utils, non-persisted `custom-convert.store.ts`, and the engine seam (`engine/conversion-engine.ts` dispatches `convertMedia(...)` → `image-manipulator-engine` | `video-engine` (mp4) | `webm-engine` | `gif-engine` | `audio-engine`; the audio engine prefers `SwipeCleanAudioEncode` and falls back to legacy `SwipeCleanAudioExtract` for m4a). Callers only use `convertMedia(...)`/`getConvertCapabilities()`. UI in `components/` (`convert-format-sheet.tsx` bottom sheet; the old `convert-format-picker.tsx` was deleted). Pure layers unit-tested. No FFmpeg. See `CONVERT_PLAN.md` for the phase ledger.
- `modules/swipe-clean-audio-encode/`: Local Expo module (`SwipeCleanAudioEncode`, apple + android) — video→**MP3/M4A/WAV**. JS API: sync `supportsMp3()` probe + `encodeAudio(inputUri, outputPath, format)`. Android: m4a = lossless AAC remux (MediaExtractor→MediaMuxer); wav = MediaCodec decode→16-bit PCM + RIFF header; mp3 = the decoded PCM through JNI (`lame_jni.c` `nativeEncode`, 192 kbps CBR joint-stereo) into **vendored LAME 3.100** compiled by `android/CMakeLists.txt` into `libswipecleanlame.so` (loaded via `System.loadLibrary`; load failure ⇒ `supportsMp3()` false, chip hidden). iOS: m4a = `AVAssetExportSession` AppleM4A; wav = `AVAssetReader` PCM (44.1 kHz stereo) + RIFF; mp3 = ObjC `SCLameEncoder` streams AVAssetReader PCM into LAME. **The LAME tree exists TWICE by design** — `lame/` (Android, referenced as `../lame`) and a byte-identical `ios/lame/` (the podspec must stay self-contained: `../` paths shift CocoaPods' base dir and silently break `public_header_files`). `lame/config.h` is hand-written and load-bearing: it typedefs `ieee754_float32_t/64_t` (LAME 3.100 ships no fallback) and deliberately does NOT define `HAVE_NASM`/`HAVE_IEEE754_FLOAT32_T`/`HAVE_XMMINTRIN_H` (defining HAVE_NASM even as 0 links nonexistent x86 asm). LGPL v2 `COPYING` is vendored in both copies.
- `modules/swipe-clean-webm/`: Local Expo module (`SwipeCleanWebm`, **Android-only**) — video→WebM. JS API: `toWebm(inputUri, outputPath)`. Full MediaCodec decode→encode transcode to VP8 over an EGL surface bridge (`InputSurface`/`OutputSurface`/`TextureRender`, the CTS/Grafika pattern) muxed with `MediaMuxer(MUXER_OUTPUT_WEBM)` — pure platform APIs, no vendored libs. **v1 is VIDEO-ONLY** (the source audio track is dropped; WebM can't carry AAC — Opus re-encode is a follow-up). First video track only; fps clamped 1–60; bitrate from source or `w*h*3` clamped 1–20 Mbps; no progress events or cancellation. iOS WebM would need vendored libvpx+libwebm — deferred/not approved; the JS probe hides the chip on iOS.
- `modules/swipe-clean-gif/`: Local Expo module (`SwipeCleanGif`, **iOS-only**) — video→animated GIF. JS API: `videoToGif(inputUri, outputPath)`. AVAssetImageGenerator frame sampling + ImageIO `CGImageDestination`(UTType.gif). Caps: first **15 s** @ **10 fps**, ≤**150 frames**, ≤**480 px**, infinite loop, uniform frame delay. Android GIF needs a vendored encoder (no platform GIF encoder) — blocked on vendoring approval; the probe hides the chip on Android.
- `modules/swipe-clean-audio-extract/`: LEGACY local Expo module (`SwipeCleanAudioExtract`, apple + android) extracting a video's audio track to M4A (Android lossless AAC remux; iOS AVAssetExportSession AppleM4A). Kept as the m4a fallback path in `audio-engine.ts`; `SwipeCleanAudioEncode` supersedes it. GOTCHA (applies to ALL local modules): a module's `android/build.gradle` MUST set `versionCode`/`versionName` or it cascades into a misleading async-storage `compileSdk` error.
- `src/i18n/`: `index.ts` (i18next init + `applyLanguagePreference`, RTL), `languages.ts`, and `locales/*.json` (10 files, parity-checked).
- `src/services/`: Native/service layer — media-library access, permissions, compression, the compression `background-compression-worker` AND the Smart Clean `background-smart-clean-scan-worker` (both wrap RNBA), notifications, deletion-queue/history mapping, stats, image prefetch, settings. (Ads now live in `src/features/ads/`; the old placeholder `ads-service.ts` was removed.)
- `src/components/`: Reusable UI — `app-logo` (SVG brand mark), app header, cached image, swipe card, month selector, `photo-grid` (memoized `PhotoTile` + `getPhotoTileSize`, used by the virtualized Marked-for-Deletion list), stats cards, settings rows, premium card, ad banner (self-hides for Pro), deletion confirmation, `pro-upgrade-sheet`, compression notification wrapper, video player, the custom `animated-tab-bar` (sliding accent highlight pill + selection haptic), and the gallery multi-select pieces (`gallery-month-scrubber`, `selection-action-bar`).
- `src/hooks/`: `useAppTheme` and `usePhotoLibrarySync`.
- `src/theme/`: Light/dark theme palettes and accent colors.
- `src/utils/`: Date (i18n-aware, incl. `formatWeekdayDay` "Mon 24"), byte/resolution formatting, month/media filtering, `debounced-storage.ts` (shared debounced `StateStorage`), and `gallery-grid.ts` (native-free gallery geometry, unit-tested: `buildGalleryLayout`/`hitTestSectionedIndex`/`photoIndexAtOffset` power the month-sectioned grid + scrubber; legacy `buildMonthSpans`/`hitTestGridIndex`/`rangeIndices` retained).
- `src/models/photo.ts`: Shared domain types for media assets, settings, stats, deletion history, compression, permissions, and filters.
- `scripts/check-i18n.mjs`: Validates every locale against `en.json` for key + interpolation-placeholder parity (`npm run i18n:check`).
- `.maestro/`: Maestro UI/E2E flow files (`*.yaml`) + `config.yaml` + `README.md`. Tracked; the Maestro binary/screenshots are not (see `.gitignore`).
- `android/`: Generated native Android project. Media/notification/foreground-service permissions, the RNBA service declaration, and an adaptive launcher icon (`mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher*.xml` + `drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml`).
- `ios/`: Generated native iOS project (tracked since 2026-06-29; regenerate with `npx expo prebuild -p ios` if broken). Swift `AppDelegate`, `SwipeClean.xcodeproj` + `.xcworkspace`, `Podfile` (+ `Podfile.properties.json`: Hermes, dev-client network inspector), `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy`, entitlements. `ios/Pods/` and `ios/build/` are gitignored (root `.gitignore` + the CocoaPods-generated `ios/.gitignore`).
- `CONVERT_PLAN.md`: The Convert feature's cross-platform architecture + phase/status ledger (which formats are live/verified/blocked per platform). Keep it updated as phases land.
- `dist/`, `dist-android/`, `.expo/`, `node_modules/`, logs, and screenshots: generated/local artifacts ignored by git.

## 4. Current Implemented Features
- Media permission and library loading
  - What it does: Requests/checks photo and video permissions, quickly refreshes the newest media page, maps native assets into persisted indexed assets, estimates size when file size is unavailable, prefetches images, and starts the first cooperative full-library scan only when no saved full estimate exists yet.
  - Main files: `src/services/permission-service.ts`, `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`, `src/services/image-cache-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`, `src/hooks/use-photo-library-sync.ts`.
  - Status: complete but native-permission behavior needs ongoing device testing. The media index is the source of truth for gallery assets.

- Swipe review
  - What it does: Shows a stacked swipe card. Right swipe keeps media, left swipe marks it for deletion. Tracks reviewed IDs, deletion marks, undo, selected month/media filters, and progress for the active indexed scope. When a scope has media but no swipeable cards left, it shows a completed-state panel with Review Delete List, Start Over, and Next Month/Choose Month actions.
  - Main files: `src/screens/swipe-screen.tsx`, `src/components/swipe-photo-card.tsx`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`, `src/utils/months.ts`.
  - Status: complete for core flow.

- Month and media-type filtering
  - What it does: Lets users choose all media/photos/videos and filter by month from the persisted media index; displays per-scope counts, bytes, and cleanup progress.
  - Main files: `src/components/month-selector.tsx`, `src/components/month-selector-bottom-sheet.tsx`, `src/utils/months.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`.
  - Status: complete.

- Selected media grid ("All Media" gallery)
  - What it does: Shows the current scope's media — EXCLUDING items already queued for deletion — as a dense, edge-to-edge `FlashList` grouped into **month sections** (a text header per month + that month's square tiles; responsive `numColumns = clamp(round(listW/100), 3, 6)`). A quick TAP opens the previewer (normal mode) or toggles a tile (select mode); a LONG-PRESS-DRAG enters multi-select (Medium haptic, `SelectionActionBar`: X exit / live pluralized count / Trash) and paints a range — a stroke that starts on a selected tile DESELECTS, otherwise selects — with JS `setInterval` edge auto-scroll. The list **scrolls normally in select mode too**: the wrapper is `Gesture.Exclusive(pan.activateAfterLongPress(180), tap)` with NO `blocksExternalGesture` (that broke native scroll — see Feature History) and NOT composed with a `Gesture.Native()` (FlashList v2's scroll isn't represented by it); the pan only activates after a long press, and a `painting` state flag sets `scrollEnabled={false}` for the stroke so flicks scroll and press-drags paint. Trash batch-marks via `markManyForDeletion` (deferred Marked-for-Deletion queue, NOT an immediate OS delete) which then removes them from this grid; hardware Back exits select mode. A right-edge fast-scroll scrubber (shown whenever the list scrolls) drags to scroll (driving scroll via `onScrubbingChange`→`scrollEnabled=false`) and pops a date bubble — full date for "All Media", weekday+day ("Mon 24") for a single-month scope.
  - Main files: `src/screens/selected-photos-screen.tsx`, `src/components/{gallery-month-scrubber,selection-action-bar,cached-image,media-thumbnail}.tsx`, `src/utils/{gallery-grid.ts,date.ts}`, `src/store/app-store.ts` (`markManyForDeletion`), `src/services/stats-service.ts` (`withSwipeBatch`).
  - Status: complete; green typecheck/vitest/i18n/lint. Month sections, the date scrubber bubble, per-month grouping, normal scrolling, and the select-mode transform-crash fix are device-verified (Galaxy S24 dev client). The reworked select-mode gesture feel (scroll-while-selecting, long-press-paint, paint-deselect) and the single-month "Mon 24" label are implemented but pending a final on-device pass. See Feature History 2026-06-29.

- Deletion review and permanent delete ("Marked for Deletion" screen)
  - What it does: Displays marked items grouped by month in a compact 3-column grid, allows restoring from the marked queue, confirms deletion, calls `MediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync`, removes deleted assets from state, records deletion history, and updates stats. The screen is a SINGLE virtualized `FlatList` over a flattened header/tile-row model with incremental pagination (`PAGE_SIZE` 60 items, grown via `onEndReached`), so opening the screen with hundreds/thousands of marked items no longer mounts every thumbnail at once (see Feature History 2026-06-14 "Marked for Deletion perf"). Month section headers show FULL per-month count/bytes even while a month is only partially paged in; the Delete button always operates on the entire marked scope, not just the loaded page.
  - Main files: `src/screens/review-delete-list-screen.tsx`, `src/components/photo-grid.tsx` (exports the memoized single-tile `PhotoTile` + `getPhotoTileSize`), `src/components/media-thumbnail.tsx`, `src/components/delete-confirmation-dialog.tsx`, `src/services/deletion-queue-service.ts`, `src/services/history-service.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`.
  - Status: complete but destructive native deletion is high-risk and should be tested on real devices/emulators.

- Media preview
  - What it does: Opens photo/video preview from library, marked queue, or history. Photos support pinch/pan zoom and tap chrome toggle. Videos play in-app, loop by default, and expose custom play/pause controls in the preview chrome.
  - Main files: `src/screens/photo-preview-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx`, `src/components/video-media-player.tsx`, `src/components/cached-image.tsx`.
  - Status: complete for core in-app photo/video preview; needs real-device codec/URI testing.

- Storage stats and swipe distribution
  - What it does: Shows total indexed bytes, cleared bytes, indexed count, and marked count as 4 stat cards, followed by an SVG pie chart of swipe distribution (Kept / Deleted / Restored) and the Advanced Stats section. As of 2026-06-15 the Stats screen no longer renders a "Largest Photos" preview section (the previews + "View all" link were removed); the standalone Largest Photos screen/route was subsequently removed entirely (see the 2026-06-15 cleanup entry). The swipe-distribution chart was rewritten into a `react-native-svg` pie + legend with a dedicated empty state (`ChartPie` icon + `distribution.emptyTitle`/`emptyMessage`) and dropped the Favorite/Missed categories (`distribution.favorite`/`missed` keys are now orphans; the `AppStats` fields remain in `models/photo.ts` but are unused here).
  - Main files: `src/screens/stats-screen.tsx`, `src/components/stats-card.tsx`, `src/components/swipe-distribution-chart.tsx`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`.
  - Status: partial. Stats are local/session-derived.

- Compression cleanup
  - What it does: Identifies compressible large photos/videos from the shared media index, shows a persisted global estimated savings total, provides an Estimate now/Estimate again action, queues single or batch compression jobs, compresses via native compressor, verifies output size (a re-encode whose output is NOT smaller than the original — common for already-efficient/low-bitrate videos — fails verification BEFORE saving, so a larger "compressed" copy is never written to the gallery; surfaced as an "already optimized" message), saves compressed copies to the media library, tracks completed/failed jobs, supports original-file decisions, and shows per-item overlays/viewers. The Compress screen renders a flat 3-up `FlatList` (no month sections) narrowed by a custom Filter dialog (month + Both/Photos/Videos). FREE-TIER MONETIZATION (2026-06-12): photo compression is unlimited; VIDEO compression requires a rewarded ad and is capped at 2/day (then routes to Premium) via a custom opt-in dialog that shows the remaining count; "Compress All" is Pro-only (Free users get an "Upgrade to Pro" button); a banner sits at the screen bottom and an interstitial fires after each compression. While a "Compress All" batch runs, the button becomes Pause/Resume Compression with a circular icon-only Stop button beside it (store `paused` flag + `pauseCompression`/`resumeCompression`/`stopCompression`; pause halts the queue AFTER the current item — images can't abort mid-encode — and stop cancels the queue + active job and tears down the foreground service). Pro = unlimited + ad-free. COMPRESS-ALL ORIGINAL PROMPT (2026-06-14, ANDROID ONLY): on Android, tapping "Compress All" now opens an UPFRONT dialog (`compress-all-original-dialog.tsx`) asking whether to delete the originals (after each file compresses) or keep them; the choice is stored on the batch as `originalPolicy` (`"delete" | "keep" | "ask"`) and auto-applied when the batch finishes via `refreshBatchSummary` (calling `deleteAllOriginals`/`keepAllOriginals`), so the post-batch decision sheet (`CompressionCompleteSheet`) is SKIPPED for Compress All on Android. iOS is intentionally untouched (the iOS compress workflow is owned by a separate agent): `handleCompressAll` routes non-Android straight to `runCompressAll("ask")`, the dialog is not rendered, and iOS keeps the legacy enqueue → post-batch sheet flow. The store stays platform-agnostic (default `originalPolicy` `"ask"`), so the gating lives entirely in `history-screen.tsx`. ANDROID SINGLE-ITEM RESULT FLOW (2026-06-14): when a single (non-batch) compression finishes while the user is still on the detail screen, it auto-opens the media viewer in "result mode" — the compressed media plus a peek/expand bottom sheet (`compression-result-sheet.tsx`) with the before/after numbers and the original-file decision (Delete original / Keep / Delete compressed copy). The detail screen is REPLACED (not pushed-over) so it leaves the back stack; Close and Android back both return to the origin. The global `CompressionCompleteSheet` modal is therefore batch-only on Android (single + batch on iOS, which keeps the old flow).
  - Main files: `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/compression-detail-screen.tsx`, the Filter native form-sheet (`app/compress-filter.tsx` + `src/components/compress-filter-sheet.tsx` + `src/features/compression/{compress-filter.store,use-compressible-pool}.ts`), `src/components/video-compress-ad-dialog.tsx`, `src/features/compression/*` (incl. `free-video-quota.store.ts`), `src/features/ads/*`, `src/services/compression-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`.
  - Status: partial/experimental. Cleanup rendering is FlatList-virtualized while discovery and persisted estimates come from the media index. After the first saved estimate, the app does not repeatedly re-estimate on refresh; the user can manually run Estimate again from Cleanup. The Compress All action is disabled until a saved estimate/full scan exists so it represents the full indexed cleanup opportunity. Native compression, output verification, foreground service behavior, and original deletion decisions require real-device regression testing.

- Android foreground compression notifications
  - What it does: Requests notification permission, runs compression in an Android foreground service, throttles progress updates, and shows completion/failure notifications that deep-link back into the app.
  - Main files: `src/features/compression/compression.notifications.ts`, `src/services/background-compression-worker.ts`, `src/services/notification-service.ts`, `app/_layout.tsx`, `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`.
  - Status: partial/experimental. Android path is wired; iOS behavior is less complete because foreground-service logic is Android-specific.

- Settings
  - What it does: Persists app settings (dark mode, minimal accent-color picker, notification reminder toggles), a Language picker (10 languages + System default), a **real Account & Security** section (4-digit App Lock passcode via SecureStore + Face ID/Touch ID via LocalAuthentication, with a root-mounted lock screen that prompts ONLY on cold launch — not on every foreground), a **Permissions** section showing media + notification grants as tappable checkboxes, a **Notifications** section that schedules cleanup/compression/Pro reminders. (The dev "Pro Override" was removed in the 2026-06-12 override-removal change.) The Privacy section and the header check button were removed; the footer reads "SwipeClean by Cognitx team". See the 2026-06-11 Feature History entry.
  - Main files: `src/screens/settings-screen.tsx`, `src/components/{settings-row,passcode-pad,app-lock-gate}.tsx`, `src/services/{settings-service,app-lock-service,reminder-notification-service}.ts`, `src/hooks/use-app-theme.ts`, `src/theme/colors.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`, `src/store/subscription-store.ts`, `app/_layout.tsx`.
  - Status: implemented. App Lock + biometric are wired but their native modules (`expo-secure-store`/`expo-local-authentication`) require a native rebuild to activate (fail-open until then); reminders work on the current APK. Leave feedback and report a bug remain placeholders.

- Localization (i18n)
  - What it does: Full multi-language UI across every screen/component via `react-i18next`, with device-language detection, a Settings picker, RTL handling (Arabic), and i18n-aware date/noun helpers. `en.json` is canonical (528 keys as of 2026-06-27); all 10 locales are kept in parity (enforced by `npm run i18n:check`).
  - Main files: `src/i18n/*`, `src/utils/date.ts`, `src/utils/months.ts`, `scripts/check-i18n.mjs`.
  - Status: complete. RTL layout mirroring needs an app reload.

- Free vs Pro subscription / feature gating
  - What it does: Centralized feature-access layer. `feature-flags.ts` lists every Free and Pro feature; components gate with `canUseFeature(key)` and open a global paywall on denial. Ads self-hide for Pro. The Premium tab (redesigned 2026-06-12) shows Free users an animated upsell — a crown hero with a pulsing halo, a compact "What's included" Pro-feature showcase with staggered Reanimated entrances, and selectable Monthly/Yearly plan cards feeding one gradient CTA + Restore — and shows Pro users the Smart Clean screen.
  - Main files: `src/features/subscription/*`, `src/store/subscription-store.ts`, `src/store/paywall-store.ts`, `src/components/pro-upgrade-sheet.tsx`, `src/components/ad-banner.tsx`, `src/screens/premium-screen.tsx`.
  - Status: client-side RevenueCat billing is implemented and verified against the Test Store; real Play/App Store purchases still need dashboard products + a store sandbox. As of 2026-06-12 Pro is granted ONLY by an active subscription (`subscriptionStatus === "active"`) — the `__DEV__` debug override and the `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO` build unlock were REMOVED, so subscribe → cancel correctly reverts to Free. `compressAll` is a Pro feature (the Free batch action is replaced by an upgrade button).

- Smart Clean (Pro)
  - What it does: Scans the library with 8 real detectors — large photos/videos, screenshots, exact-duplicate photos (MD5), memes (heuristic), and the pixel-pipeline similar/blurry/duplicate-videos (perceptual dHash + variance-of-Laplacian). Shows per-category counts/reclaimable bytes, a One-Tap Recommendations aggregate, and a review→confirm→delete flow through the single audited deletion path with hard keeper protection. The scan runs in an Android foreground service (survives backgrounding), checkpoints continuously, and AUTO-RESUMES an interrupted scan on next launch; results persist across restarts. Scans cheapest-detector-first so fast categories surface in seconds. Per-asset hashes persist so re-scans are fast.
  - Main files: `src/features/smart-clean/*` (detectors, runner store, feature cache, capability probes, notifications, review sheet/store), `src/services/background-smart-clean-scan-worker.ts`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`.
  - Status: functional and device-verified. The Tier-2 pixel detectors need a native build with `expo-image-manipulator` (present on current dev/release APKs); detector thresholds (screenshot over-match, dHash/blur) want tuning on large libraries.

- Media-format Convert ("Studio", Pro)
  - What it does: Converts media to a different format inside the tab-4 "Studio" screen (swipeable animated `[ Clean | Convert ]` pager; Clean = Smart Clean). REDESIGNED 2026-06-29 as a per-item "workbench": an animated (Reanimated `FadeInDown`/`LinearTransition`) browse-dropzone morphs into a type-grouped staging list (Images / Video sections); each staged item shows a colored **source-format badge** (from `sourceFormatLabel`) and a tappable **"from → to" pill** opening the `ConvertFormatSheet` bottom sheet, which detects the source format, groups targets by output kind, and **hides the source's own format** ("same as source" note) — targets are chosen PER ITEM, not per media kind. Target matrix: image → jpg/png/webp (HEIC/HEIF and GIF-first-frame decode work as inputs via `expo-image-manipulator`); video → MP4 (react-native-compressor), **WebM** (Android-only module), **GIF** (iOS-only module), and audio-extract **MP3 / M4A / WAV** (`SwipeCleanAudioEncode` with vendored LAME for MP3). Each chip appears only where its engine is compiled in (per-engine `ConvertCapabilities` probes). Stage up to 5 files (`MAX_BATCH` 5): 1 item → `/convert-run` (foreground run screen with cancel-confirm modal), 2–5 → `/convert-batch` (serial FIFO drain, per-item status, cancel-all). "Recent converted" lists past results (view/share/remove). Conversion is NON-destructive; image/video outputs save to the gallery (hard failure if the save fails); audio saves best-effort to the device media store on Android (so music players can open it) and stays sandbox+Share on iOS. No "did it shrink?" check (a format change may grow the file). No FFmpeg.
  - Main files: `src/screens/studio-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/convert-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/convert-run-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/convert-batch-run-screen.tsx`, `src/features/convert/*` (types, targets, store, service, selectors, utils, `engine/*` incl. `webm-engine.ts` + `gif-engine.ts`, `components/convert-format-sheet.tsx`), `modules/swipe-clean-audio-encode/*`, `modules/swipe-clean-webm/*`, `modules/swipe-clean-gif/*`, `modules/swipe-clean-audio-extract/*` (legacy m4a fallback), `app/convert-run.tsx`, `app/convert-batch.tsx`, `CONVERT_PLAN.md`.
  - Status: Pro feature (Pro-only Studio tab + `"mediaFormatConvert"` in `PRO_FEATURES`). Phase 1 UI + same-format exclusion device-verified on the Galaxy S24. WAV/M4A verified building AND surfacing on both platforms; MP3 (LAME) builds verified on both (Android emulator + iPhone 17 Pro sim, 2026-06-29) with runtime encode QA pending; WebM Android compile-verified, runtime QA pending; GIF iOS code-complete, build unconfirmed. Parity gaps: WebM has no iOS side (needs libvpx — not approved), GIF has no Android side (needs a vendored encoder — not approved). Pure target/selector layers unit-tested (91 tests green).

- Animated bottom tab bar + page transition
  - What it does: Replaces the default tab bar with a custom `AnimatedTabBar` — a single accent highlight pill (50×28, radius 16) that springs horizontally (`{ damping: 22, stiffness: 240, mass: 0.7 }`) to the active tab via a Reanimated `translateX` shared value (first layout jumps, later changes spring); pill color is accent/dark-mode aware and tab selection fires `Haptics.selectionAsync()`. A "pop" scene transition in the tab layout makes the incoming screen rise (`translateY` 16→0), zoom (`scale` 0.9→1) and fade (`opacity` 0→1) over 220 ms with `Easing.out(Easing.cubic)`. The `premium` route is unchanged (label/icon swap to Studio/`Wand2` for Pro).
  - Main files: `src/components/animated-tab-bar.tsx`, `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx`.
  - Status: complete; the spring/haptic/transition feel is pending an on-device check.

- Advanced Stats (Pro)
  - What it does: A persisted append-only cleanup-events ledger drives weekly/monthly cleanup reports, a storage-reclaimed trend, and cleanup/compression history. The history lists are rendered (since 2026-06-15) as a `surfaceSoft` card of icon-badge rows — each row has a 36px circular tinted badge with a per-event-type lucide icon (`eventVisual()`), a two-line label+date stack, and a pill-shaped green bytes badge — with hairline row separators and an icon-badge empty state (`emptyIcon` prop). Free users see a locked card → paywall. Events are emitted only on terminal success paths (delete/compress/original-deleted/smart-clean-confirmed).
  - Main files: `src/store/cleanup-events-store.ts`, `src/features/advanced-stats/*`, `src/screens/stats-screen.tsx`.

- Brand and bottom navigation
  - What it does: `AppLogo` (SVG swipe-cards mark, follows the accent color) appears left-aligned in the app headers; the home-screen launcher uses an adaptive vector icon (white tile + the mark). Bottom tabs use distinct icons and the labels Swipe / Compress / Stats / Premium(Smart Clean).

- Ads (Google AdMob)
  - What it does: Renders a real AdMob `BannerAd` (anchored adaptive) for non-Pro users (review-delete success screen AND the Compress screen bottom), shows a frequency-capped interstitial at the delete-success break point AND after every compression, and a REWARDED ad that Free users watch to unlock a single video compression (2/day). A shared full-screen-ad cooldown (`full-screen-ad-gate.ts`) ensures interstitial + rewarded never stack. Pro/`noAds` users see no ads. Dev builds serve Google TEST ads; release builds use real units (rewarded is a TEST placeholder until a real unit is created).
