### 2026-06-09 - Free + Pro Subscription / Feature-Gating Model (Stage 2: Smart Clean + Advanced Stats)
- What was added: The Pro surfaces that build on the Stage 1 feature-access layer — a **Smart Clean** tab (Pro) and a **Advanced Stats** section (Pro) backed by a new cleanup-events ledger. Still NO real billing. Detection is intentionally dormant (honest placeholders, no fabricated/destructive results). Designed via a 3-architect design panel + synthesis, then adversarially reviewed (8 agents, 6 dimensions) which caught one real bug (fixed, see below).
- Advanced-stats data model:
  - `src/features/advanced-stats/cleanup-event.types.ts` — flat, primitive-only event types (`CleanupEvent`, `CleanupReport`, `StorageTrendBucket`). 6 event types: itemDeleted, itemCompressed, originalDeletedAfterCompression, compressionFailed, smartCleanSuggestionViewed, smartCleanActionConfirmed.
  - `src/store/cleanup-events-store.ts` — `useCleanupEventsStore` (persist key `swipeclean-cleanup-events-store`), append-only NEWEST-FIRST, **EVENT_CAP=2000** sliced on every `recordEvent` (no unbounded AsyncStorage growth), bulletproof `onRehydrateStorage` gate, `partialize:{events}` only. Exposes the imperative `recordCleanupEvent()` helper for non-React emission and **no** getReport()-style method (derivation is pure selectors only — avoids the Stage 1 fresh-object-selector crash).
  - `src/features/advanced-stats/cleanup-report.selectors.ts` — PURE O(n) selectors (`buildReport`/`buildWeeklyReport` 7d/`buildMonthlyReport` rolling 30d/`buildStorageTrend` fixed-length zero-filled weekly buckets/`buildCleanupHistory`/`buildCompressionHistory`) with `?? 0`/`?? 1` guards. Consumed via `useMemo([events])` (NOT a store selector).
  - `advanced-stats-section.tsx` (Pro) subscribes to the STABLE `events` array + `hasHydrated` primitive only, derives all reports in useMemo, gates body on hasHydrated, shows EmptyState when no events. `advanced-stats-locked-card.tsx` (Free) → parent opens `paywall("advancedStats")`.
- Event emission (after-success, terminal paths only; cancelled never counts):
  - `app-store.permanentlyDeleteMarked` → ONE batched `itemDeleted` (count+bytes) after the success `set`.
  - `compression.store.markCompleted` → `itemCompressed` (POST-set `status==="completed"` guard so cancelled jobs don't count).
  - `compression.store.markOriginalDeleted` → `originalDeletedAfterCompression` (verified-delete path).
  - `compression.store.markFailed` → `compressionFailed` (POST-set `status==="failed"` guard).
  - `smart-clean-screen` → `smartCleanSuggestionViewed` (once per `ready` card, useRef guard — dormant) and `smartCleanActionConfirmed` (after a confirmed delete — dormant). Never emitted from the `updateProgress` hot path.
- Smart Clean (Pro):
  - `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean.types.ts` — `SmartCleanDetector` contract (key, featureKey, requiresFullAccess, async `detect()`), result with `status: not_available|needs_permission|empty|ready`.
  - `smart-clean.service.ts` — 8 PLACEHOLDER detectors (duplicate/similar photos, duplicate videos, blurry, screenshots, memes, large videos/photos) that resolve to `status:"not_available"` with empty groups and undefined count/bytes (no fabricated results). `CATEGORY_ICON` map. A real detector drops in later by returning `ready` — no UI rewrite.
  - `smart-clean-card.tsx` — honest per-status card (Coming soon / disabled action; the `ready` count+"Review N" branch is unreachable in Stage 2).
  - `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx` — header + One-Tap Recommendations banner (dormant) + limited-access permission note (Open Settings) + the 8 cards + AdBanner (self-hides for Pro). A review→preview→confirm→delete flow is WIRED (reuses `DeleteConfirmationDialog` + the single audited `permanentlyDeleteMarked`, never deletes under limited access) but unreachable until a detector returns `ready`. Runs detectors via an effect keyed on accessLevel + last scan stamp (assets read imperatively, not via a fresh-array selector).
- Premium-tab swap + dynamic tab label:
  - `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx` — the premium tab keeps `name="premium"` (so `/premium` deep links + back-stack survive); only `title`/`tabBarIcon` change for Pro (Smart Clean + Wand2 vs Premium + Star) from `useFeatureAccess().isPro`.
  - `premium-screen.tsx` — Pro now early-returns `<SmartCleanScreen />` (its own AppHeader, so no doubled header); Free keeps `UpgradeView`. The Stage 1 `ProActiveView` was retired.
  - `stats-screen.tsx` — Pro shows `<AdvancedStatsSection />`, Free shows the locked card → paywall. Rendered INDEPENDENTLY of the `loadingPhotos` guard (events aren't media-index-derived).
- Bug found by the adversarial review and fixed: **double-counted reclaimed bytes**. A compress+delete emitted both `itemCompressed` and `originalDeletedAfterCompression` carrying the same `savedBytes`, and both the weekly "Space reclaimed" card and the storage trend summed both buckets (~2× inflation). Fix: "reclaimed" now counts only ACTUAL deletions (`itemDeleted` + `originalDeletedAfterCompression`); compression-only savings (`itemCompressed`) are excluded — which also correctly reports 0 reclaimed for compress+keep-original. (`advanced-stats-section.tsx` weekly value + `buildStorageTrend` type filter.)

NOT exercised on-device: events populating the reports (needs real deletes/compression; this tablet has no compressible media and deleting the user's real photos was avoided) and the dormant Smart Clean delete path (no detector returns `ready`) — both covered by typecheck + the adversarial review + the double-count fix.

### 2026-06-09 - Smart Clean REAL detection, all tiers (Stage 3)
- What was added: Real detection behind the 8 Smart Clean placeholder cards, tiered by feasibility, with a runner store, a persisted feature cache, and runtime native-capability gating so the app degrades gracefully on the current APK and lights up tiers after a native rebuild. Designed via a 3-architect design panel + synthesis; adversarially reviewed (12 agents) which found 6 issues (fixed). Verified on-device.
- New dependencies (added to package.json; native side only present after a gradle rebuild): `expo-file-system@~19`, `expo-image-manipulator@~14`, `jpeg-js@^0.4.4`. (Note: `expo-file-system`'s native module turned out to already be in the dev client, so Tier-1 hashing works on the current APK too.)
- Capability gating (`src/features/smart-clean/native-capabilities.ts`): `probeCapability("fileHashing"|"imageManipulator"|"videoThumbnail")` checks native presence via expo-modules-core's `requireOptionalNativeModule(name)` (returns null, never throws). CRITICAL lesson (cost a dev red box during verification): do NOT `import("expo-image-manipulator")`/`import("expo-file-system")` just to probe — evaluating those wrappers runs a top-level `requireNativeModule(...)` that throws "Cannot find native module", and Metro surfaces it as an uncaught error (red box in dev, crash in release) EVEN inside `await import(...).catch()`. So we probe by native-module NAME (`ExpoImageManipulator`, `ExponentFileSystem`/`ExpoFileSystem`) and only the detector code imports the wrappers — AFTER the probe passes. No top-level import of the gated modules anywhere → startup-safe.
- Runner + cache:
  - `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts` (NOT persisted) — cancellable, progress-tracked scan that runs detectors SEQUENTIALLY (never Promise.all) with `SCAN_YIELD_MS` yields; module-level `runToken`/`runPromise` JOIN-not-preempt discipline mirroring media-index-store; resolves-with-error (never rejects) so the loader can't strand. Screen subscribes to primitives + the stable `resultsByKey` ref only.
  - `src/features/smart-clean/feature-cache-store.ts` (persisted, key `swipeclean-smart-clean-feature-cache`) — per-asset `{md5,dHash,blurVar,vHash}` keyed by mediaId, invalidated by `modKey` (modificationTime ?? creationTime ?? 0), FIFO-capped at 8000, stores only small hex/scalars (never pixels), bulletproof onRehydrate. Detectors read via the non-React `featureCacheApi`.
- Detectors (`src/features/smart-clean/detectors/`), each behind its capability/tier:
  - Tier 0 (metadata, current APK): `tier0-large.ts` largePhotos/largeVideos by size/duration.
  - Tier 1 (media-library/file-system, current APK): `tier1-screenshots.ts` (Screenshots album + filename + screen-dimension + EXIF-absence score; EXIF probed only for borderline candidates), `tier1-duplicate-photos.ts` (size+dimension candidate buckets → MD5 confirm via `expo-file-system/legacy`; degrades to not_available without `fileHashing`).
  - Tier 2 (pixel pipeline, needs `imageManipulator` → not_available until rebuild): `image-pipeline.ts` (manipulator downscale → jpeg-js decode → grayscale), `hash-utils.ts` (pure dHash 9x8→64-bit/16-hex, Hamming popcount, union-find, Laplacian variance), `tier2-similar.ts` (dHash + month/orientation buckets), `tier2-blurry.ts` (variance-of-Laplacian), `tier2-duplicate-videos.ts` (compressor thumbnail dHash).
  - Tier 3 (heuristic, current APK): `tier3-memes.ts` — conservative metadata heuristic (filename/album/size/EXIF) behind a pluggable `MemeClassifier` interface for a future on-device ML model.
  - keepMediaId rules: duplicatePhotos = earliest creationTime; similarPhotos = highest resolution; duplicateVideos = largest bytes; large/screenshots/blurry/memes = keeper-less (every item deletable).
- Screen + sheet: `smart-clean-screen.tsx` rewritten to drive the runner (Scan/Scan again button, progress bar, per-card scanning state, One-Tap Recommendations aggregate, limited-access permission CTA). `components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` — preview grid with per-item selection and THREE keeper guards; reuses the single audited `permanentlyDeleteMarked` (never deletes under non-granted access). `SmartCleanItem.sizeBytes` uses `getOriginalBytes` so the reclaim numbers are real.
- Review fixes (6 confirmed → all fixed): (1) cross-detector duplicate `mediaId` in the Recommendations aggregate caused inflated count/bytes + a React duplicate-key collision → dedupe by mediaId in both the recommendation memo and the review sheet (keeper-aware); (2) post-delete re-scan could JOIN a stale in-flight scan → `cancel()` before the refresh runScan; (3) Smart Clean delete logged a zeroed `itemDeleted` and dropped its reclaim from advanced stats → `permanentlyDeleteMarked(ids, { emitDeletionEvent:false })` for Smart Clean, and `smartCleanActionConfirmed` now counts into reclaimed totals + storage trend (so the real bytes are reflected, with no duplicate history row); plus the capability-probe red-box fix above.
- WHAT RUNS WHERE: on the CURRENT dev-client APK — large photos/videos, screenshots, memes (heuristic), and exact-duplicate photos (MD5) all run for real; similar/blurry/duplicate-videos show "Coming soon" until a gradle rebuild adds `expo-image-manipulator`'s native side. The whole pixel pipeline + thresholds are implemented and unit-logic-correct; they activate by rebuilding (no code change).
- Files: new `src/features/smart-clean/{native-capabilities,file-system-capability,feature-cache-store,smart-clean-store}.ts`, `src/features/smart-clean/detectors/{shared,hash-utils,image-pipeline,tier0-large,tier1-screenshots,tier1-duplicate-photos,tier2-similar,tier2-blurry,tier2-duplicate-videos,tier3-memes}.ts`, `src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx`; edited `smart-clean.types.ts`, `smart-clean.service.ts`, `components/smart-clean-card.tsx`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, `src/store/app-store.ts` (permanentlyDeleteMarked options), `src/features/advanced-stats/cleanup-report.selectors.ts`, all `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (+10 keys → 416), `package.json`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 416 keys). On-device (Galaxy Tab A9+, SM-X400, dev-client; required a Metro restart with `-c` after bulk-adding files — the long-running Metro's Haste map went stale): app launches cleanly (no startup native crash); Smart Clean scan ran end-to-end — Screenshots 2/2.9MB, Memes 6/3.6MB, Duplicate photos "Nothing found" (MD5 ran), Similar/Blurry/Duplicate-videos "Coming soon" (no crash/red box after the requireOptionalNativeModule probe fix), One-Tap Recommendations "≈6.5MB across 8 items" (dedup correct = 2+6), and the Review-all sheet rendered 8 unique thumbnails with no duplicate-key warning. NOT exercised: an actual Smart Clean deletion (avoided destroying the user's real photos) and Tier-2 detection results (need a native rebuild).
- UPDATE 2026-06-09 (same day): the dev client WAS rebuilt (`gradlew :app:assembleDebug`, ~2.5 min, autolinking picked up the new modules) and reinstalled on the tablet. Tier 2 is now LIVE and verified on-device: after the rebuild a Smart Clean scan flipped Similar/Blurry/Duplicate-videos from "Coming soon" → "Nothing found" (the manipulator→jpeg-js dHash / Laplacian / thumbnail-hash pipeline actually executed on real photos with NO crash and NO "Cannot find native module" — the requireOptionalNativeModule probe held). Full Stages 1–3 re-walkthrough on the rebuilt APK passed (Free subscription screen + ads + locked advanced-stats card; Pro Smart Clean tab + advanced-stats section; all-tier scan). The device library is small so similar/blurry/duplicate results were empty, but the detectors ran.
- UPDATE 2026-06-09 (review-sheet bug fixes, device-verified): two bugs reported in the Smart Clean review sheet were fixed in `components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx`. (1) Thumbnails showed gray: the sheet was an RN `<Modal>` and expo-image fired the Glide load before the cell measured → it requested `SIZE_ORIGINAL` (Glide dims `[-2147483648x...]`) and large images failed to decode. Fix: render the sheet as an in-tree absolute overlay (not RN Modal), use expo-image's `<Image>` directly with an explicit pixel `style={{width:cell,height:cell}}` (not CachedImage's absoluteFill), and gate the thumbnails behind a one-frame `imagesReady` delay so the cell is measured first — Glide then requests a downsampled `[231x231]` and loads reliably (verified: memes review renders all 6 real images, no Glide failures). (2) The footer (Cancel/Delete) overlapped the bottom navigation: the RN Modal rendered edge-to-edge while the app is non-edge-to-edge, so `useSafeAreaInsets` read ~0. The in-tree overlay sits within the screen content area (above the tab bar) so the footer is clear; Android back now closes it via a `BackHandler` listener (the overlay has no Modal `onRequestClose`). Trade-off: the dim backdrop no longer covers the tab bar (acceptable for a confirm sheet). NOTE: during verification, stray `adb screencap` temp files (`/sdcard/ds.png` etc.) had been media-indexed and matched the screenshot heuristic, then deleted → they appeared as gray cells; cleaned from MediaStore. Lesson: write device screencaps to `/data/local/tmp`, not `/sdcard`, so they aren't indexed into the gallery.
- UPDATE 2026-06-09 (pre-scan card copy): before a scan, every Smart Clean card showed "Coming soon" because the screen's pre-scan placeholder used `status:"not_available"`. Now that the detectors are implemented this read as unbuilt. Added a distinct `"idle"` status to `SmartCleanStatus`; the pre-scan placeholder uses `idle`; the card shows a muted "Not scanned" pill (i18n `smartClean.notScanned`, all 10 locales) and hides the per-card action button (the top "Scan now" drives it). `"not_available"` → "Coming soon" is retained for genuinely capability-absent builds. Verified on the S24: cards read "Not scanned" pre-scan. Also: the SwipeClean dev client was installed/updated on the S24 (SM-S921B, RFCX2035YHT) — it has a large real library (~11 GB heavy media, 3000+ assets), the right device to stress-test detectors.
- Remaining work: spike the pixel pipeline's perf/thresholds on a LARGE library (the S24 has 3000+ assets — run a real Smart Clean scan there); optionally a real ML `MemeClassifier`; tune detector thresholds (DHASH_*/BLUR_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD) on real data; exercise an actual Smart Clean deletion (avoided on the user's real photos); the screenshot detector over-matched some non-screenshot images (Canva/WhatsApp/downloads) via the dimension+EXIF heuristic — tighten it on real data.
- Assumptions: `requireOptionalNativeModule` is the safe probe (no wrapper eval); reusing `low|medium|high`-style metadata is fine; deletion stays on the single audited `permanentlyDeleteMarked` path; heuristic memes are labeled honestly and always require preview+confirm.

### 2026-06-09 - Smart Clean scan: separate ongoing notification with Stop (background-style)
- What was added: the Smart Clean scan now surfaces its OWN ongoing (sticky, non-dismissible) Android notification with a **Stop** action button — like compression, but DELIBERATELY SEPARATE so the two coexist and never overwrite each other. JS-only; NO native rebuild (expo-notifications is already in the APK). Designed around the hard constraint that `react-native-background-actions` (compression's foreground service) is a SINGLETON — only one task at a time — so the scan cannot reuse it while a compression runs. Adversarially reviewed (workflow, 3 dimensions × independent verifiers) which found 4 real issues; all fixed and device-verified.
- New file `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-notifications.ts` (`SmartCleanScanNotifications`): posts via `expo-notifications` on its OWN channel/id/category/data-type all named `smart-clean-scan` (vs compression's `compression-progress`/`compression-complete` + the background-actions FG service) → different ids guarantee no collision. `showProgress(current,total)` schedules a `sticky:true, autoDismiss:false, sound:false` notification (LOW-importance channel → silent, no heads-up); `dismiss()` clears it; `addStopListener(onStop)` fires on `actionIdentifier === "smart-clean-stop"`.
- Wiring: `smart-clean-store.ts` `runScan` calls `showProgress(i+1,total)` per detector and `dismiss()` on complete/error; `cancel()`/`reset()` also `dismiss()`. `app/_layout.tsx` registers a GLOBAL Stop listener (`cancel()` works even when the Smart Clean screen is unmounted) and a dismiss-on-app-start to clear any notification stranded by a killed session. `smart-clean-screen.tsx` no longer cancels the scan on unmount — like compression, the scan PERSISTS across in-app navigation, surfaced by the notification; returning to the screen re-attaches to the running runner.
- The hard bug (4 review findings, all one root cause): `void showProgress()` and `void dismiss()` are fire-and-forget, and `scheduleNotificationAsync` (channelId trigger) presents on a native IO coroutine AFTER its JS promise resolves — so the last show's deferred `notify()` lands AFTER `dismiss`'s `cancel()`, stranding a non-dismissible "Scanning 8 of 8…" notification with no scan running (reproduced on-device: screen showed results while the sticky notification persisted until force-stop). Fix has three parts: (1) a module-level FIFO `opQueue` serializing all post/dismiss ops in call order; (2) `showProgress` records `lastScheduleAt = Date.now()` after the schedule resolves; (3) `dismiss()` POLLS `getPresentedNotificationsAsync` — cancelling every ~110ms — and only terminates once the notification is stably absent (3 consecutive) AND `Date.now()-lastScheduleAt > 1500ms` (no present can still be in flight). This makes "last call wins" hold despite the deferred native present, terminates promptly when nothing is pending (post-delete cancel / app start, since `lastScheduleAt` is old/zero), and never strands. Finding 4 (foreground display silently relied on compression's `setNotificationHandler`) fixed by registering the scan's OWN type-aware handler in `ensureSetup` (banner+list for both; sound only for non-scan; the LOW scan channel is silent regardless) so it no longer depends on the compression feature.
- Caveat (documented in-file): this is a notification, NOT a second foreground service (the library can't run two), so a FULLY backgrounded app may have its JS scan suspended by the OS — the notification stays, progress pauses, resumes on return. In-app navigation keeps it running.
- Files: new `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-notifications.ts`; edited `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts`, `app/_layout.tsx`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`. No new i18n keys (reused `smartClean.title/scanningProgress/stop`). No package/native changes.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 417 keys, parity). On-device (tablet SM-X400, dev-client, JS reload): `dumpsys notification` confirmed the posted record `tag=smart-clean-scan channel=smart-clean-scan flags=ONGOING_EVENT|SILENT actions=1 [0]"Stop"->broadcastIntent` on a separate LOW channel; the orphan bug was reproduced before the fix and is GONE after (presence timeline `.PPP....` — appears during scan, then absent and stays absent through completion); a 5-tap rapid re-scan stress test left zero orphaned notifications. NOT exercised live on this device: pressing the notification Stop mid-scan (the 12-asset library scans in ~1s, too fast to catch) and simultaneous compression+scan coexistence — both are guaranteed by the distinct channel/id and the proven dismiss path, and confirmed by review; better stress-tested on the S24's large library. (The notification Stop button was subsequently verified on the S24 — see the standalone-release entry below.)

### 2026-06-09 - Standalone (release) build + Pro test unlock, installed on the S24
- What was done: produced a STANDALONE release APK (JS embedded, `__DEV__=false`, no Metro/dev-launcher) and installed it on the S24 (SM-S921B, RFCX2035YHT) as a normal application — at the user's request ("install the app as an application not as a dev build").
- Build: `android/app/build.gradle`'s `release` buildType already signs with the DEBUG keystore (`signingConfig signingConfigs.debug`, the RN/Expo template default) and `minifyEnabled` is off — so `:app:assembleRelease` builds with NO new keystore and is debug-signed, i.e. the SAME signature as the installed dev-client, so `adb install -r` replaces it and KEEPS app data. Recipe: `JAVA_HOME="C:\Program Files\Android\Android Studio\jbr"`, `& "<repo>\android\gradlew.bat" -p "<repo>\android" :app:assembleRelease --console=plain` (~3.5 min; `createBundleReleaseJsAndAssets` embeds the JS bundle → no Metro at runtime). Output: `android/app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk` (117 MB). Installed via `adb install -r` (Success).
- Pro test unlock (because `__DEV__=false` hides the debug Pro Override and real billing is still a placeholder → a true release runs Free-only, locking Smart Clean): added a build-time flag `FORCE_PRO_ACCESS` in `src/features/subscription/feature-access.service.ts` = `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO === "1"` (Expo inlines `EXPO_PUBLIC_*` into the bundle at build time). `FeatureAccessService.isProUser` returns true when set; `buildSubscriptionSnapshot` (in `src/store/subscription-store.ts`) reports the unlock as `debug_override`/`debug` (a local, non-real unlock — same treatment as the dev override). This does NOT weaken the production rule for `debugProOverride`. It was enabled for THIS build via a TEMPORARY `.env.local` (`EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO=1`) that was DELETED after building — so default/future builds revert to Free/production. To rebuild a Pro test build later, re-create `.env.local` (or set the env var) before `assembleRelease`. SECURITY: like `debugProOverride`, `FORCE_PRO_ACCESS` is a test convenience only and must never gate a real store release.
- Files: edited `src/features/subscription/feature-access.service.ts` (FORCE_PRO_ACCESS + isProUser), `src/store/subscription-store.ts` (snapshot localUnlock). No new deps, no i18n changes. (`.env.local` was temporary and is NOT in the tree.)
- Verification (S24, standalone, NO `adb reverse`/Metro): launched via the MAIN/LAUNCHER intent → opened straight into the app (no dev launcher), media library loaded (3,165 photos / 13 GB), no crash/DevLauncher in logcat. 4th tab = **Smart Clean** (Pro unlocked → `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO` inlined correctly). Ran a Smart Clean scan: `dumpsys notification` showed the ongoing record `tag=smart-clean-scan channel=smart-clean-scan flags=ONGOING_EVENT|SILENT actions=1 [0]"Stop"->broadcastIntent`, body "Scanning 1 of 8…" in sync with the in-screen banner. Expanded the notification and tapped its **Stop** action → the scan cancelled (screen returned to idle/"Not scanned") AND the notification dismissed immediately and stayed gone (no orphan). This is the notification-Stop path that the tablet's ~1s scan was too fast to exercise.
- Note: the S24 now runs the STANDALONE RELEASE app (not the dev client). To go back to dev-client development on it, reinstall a debug build (`assembleDebug` + `adb install -r`) and use Metro again.

### 2026-06-10 - Cleanup screen: month sections (collapsible, descending) + de-cluttered cards
- What changed (`src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, the "Cleanup"/compression tab): per user request — (1) removed the **"Done" filter** chip entirely (the `CleanupFilter` type, the chip, and every `activeFilter === "done"` branch: summary text, savings pill, compress-all guard, empty state, `getBackgroundSavings`); (2) the heavy-media list is now **grouped into month sections in DESCENDING order** (newest month first); (3) each month section is **collapsible/expandable** via its header (chevron up/down); (4) removed the per-card **"Save X%"** badge AND the "Done/Review/Deleted/Copy saved" status badge (the whole top-right badge block). The Media/Videos/Large Photos filters are kept (not in scope to remove); month grouping applies within the active filter.
- How: switched the 2-column `FlatList` to a `SectionList`. `monthSections` buckets `heavyMedia` by `PhotoAsset.monthKey`, sorts `b.localeCompare(a)` on the zero-padded `"YYYY-MM"` keys (lexical == chronological → newest first), and labels each via the existing `monthLabel()` (localized, e.g. "June 2026"). Each section's `data` is the month's items chunked into rows of 2 (`chunkIntoRows`) so the 2-col grid is preserved; a collapsed month sets `data: []` (header stays, no rows). Collapse state is a `Set<string>` of monthKeys (`collapsedMonths`) toggled by `MonthSectionHeader`. Header subtitle ("N items · size") reuses `getMediaTypeNoun()` + `formatBytes()`. `stickySectionHeadersEnabled`. **No new i18n keys** (reused `monthLabel`/`getMediaTypeNoun`/existing cleanup.* keys); the now-unused `cleanup.done`/`cleanup.doneSummary`/`cleanup.doneEmpty`/`cleanup.saved`/`common.savePercent` keys are left in the locale files (still parity-valid; some are used by other screens).
- Bug found by the adversarial review (1 confirmed) + fixed: a `SectionList` renders `ListEmptyComponent` whenever its item count is 0 — which ALSO happens when every month is collapsed (all `data: []`), so collapsing all months wrongly showed the "No heavy media found" empty state even though media exists. Fix: gate the empty component on the real data — `ListEmptyComponent={heavyMedia.length > 0 ? null : (loading ? <Loader/> : <EmptyState/>)}` — so collapsed-but-non-empty renders nothing extra (just the headers). Everything else in the review (keyExtractor uniqueness, descending grouping with no drops/dups, memo deps/selector stability, sticky-header layout, no dangling `done` logic) came back clean.
- Files: edited `src/screens/history-screen.tsx` only. No deps, no native, no i18n-file changes.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 417 keys, parity). Tablet (SM-X400, dev-client): "Done" chip gone (only All Media/Videos/Large Photos); it has no heavy/compressible media so it correctly shows the empty state (validates the `heavyMedia.length === 0` branch of the fix). S24 (SM-S921B, STANDALONE release with the fix, real ~11 GB library): month sections render **June 2026 → May 2026 → April 2026** (descending); tapping a header collapses/expands it (chevron flips, cards hide/show); collapsing June + May while April stays expanded shows NO empty state (the fix); cards have **no "Save %"/"Done" badge** (just filename + "orig → compressed" + video play icon). Pro was unlocked in that build via the temporary `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO=1` `.env.local` (deleted after building).

### 2026-06-10 - Cleanup screen lag fix (index persist debounce + identity-stable merges + render memoization)
- Problem: big lag spikes on the Cleanup tab with large libraries (S24: ~3,165 assets). Root causes, worst first: (1) zustand persist re-serialized the ENTIRE media index (`JSON.stringify` of thousands of assets, multi-MB) on EVERY `media-index-store` set — ~53× during a full scan (one per 60-asset page) plus once per 45s poll/app-foreground/media-change event via `refreshNewestPage`; (2) `mergeIndexedAssets` rebuilt `assetsById`/`orderedIds` (full O(N log N) re-sort) even when nothing changed, so every poll churned object identities → every subscribed screen re-derived and re-rendered everything; (3) the Cleanup screen re-ran `CompressionService.estimate()` over the whole filtered list in three places per render (the indexed assets already carry precomputed estimates), `MediaCard` was unmemoized, and `renderItem`/`renderSectionHeader` were inline closures — so any parent re-render re-rendered every mounted row; (4) video thumbnails re-extracted natively (`createVideoThumbnail`) on every card mount/remount and whenever the asset object identity changed.
- Fix:
  - `src/store/media-index-store.ts`: custom debounced `PersistStorage` (800ms trailing; stringify+write happen once per burst instead of per set; skips the write entirely when the partialized state is shallow-equal to what storage already holds AND nothing is queued — last-call-wins preserved). `lastQuickRefreshAt` is no longer persisted (written-only field; persisting it forced a full index write after every idle poll). `mergeIndexedAssets` is identity-stable: reuses existing asset objects when all raw+derived fields match (`isSameIndexedAsset`; derived fields compared too so estimator-constant changes in an app update still replace stale persisted objects) and returns the SAME map/order references on a no-op merge → idle polls now cause zero derive/re-render/persist work downstream. `removeUnseenAfterCompleteScan` likewise returns same refs when nothing was pruned. The full-scan loop commits merged pages every `SCAN_SET_BATCH_PAGES=3` pages instead of every page (summary/progress still update per commit; final partial batch always commits before the completion set). `refreshNewestPage` reuses `state.summary` when the merge was a no-op. A persist `merge` recomputes `summary` from the rehydrated assets at startup, because mid-scan partial states can be persisted (debounce + kill) — this keeps the "unchanged merge keeps the summary" optimization sound and self-heals the savings pill after an interrupted rescan.
  - `src/services/compression-service.ts`: `createThumbnail` caches video-thumbnail promises in a module Map keyed `${id}:${modificationTime}:${uri}` (FIFO cap 1000; rejected promises evict only their own entry; concurrent requests dedupe to one native call).
  - `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`: uses precomputed `estimatedOriginalBytes`/`estimatedCompressedBytes` from `IndexedMediaAsset` (no render-time `estimate()` over the list), `React.memo`'d `MediaCard`, `useCallback`'d `keyExtractor`/`renderRow`/`renderSectionHeader`, SectionList virtualization tuning (`windowSize 9`, `maxToRenderPerBatch 8`, `initialNumToRender 8`, `updateCellsBatchingPeriod 40`), `MediaImage` effect keyed on `[id, uri, mediaType, modificationTime]` instead of the asset object, and `monthSections` depends on `i18n.language` so month titles re-localize on language change.
- Adversarially reviewed (1 agent, full diff + interacting files): found 4 confirmed defects in the first cut — stale-summary-forever after an interrupted rescan (fixed via the persist `merge` recompute), month titles sticking in the old language (fixed via `i18n.language` dep), `MediaImage` deps defeating the modificationTime cache keying (fixed), and identity reuse freezing stale derived estimates across app updates (fixed by comparing derived fields). All fixed before shipping.
- Files changed: `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/services/compression-service.ts`, `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. New dependencies: none. New i18n keys: none.
- Behavior notes: the index store can now lose up to ~800ms of trailing writes on process death — harmless by design (the index is derived data; the next refresh/scan rebuilds it, and the rehydration `merge` recomputes the summary). Scan progress (savings pill) updates every 3 pages (~180 assets) instead of every page.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 417 keys, parity). On-device verification on the S24 standalone release build is the intended test (user-requested install).
- Known pre-existing hazard documented during review (NOT fixed here): `PhotoLibraryService.getPhotosPage` swallows native errors into `{photos: [], hasNextPage: false}`, so a transient failure mid-scan can make a full scan "complete" truncated and prune everything past the failure point from the index until the next Estimate again. (FIXED 2026-06-10 — see the next entry.)

### 2026-06-10 - Full-scan truncation fix (distinguish page-read failure from end-of-library)
- Problem: `PhotoLibraryService.getPhotosPage` caught native `MediaLibrary.getAssetsAsync` errors and returned `{ photos: [], hasNextPage: false }` — identical to a genuine end-of-library page. `useMediaIndexStore.startFullScan` therefore treated a transient mid-scan native failure as a normal final page: the scan "completed", ran `removeUnseenAfterCompleteScan` (pruning every indexed asset past the failure point) and recorded `lastFullScanCompletedAt`. One transient error silently truncated the persisted media index, so Swipe/Cleanup/Stats all shrank until the user manually tapped "Estimate again". (This is the hazard flagged in the prior entry.)
- Fix (minimal, routes into the scan's existing error path):
  - `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`: `GetPhotosPageResult` gained an optional `error?: boolean`. The `getPhotosPage` catch now returns `{ photos: [], hasNextPage: false, error: true }`, making a native read failure distinguishable from an empty/end page. Other callers (`refreshNewestPage`, `getPhotos`/`getLargestPhotos`) don't read the field, so their treat-as-empty behavior is unchanged.
  - `src/store/media-index-store.ts`: the `startFullScan` page loop now checks `page.error` immediately after each fetch and, if set (and the scan token still matches), `throw`s. The throw routes into the IIFE's existing `.catch`, which sets `status: "error"` WITHOUT running `removeUnseenAfterCompleteScan` and WITHOUT setting `lastFullScanCompletedAt`, and the promise still RESOLVES (never rejects) so awaiting callers clear their loaders. A superseding scan (token changed mid-await) bails quietly via `return`.
- Why this is safe: `status: "error"` was already a tolerated state (set by the permission-denied branch and the pre-existing `.catch`), so no UI surface breaks. Already-merged pages stay (additive merge) — the index keeps what was successfully scanned and is NOT pruned. Because `lastFullScanCompletedAt` is left unset, the auto-scan skip gate (`canSkip`) won't suppress a retry on the next launch/refresh, and `app-store.reconcileMediaIndex`'s `reconciled` guard (`lastFullScanCompletedAt >= startedAt`) stays false, so a failed limited-access reconcile does NOT prune the `markedForDeletion`/`reviewedPhotoIds` queues against a half-built index. The JOIN-not-preempt concurrency discipline (`fullScanPromise`/`scanToken`/`forceRescanRequested`) is untouched.
- Files changed: `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. New dependencies: none. New i18n keys: none. New commands/config: none.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0). Not yet exercised on a physical device this session (the failure path needs a transient native `getAssetsAsync` error mid-scan, which is hard to force on-device); the change is conservative and reuses the already-verified error lifecycle.
- Assumptions: A page-read failure should abort the scan as an error rather than completing a partial index — preserving the existing (additive, un-pruned) index is preferable to truncating it. Per-page transient failures are not retried in-place; the next scheduled scan/refresh retries the whole scan from the start (the scan already always restarts from `after: undefined`).

### 2026-06-10 - Smart Clean: durable background scan + persist + auto-resume + progress fixes
- Problem: the Smart Clean scan ran as plain foreground JS with a sticky notification that only *looked* like background work — backgrounding the app suspended the JS thread (scan + notification froze), killing the app lost the scan, results were never persisted (every reopen reset to "Not scanned" and rescanned from scratch), and progress was coarse (8 steps) and off-by-one between the in-app counter and the notification. User asked for a true background scan that survives app death, persists scanned data, and continues where it left off.
- Scope decision (asked the user): literal "execute while the process is dead" is impossible in JS without a native rewrite. Chose the durable equivalent — a real Android foreground service (runs while backgrounded/screen-off) + continuous checkpointing + auto-resume on next launch. NO native rebuild (RNBA + the `dataSync` service/perms are already in the APK from compression; all changes are JS/TS).
- Design (planned, then validated by a Plan agent + an adversarial review):
  - **Foreground service (parallel wrapper, not a shared coordinator):** new `src/services/background-smart-clean-scan-worker.ts` clones `BackgroundCompressionWorker` with a distinct `taskName "SwipeCleanScan"`. The scan loop runs INSIDE the RNBA task so it keeps executing while backgrounded. RNBA is a hard singleton: the scan acquires the service only when free (`!isRunning()`), else runs plain-JS and still checkpoints. A module `serviceHeld` flag tracks whether the SCAN (not compression) owns it. One-directional yield: `compression.store.runCompressionJob` calls `useSmartCleanStore.getState().releaseForegroundService()` before `runInForeground`, so a backgrounded scan drops the service for compression (compression always has priority) and continues as plain JS.
  - **Notification by ownership:** service held → RNBA's foreground-service notification (progress bar, tap → app); not held (degraded/non-Android) → the existing `SmartCleanScanNotifications` expo path WITH its Stop button. Mutually exclusive (no double notification); the runner's `updateScanNotification` switches on `serviceHeld`.
  - **Persist + detector-level resume:** `smart-clean-store.ts` is now a persisted zustand store (key `swipeclean-smart-clean-results`). Results are persisted COMPACT (mediaIds only) and expanded ONCE in `hydrateFromPersisted` against the media index (prune deleted items, recompute via `finalizeResult`) into a stable `resultsByKey` — never expanded in a selector (avoids the Stage-1 fresh-object-selector crash). A detector with an entry in `resultsByKey` is "done"; resume seeds from the checkpoint and starts at the first detector with no entry. The expensive per-asset hashing is already durably cached (feature cache), so a resumed detector reuses prior hashes. `partialize` persists `restoredCompact ?? compactResults(resultsByKey)` so a pre-hydration write can't overwrite good data with empty. `phase` "scanning"→persisted "interrupted"; manual Stop → "idle" (no auto-resume).
  - **Auto-resume:** new `SmartCleanResumeSync` in `app/_layout.tsx` fires `resumeIfInterrupted()` once both stores are ready (reactive on `hasHydrated` + `orderedIds.length>0`) and on AppState "active"; the screen also calls it on mount. All funnel through `runScan`'s `runPromise` JOIN (idempotent). `runSignature = lastFullScanCompletedAt|accessLevel|orderedIds.length`; signature mismatch on resume → show pruned results as "complete", no auto-resume against a changed library.
  - **Progress fixes:** the runner now forwards each detector's `onProgress` (detectors already emit it) so the bar moves smoothly; a discrete 1-based `activeIndex` drives BOTH the in-app "X of Y" counter and the notification (kills the off-by-one). Throttled intra-detector RNBA notification updates (1.2s) so a long detector's bar isn't frozen.
  - **Feature-cache write amplification:** new shared `src/utils/debounced-storage.ts` (`createDebouncedStorage`, extracted pattern); the feature cache (which upserted on every asset) now persists debounced (~800ms) instead of once-per-asset.
- Adversarial review (1 agent, full trace) found 1 real cross-feature bug + 1 latent footgun, both fixed: (A1) the scan's foreground `finally` called `BackgroundService.stop()` unconditionally — after a mid-scan release to compression that would stop COMPRESSION's service (global stop) and trip `resumePendingJobs` into failing the job; fixed to stop only when `serviceHeld`. (B2) `runScan({resume:true})` against a not-yet-ready index would null `restoredCompact` and persist empty (losing the checkpoint); fixed to bail until the index is ready. Confirmed-clean areas: empty-overwrite guard, concurrent-resume JOIN, beginAt off-by-one, subscription safety, counter↔notification agreement, no require cycle (compression.store→smart-clean-store doesn't close a loop; no gated native module imported at startup).
- Files: new `src/services/background-smart-clean-scan-worker.ts`, `src/utils/debounced-storage.ts`; rewrote `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts`; edited `src/features/smart-clean/feature-cache-store.ts`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts` (one yield call), `app/_layout.tsx`. No new deps. No i18n keys (reused `smartClean.title`/`scanningProgress`/`stop`).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 417 keys). On-device (S24, standalone release with `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO=1`): device tests = background survival (progress advances while backgrounded), resume-after-kill (continues from last detector, cached hashes), results persistence across kill, stale invalidation on library change, compression coexistence (scan yields the service, no orphan notification, compression not falsely failed), and progress/counter agreement. Caveat to confirm on-device: A2 from review — whether `await releaseForegroundService()`'s `stop()` clears `isRunning()` before compression re-checks (depends on RNBA flipping its flag synchronously); compression already relies on this elsewhere.

### 2026-06-10 - Smart Clean review sheet: virtualized grid (fixes large-collection crash) + renders above the tab bar
- Problems: (1) reviewing a big collection (>5 GB — especially the One-Tap "Review all" aggregate of every detector's groups) lagged then crashed/ANR'd: the review sheet rendered EVERY item via `items.map()` inside a plain `ScrollView`, so thousands of `expo-image` views mounted and decoded at once → memory blowup. (2) The review bottom sheet rendered UNDER the bottom tab bar — it was an in-tree absolute overlay mounted inside `SmartCleanScreen` (which lives inside the tab navigator), so the tab bar painted on top of it.
- Fix:
  - **Virtualized grid:** `smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` grid is now a `FlatList` (numColumns 3–4, bounded `maxHeight`, `windowSize`/`maxToRenderPerBatch`/`initialNumToRender` tuned, `removeClippedSubviews`) with a `React.memo`'d `ReviewCell` so only on-screen cells mount/decode and a selection toggle re-renders just the toggled cell. `selectedBytes` is memoized. The keeper-protection (3 guards: initial selection excludes keepers, toggle rejects keepers, confirm filters keepers) and the expo-image explicit-cell-size + one-frame `imagesReady` gate are preserved.
  - **Above the tab bar:** the sheet is now driven by a tiny non-persisted store `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-review-store.ts` (`open/setBusy/close`, holds title/groups/onConfirm) and mounted ONCE in `app/_layout.tsx` AFTER the `<Stack>` (above the tab navigator), so its absolute overlay covers the bottom tab bar. It stays an in-tree overlay (NOT an RN `<Modal>`) so the prior fixes hold (correct `useSafeAreaInsets`, no Glide gray-thumbnail/SIZE_ORIGINAL bug) — the documented reason the sheet had been moved off Modal in the first place. The backdrop-tap / Android-back / Cancel all close it; covering the tab bar gives true modal behavior.
  - `smart-clean-screen.tsx` no longer renders the sheet or holds `reviewTarget`/`deleting` state; `handlePrimary` (ready detector) and the recommendations "Review all" call a `startReview(detectorKey, title, groups)` helper that opens the store with an `onConfirm` closure capturing the detectorKey. `handleConfirmDelete(detectorKey, ids, bytes)` reads permission fresh via `getState()` (it now runs from the root sheet's callback), drives `busy`/`close` through the review store, and otherwise keeps the audited deletion path (`permanentlyDeleteMarked({emitDeletionEvent:false})` + `smartCleanActionConfirmed` event + feature-cache prune + post-delete re-scan).
- Files: new `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-review-store.ts`; rewrote `src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` (store-driven + FlatList); edited `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, `app/_layout.tsx`. No new deps, no i18n keys.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 417 keys). On-device (S24, standalone release, Pro test-unlock): open a large detector / "Review all" → grid scrolls smoothly with no crash; the sheet (and its delete-confirm dialog) overlay the bottom tab bar.

### 2026-06-10 - Smart Clean: scan cheapest-first so results surface fast (cold-cache UX)
- Problem: after clearing app data (cold cache), a scan on a large library (~3,000 photos / 13 GB) takes minutes — confirmed on-device it was pegged at ~103% CPU stuck on detector "2 of 8" (similarPhotos). Root cause is inherent cold-cache cost: the media index rebuilds (per-asset `getAssetInfoAsync`) and the Tier-2 pixel detectors (similar/blurry) decode EVERY photo (native downscale + jpeg-js decode), twice total. The hashes persist (feature cache) so re-scans are fast; clearing data forced the full recompute. Not a bug, but the heavy detectors ran early so nothing showed for a long time.
- Fix (per user choice "show results fast"): decoupled SCAN order from DISPLAY order. `smart-clean.service.ts` now exports `SMART_CLEAN_SCAN_ORDER` (cheapest first: largePhotos, largeVideos, screenshots, memes, duplicatePhotos, duplicateVideos, similarPhotos, blurryPhotos) alongside `SMART_CLEAN_DETECTORS` (unchanged card/display order). `smart-clean-store.ts`'s scan loop (`total`/`firstUndone`/`detector = [i]`) iterates `SMART_CLEAN_SCAN_ORDER`. Cards still render in `SMART_CLEAN_DETECTORS` order and each populates when ITS detector finishes (results keyed by detector.key), so the card layout doesn't move — the instant/cheap categories just fill within seconds while similar/blurry finish last in the background service. Resume is order-independent (keyed by detector.key). No threshold/detection changes.
- Files: `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean.service.ts`, `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts`. No deps, no i18n. Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0). On-device: cold scan shows large-files/screenshots/memes/duplicates results quickly; similar/blurry populate last.

### 2026-06-11 - Settings overhaul: App Lock + biometric, reminders, permission checkboxes, compact UI
- What changed (all in/around `src/screens/settings-screen.tsx`): (1) removed the no-op check button in the header top-right (left back-arrow + centered title + a width-matched spacer). (2) **Account & Security is now real**: a 4-digit **App Lock** passcode (enter → confirm) stored in `expo-secure-store`, and **biometric unlock** (Face ID/Touch ID/fingerprint) via `expo-local-authentication`. (3) **Minimal accent picker** — a dedicated right-aligned swatch row (28px dots, selected = thin ring) instead of the old below-text 34px circles. (4) **Compact rows** — `SettingsRow` gained a `trailing` accessory rendered far-right/vertically-centered (chevrons/checkboxes no longer sit under the text), reduced min-height (76→58), paddings and font sizes. (5) **Permissions section as checkboxes** — Photos & Videos AND Notifications each show a checkbox (checked = granted) and tap to request/open-settings. (6) **Notifications section** lists what the app sends — cleanup reminders (twice daily), compression reminders (Mon/Wed/Fri), a weekly Pro highlight — gated by a master toggle and actually scheduled. (7) removed the **Privacy** section (analytics/usage/error) and the no-op Privacy Policy row. (8) bottom credit → **"SwipeClean by Cognitx team"** (new `settings.madeBy`; the old `common.version` string is now unused but left in the locales).
- New files: `src/services/app-lock-service.ts` (SecureStore PIN + LocalAuthentication biometric, both **capability-probed by native-module NAME** via `requireOptionalNativeModule` — `ExpoSecureStore`/`ExpoLocalAuthentication` — and **lazy-imported only after the probe**, mirroring `smart-clean/native-capabilities.ts`; FAILS OPEN so a missing module never strands the user and setup is blocked when SecureStore is absent); `src/services/reminder-notification-service.ts` (recurring DAILY/WEEKLY local notifications via expo-notifications — already in the APK, no rebuild — with stable per-slot identifiers cancelled+rescheduled idempotently, gated on master toggle AND OS permission, copy pulled from the live i18n instance; weekday is 1=Sunday); `src/components/passcode-pad.tsx` (presentational dots + numeric keypad with shake-on-error and an optional biometric key, shared by the lock screen and Settings); `src/components/app-lock-gate.tsx` (root-mounted full-screen lock that covers tabs+sheets; locks on cold start and on return-to-foreground; auto-prompts biometric first, cancel → passcode pad in the SAME screen).
- Files edited: `src/components/settings-row.tsx` (trailing accessory + compaction), `src/screens/settings-screen.tsx` (rewrite), `app/_layout.tsx` (mount `<AppLockGate/>` LAST, above everything; add `<ReminderSync/>`), `src/models/photo.ts` (+`compressionRemindersEnabled`,`proNotificationsEnabled`; biometric/appLock defaults flipped to false), `src/services/settings-service.ts` (defaults), all `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (+39 keys → **456**: new `lock.*` and `reminders.*` namespaces + `settings.*`/`permissions.*` additions; fanned out one agent per locale, parity-checked), `app.json` (added `expo-secure-store` + `expo-local-authentication` config plugins), `package.json`/`package-lock.json`.
- New dependencies: `expo-local-authentication@~17.0.8`, `expo-secure-store@~15.0.8` (installed via `npx expo install`). **NATIVE REBUILD REQUIRED** before App Lock / biometric activate — their native modules are not in the current APK. Until the rebuild, both probes return false: App Lock setup shows "Available after the next app update" and the gate never locks (fail-open). The reminder/notification work needs NO rebuild (expo-notifications already present). Rebuild recipe (unchanged): `JAVA_HOME` → Android Studio JBR, `:app:assembleDebug` (or `assembleRelease`) from `android/`, `adb install -r`.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `node scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (10 locales, 456 keys, parity). Designed + reviewed adversarially via Workflow (3 dimensions × independent verifiers, 12 agents): two real defects found and fixed — (a) the PasscodeDialog verify ran in a `useEffect` whose `busy` dep cancelled its own in-flight check (verification could never land) → rewritten to imperative `handleChange`; (b) cancelling mid-verify could still apply the result (silently disabling App Lock after Cancel) → guarded with a `tokenRef` bumped on every flow (re)open. Also fixed: reminders not re-localizing on language change (the `i18n.changeLanguage` swap resolves async after the toggle-driven apply) → `ReminderSync` now reschedules on i18n's `languageChanged` event. Reviewer over-flags dismissed after inspection: biometric-toggle "post-unmount setState" (it's a Zustand action, safe), weekday numbering (1=Sunday is correct per expo types), confirm-step/biometric-cancel "messaging" (spec met via distinct titles + always-visible pad). NOT yet exercised on a device this session (needs the native rebuild for biometric/SecureStore; the reminder scheduling is exercisable now).
- Assumptions: App Lock (passcode) is the master and biometric requires it (so there is always a passcode fallback) — enabling biometric is blocked unless App Lock is on and a credential is enrolled, and disabling App Lock also disables biometric. 4-digit PIN (user choice). Reminder cadence chosen within the user's ranges: cleanup 11:00 & 18:00 daily; compression Mon/Wed/Fri 18:00; Pro Sunday 12:00 (tune the slot arrays in `reminder-notification-service.ts`). The PIN is stored in Keystore-backed SecureStore (acceptable for an app-lock PIN). The Permissions section shows only the two USER-GRANTABLE runtime permissions (media, notifications) — install-time manifest perms (wake lock, foreground service) are not user-toggdleable and are intentionally omitted.
- Remaining TODOs: rebuild the dev client to activate biometric + SecureStore, then device-QA the full lock flow (cold start, background→foreground re-lock, biometric→cancel→passcode, wrong-PIN, change/disable passcode, fail-open when SecureStore absent) and on-device reminder delivery; the `analyticsCollectionEnabled`/`usageDataCollectionEnabled`/`errorReportingEnabled` settings fields and the `settings.privacy*`/`common.version` locale keys are now unused (left in place, parity-valid) and could be pruned later.
- UPDATE (same day): the S24 (RFCX2035YHT) standalone release was rebuilt (`:app:assembleRelease` with a temp `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO=1` `.env.local`, deleted after) and reinstalled — autolinking confirmed `expo-local-authentication@17.0.8` + `expo-secure-store@15.0.8` in the APK, and the on-device Settings screen shows App Lock/Biometric as LIVE (not the "after update" fallback). The feature is therefore active on the S24; other devices (e.g. the Tab A9+) still need their own rebuild.

### 2026-06-11 - Expo Go demo mode + shareable Pro Android APK
- Goal: let a friend abroad try the app. iOS realities (documented here so nobody re-litigates them): you CANNOT build iOS on this Windows machine, there is no `ios/` native project, and installing on a real iPhone requires Apple code-signing (a paid Apple Developer account — TestFlight or ad-hoc). A "local network" can't reach a remote friend. **Expo Go + tunnel** is the only no-Apple-account route, BUT the app statically imports two third-party NATIVE libs that are NOT in the Expo Go runtime — `react-native-compressor` (compression-service + compression.service) and `react-native-background-actions` (the two background workers) — so vanilla Expo Go crashes on launch. (Every other dep, incl. expo-secure-store / expo-local-authentication / expo-image-manipulator / expo-file-system / reanimated / svg, IS in Expo Go.)
- Expo Go demo (no source changes — Metro alias gated by an env flag):
  - New `demo/stubs/react-native-compressor.js` + `demo/stubs/react-native-background-actions.js` — JS stubs. Compressor: `compress()` rejects (compression jobs fail gracefully via the store's friendly-error mapping), `getFileSize`→undefined (callers fall back to size estimates), `createVideoThumbnail`→`{path:uri}`. BackgroundService: `start` runs the task INLINE as plain foreground JS (the Smart Clean scan still runs — its documented degraded path), `stop`/`updateNotification` no-op, `isRunning` tracked so the worker guards behave.
  - `metro.config.js`: when `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_EXPO_GO_DEMO === "1"`, `resolver.resolveRequest` aliases those two package names to the stubs; otherwise untouched default resolution. ALL native builds (gradle/EAS) and normal `expo start` do NOT set the flag → real modules. Unit-verified (flag off → no custom resolver; on → stub paths; other modules pass through) and `expo export --platform ios` in demo mode bundled with **0 resolution errors** (6.87 MB hbc).
  - `package.json` script `start:demo` = `set EXPO_PUBLIC_EXPO_GO_DEMO=1&& expo start --tunnel --go` (demo alias + ngrok tunnel). Workflow: `npm run start:demo` -> share the QR/URL; the friend installs **Expo Go** from the App/Play Store and scans (Camera app on iOS). Works in Expo Go on iOS AND Android. The Metro dev server must stay running on this machine while the friend uses it. As of 2026-06-13 this no longer Pro-unlocks; Pro still requires an active RevenueCat subscription.
  - WORKS in the demo: swipe/review, month/stats/largest-photos, Settings incl. App Lock + biometric + reminders, full i18n, and Smart Clean PHOTO detectors (large / screenshots / duplicate / similar / blurry / memes). NOT available: media compression (compressor stubbed) and duplicate-VIDEO detection (needs compressor thumbnails); the background scan runs as foreground JS, not a true service. (These are the accepted trade-offs of the Expo Go route.)
- Shareable Pro Android APK: the standalone release APK built earlier this session (Pro via `EXPO_PUBLIC_FORCE_PRO`, all latest code) was copied to **`dist-share/SwipeClean-Pro.apk`** (117.5 MB). This was a historical artifact only; after the 2026-06-12 active-subscription-only change and 2026-06-13 cleanup, do not rebuild Pro-share APKs via FORCE_PRO.
- Files: new `demo/stubs/{react-native-compressor,react-native-background-actions}.js`, edited `metro.config.js`, `package.json`, `.gitignore` (added `dist-share/`, `dist-demo-check/`); copied `dist-share/SwipeClean-Pro.apk`. No app source changes, no new deps, no i18n. Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0; stubs are JS, not type-checked; app still resolves the REAL module types), metro-alias unit check, and a clean demo `expo export`. The demo flag must NEVER be set for a real store build.

### 2026-06-11 - RevenueCat subscription implementation
- What changed: replaced the placeholder billing path with a client-side RevenueCat integration while keeping the existing Free/Pro feature-access layer. The app now initializes RevenueCat from the root layout, refreshes customer info on app foreground, maps the RevenueCat `pro` entitlement into the local subscription snapshot, fetches monthly/yearly packages for the Premium screen, runs purchases, and restores purchases. The paywall still routes to the Premium tab; Pro gates remain centralized through `feature-flags.ts` and `feature-access.service.ts`.
- New dependency/config: added `react-native-purchases@10.3.0`; added Android Play Billing permission (`com.android.vending.BILLING`) in `app.json` and `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`. The "Upgrade to Premium" row is now hidden for Pro users (`useFeatureAccess().isPro`).

Test Store note: subscriptions are time-compressed (a "yearly"/"monthly" lasted ~1 hour) and auto-renew several cycles, so waiting for expiry is unreliable for returning to Free — hence the `localCancelled` override for testing.

### 2026-06-12 - Compress All: Pause / Resume / Stop controls
- What changed: a running "Compress All" batch is now controllable. While compression is active (`activeJobId` set or queue non-empty), the Compress-All button on the Compress tab is replaced by a row — a primary "Pause Compression" button (toggles to "Resume Compression" while paused) + a circular icon-only red Stop button (lucide `Square`). Added a transient `paused` flag (NOT persisted) and three actions to `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`: `pauseCompression()` (sets `paused`; `startNextJob` early-returns and its `.finally` recursion is guarded, so the queue halts AFTER the current item finishes — native image compression can't pause mid-encode), `resumeCompression()` (clears `paused`, kicks `startNextJob`), and `stopCompression()` (marks the active + all queued jobs `cancelled`, clears `queue`/`activeJobId`, and calls `CompressionNotifications.stopActive()` to tear down the foreground service + notification). The existing `cancelled` guards in `markCompleted`/`markFailed`/`updateProgress` mean a stopped item's late native result is discarded and the original file is never touched; cancelled (still-uncompressed) items remain compressible and stay in the grid.
- Files: `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`, `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, all `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (added `cleanup.pauseCompression`/`resumeCompression`/`stopCompression`).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` + `npm run i18n:check` (480 keys) passed. JS-only (no native rebuild — Metro Fast-Refresh). On-device (Pro): Compress All → foreground notification + Pause/Stop controls; Pause halts after the current item; Resume continues; Stop cancels the queue and returns to Compress All.

The cold-start lock (the `initializedRef`-gated effect) is unchanged, so the lock screen is asked ONCE per fresh process launch; backgrounding / dismissing the app and reopening it no longer re-prompts. ### 2026-06-12 - Grid performance: pagination + virtualization + scroll-to-top (Compress grid + Smart Clean review sheet)
- What changed: tightened both thumbnail grids' `FlatList` virtualization so they mount/load only near-visible cells instead of a large up-front burst (a big review set froze on open).
  - Compress grid (`src/screens/history-screen.tsx`): `windowSize` 9→5, `maxToRenderPerBatch` 12→6, `initialNumToRender` 12→9, `removeClippedSubviews`. Off-screen `MediaCard`s don't mount, so off-screen `CachedImage`s and per-video `CompressionService.createThumbnail` calls never run. ALSO **paginated** (same `PAGE_SIZE` 60 / `pagedMedia` slice / `onEndReached` grows `visibleCount`, reset on filter change — Compress All & totals still use the full `filteredMedia`), plus a **scroll-to-top floating button** (lucide `ArrowUp`, appears after scrolling >600px via an `onScroll` handler + a `listRef.scrollToOffset`, label `cleanup.scrollToTop`).
  - Smart Clean review sheet (`src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx`): uses **PAGINATION** — only the first `PAGE_SIZE` (60) items are handed to the FlatList, growing by a page on `onEndReached` (footer spinner while `hasMore`); `visibleCount` resets to `PAGE_SIZE` when the sheet (re)opens or the target changes. This keeps a huge target (e.g. "Review all", thousands of items) from rendering all cells at once. The FlatList itself uses a plain window (`windowSize={7}`, **no** `getItemLayout`, **no** `removeClippedSubviews` — `removeClippedSubviews` detaches off-screen image cells on Android and made scrolling feel "replaced" rather than scrolling; don't re-add it here). Selection still spans ALL candidates (selected-by-default), so confirming deletes unrendered items too.
- Files: `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, `src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx`.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` passed; JS-only (Metro Fast-Refresh). On-device: a large Smart Clean review (incl. "Review all") and the Compress grid scroll smoothly, with thumbnails filling in as rows enter view rather than all at once.

- What changed: the full-screen review viewer (`src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-preview-overlay.tsx`) is now draggable and videos get a custom player UI instead of native controls. Drag DOWN (photo or video) and the media follows the finger, shrinks + rounds, and the black backdrop fades to reveal the review sheet behind it; release past ~⅕ of the screen (or a fast flick, `velocityY > 950`) animates it closed, otherwise it springs back — the same `Gesture.Pan` + reanimated pattern as `compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx`. The dismiss gesture uses `activeOffsetY(14)` + `failOffsetX([-18,18])` so only a clear downward drag dismisses (horizontal seeking never closes it). The scrubber + time labels are driven by polling `player.currentTime` / `player.duration` on a 250ms interval. Reuses existing i18n (`compressionViewer.playVideo` / `pauseVideo`, `swipeCard.muteVideo` / `unmuteVideo`, `common.cancel`) — no new keys, no new native module (gesture-handler / reanimated / expo-video already in use).

### 2026-06-12 - Fix: app crash when opening a video viewer from the Swipe screen
- Symptom: opening a video to full-screen view from the Swipe screen hard-crashed the app.
- Root cause (high-confidence; device was offline so the exact logcat/tombstone wasn't captured): the preview opens as a `presentation: "modal"` route, so the Swipe screen stays mounted underneath and its `SwipePhotoCard` video keeps decoding. Opening the preview spins up a SECOND video player on the same file, so two hardware video decoders run at once — on the Galaxy S24 (esp. HEVC/4K) that exceeds the concurrent-decoder limit and throws a native MediaCodec error → crash. Pausing alone wouldn't help (ExoPlayer keeps the codec while paused).
- Fix: `SwipePhotoCard` now releases its video player while the Swipe screen is blurred. A `useFocusEffect` (from `expo-router`, no new dep) drives a `screenFocused` flag; when false (preview modal open or another tab active) the card renders a plain background fill instead of `<VideoMediaPlayer>`, which unmounts the player and frees the decoder. It remounts/auto-resumes on refocus. The card is covered or off-screen while blurred, so the swap is invisible. Mute preference (card-level state) survives the remount.
- Files: `src/components/swipe-photo-card.tsx`.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` passed; JS-only. CONFIRMED ON THE S24 — opening a video viewer from Swipe no longer crashes; logcat shows the decoder opening and shutting down cleanly (`Codec shutdown complete`, no `CodecException`/FATAL/tombstone).

### 2026-06-12 - Fix: chronic OOM crashes from full-res video-thumbnail decoding (+ safe video thumbnails)
- Symptom: the app hard-crashed repeatedly (10+ `data_app_native_crash` in one day per Android dropbox), typically while scrolling video-heavy grids (Largest, Smart Clean review, Compress, etc.). Crash buffer showed `MediaCodec.BufferInfo` JNI aborts on the `MediaCodec_loop` thread, immediately preceded by a cascade of `OutOfMemoryError`s (single allocations of ~100 MB up to ~1.9 GB against the 256 MB Java heap).
- Root cause: a video's first frame is decoded at FULL resolution. BOTH thumbnail paths do this — expo-image/Glide's `VideoDecoder` and `react-native-compressor`'s `createVideoThumbnail` both call `MediaMetadataRetriever.getFrameAtTime` (the unscaled variant; `createVideoThumbnail.kt` doesn't downscale). A ≤4K frame is ~33 MB (decodes fine) but a 6K/8K frame is 100+ MB, which blows the heap; the `MediaCodec.BufferInfo` abort is just the first allocation to fail once the heap is exhausted. The S24's `largeHeap` is OFF (256 MB cap). Pre-existing (crashes predate today's UI work).
- Fix (JS-only; no rebuild, no new dependency):
  - New `src/components/video-thumb-placeholder.tsx` — `isVideoUri()` (matches local `…/name.mp4`-style URIs) + a non-decoding `VideoThumbPlaceholder` tile (play glyph on a surface).
  - `src/components/cached-image.tsx` — now renders the placeholder for any video URI UNLESS the new `allowVideo` prop is set, so a stray video URI can never trigger a full-res decode.
  - New `src/components/media-thumbnail.tsx` — the shared grid/list thumbnail. Photos pass straight through `CachedImage`. Videos render their REAL frame (via `CachedImage allowVideo`) with a play badge overlay ONLY when the source resolution is known and ≤ `SAFE_MAX_DIMENSION` (4096); otherwise the placeholder. Missing dimensions are looked up from the media index by id (`selectIndexedMediaAsset`), since e.g. `SmartCleanItem` doesn't carry width/height.
  - `src/services/compression-service.ts` — `createThumbnail` now rejects videos whose max dimension is >4096 or unknown (callers already fall back to the placeholder). This protects the Compress grid (`history-screen` thumbnail), `compression-detail`, and the `tier2-duplicate-videos` scan (which extracts a frame per candidate video) from the same OOM.
  - `src/components/swipe-photo-card.tsx` — the stack-card thumbnail uses `MediaThumbnail`; the `Image.prefetch` of upcoming cards now excludes video URIs (prefetch was decoding video frames too).
  - Call sites switched `CachedImage` → `MediaThumbnail`: `largest-photos-screen`, `selected-photos-screen`, `stats-screen`, `photo-grid`, `photo-list-item`, swipe stack, and the Smart Clean review grid (video cells).
- Trade-off / known limitation: 6K/8K videos show the play-tile placeholder instead of a frame — there is no JS-only way to decode their frame within a 256 MB heap. A real fix for those would need `android:largeHeap="true"` (one native rebuild) or a native downscaled extractor (`getScaledFrameAtTime`); both deferred per the no-rebuild constraint.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` + Android bundle build pass. On a clean S24 relaunch with this code, logcat showed 0 `getFrameAtTime`, 0 `OutOfMemoryError`, 0 crash (vs 711 frame decodes + the OOM storm beforehand); ≤4K video thumbnails render with a play badge, 8K shows the placeholder. NOTE: a cold relaunch can briefly serve a STALE bundle if it races Metro's re-transform — one intermediate run reproduced the old OOMs until a rebuild-then-relaunch; always confirm the new bundle is live before judging a device result.
