### 2026-06-14 - Android single-item compression result flow (auto-open viewer + peek/expand sheet)
- What changed (ANDROID only; iOS unchanged): reworked the post-completion UX for a SINGLE compression. When an item's job transitions to `completed` while the user is still on the compression-detail screen, the detail screen `router.replace`s itself with the media viewer in "result mode" — the compressed media plus a new peek/expand bottom sheet showing the before/after sizes and the original-file actions (Delete original / Keep original / Delete compressed copy). Close (and Android system back) return to the origin.
- Why `replace` (the bug it fixed): the first cut used `router.push`, leaving the detail screen underneath the viewer. After the user deleted the original (its asset leaves the media index), pressing Android back popped the viewer and revealed the now-asset-less detail screen showing "Media not found". `replace` removes the detail screen from the back stack so that can't happen; a focus-gated safety net in the detail screen also bails to the origin if it's ever reached for a compressed-and-deleted item (covers the manual "View Compressed" / notification paths).
- Behavior details: result mode = `Platform.OS === "android"` AND (`result=1` param OR a compressed copy exists for the id). In result mode the media's drag-down-to-dismiss is disabled (the sheet owns the bottom gesture) and the "swipe down" hint is hidden. The sheet is a custom Reanimated snap-point sheet (peek ≈ handle + "You saved X" summary + chevron; expand = full numbers + action buttons; drag up/down or tap to toggle — it never fully dismisses). Close uses `router.dismissTo(origin ?? "/(tabs)/history")`; `origin` is threaded as a route param from the Compress grid → detail → viewer, and the completion notification deep-link now carries `result=1`. The global `CompressionCompleteSheet` is gated to BATCH-only on Android (single + batch still on iOS), so it never double-shows with the in-viewer sheet. Reused existing `compression.*`/`compressionDetail.*` i18n keys — NO new keys (parity stays 425).
- Files: new `src/features/compression/components/compression-result-sheet.tsx`; edited `src/screens/compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx` (result mode, dynamic-origin Close, disabled media drag, render sheet), `src/screens/compression-detail-screen.tsx` (focus-gated auto-open via `replace`, origin threading, missing-asset safety net), `src/features/compression/components/compression-complete-sheet.tsx` (Android batch-only), `src/screens/history-screen.tsx` (pass `origin`), `src/features/compression/compression.notifications.ts` (`result=1` on the viewer deep-link).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0); `npm run i18n:check` (425 keys); `npm run lint` (0 errors). JS-only (Metro Fast-Refresh; no native rebuild). On-device exercise pending — the emulator's sample media is below the compressible threshold, so a 12MP photo + 25s video were generated/pushed as test items; the user is verifying the flow manually. A Maestro flow for this path is NOT yet added (compressible media must be present on the device first).
- iOS NOT done (deferred, per the earlier flow discussion): the iOS version would be foreground-bound with resume-on-foreground rather than a true background service; the global complete sheet still handles iOS single completions for now.

### 2026-06-14 - Marked for Deletion perf: single virtualized list + pagination (fixes multi-second open)
- Problem: opening the "Marked for Deletion" review screen with many queued items took several seconds. The screen wrapped everything in one `ScrollView`, and each month group rendered a `PhotoGrid` that was itself a `FlatList` with `scrollEnabled={false}`. A non-scrolling FlatList inside a ScrollView defeats virtualization entirely, so EVERY marked thumbnail (each a `MediaThumbnail` that can decode a video frame) mounted synchronously on open → the freeze.
- What changed (JS-only, both platforms): rewrote `review-delete-list-screen.tsx` to render a SINGLE virtualized `FlatList`. The marked items (filtered to the active scope) are stable-sorted by month descending, then flattened into list rows — a month-header row (carrying that month's FULL count/bytes from a precomputed totals map) followed by rows of up to 3 tiles. Added incremental pagination: only `visibleCount` items (start `PAGE_SIZE` = 60) are materialized and grouped; `onEndReached` grows the window by `PAGE_SIZE`. The summary card is `ListHeaderComponent`, the red Delete button is `ListFooterComponent`, and the empty state is `ListEmptyComponent`; the back/title bar is now fixed above the list (was scrolling). FlatList tuned with `initialNumToRender`/`maxToRenderPerBatch`/`windowSize`/`removeClippedSubviews`.
- `photo-grid.tsx` refactor: replaced the old whole-group `PhotoGrid` (`FlatList`) with a memoized single-tile `PhotoTile` (+ `getPhotoTileSize(width)` and `PHOTO_GRID_COLUMNS`/`PHOTO_GRID_GAP` constants). Tiles are `React.memo`'d and the screen passes stable `onRestore` (zustand action) / `onOpen` (`useCallback`) refs, so appending a page during scroll doesn't re-render already-mounted tiles. `PhotoGrid` was used ONLY by this screen, so nothing else changed.
- Correctness notes: the Delete action still deletes the ENTIRE marked scope (`marked.map(photoId)`), not just the loaded page. Month headers stay accurate while a month is partially paged in because their count/bytes come from the full per-month totals, not the visible slice. Pagination resets to `PAGE_SIZE` when the month/media-type scope changes via a render-phase "adjust state when a key changes" pattern (not a `useEffect`, so no React-Compiler `set-state-in-effect` warning and no flash of the stale window).
- Files: `src/screens/review-delete-list-screen.tsx`, `src/components/photo-grid.tsx`, and `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new dependencies, no new i18n keys (parity stays 425), no native change.
- Consistent with the 2026-06-12 "Grid performance: pagination + virtualization" pass that fixed the Compress grid and Smart Clean review sheet — same single-virtualized-list + paginate approach, now applied to the deletion review screen.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0), `npm run i18n:check` (425 keys, all 9 locales in parity), `npm run lint` (0 errors, 0 warnings on the changed files). Live on the S24 dev client via Metro Fast-Refresh. On-device confirmation of the large-queue open time is pending (user-driven device testing).

### 2026-06-14 - Compression: reject a "compressed" output that isn't smaller than the original
- Bug (reported from device): a video "compressed" to 9.8 MB from a 6.2 MB original — the output was LARGER than the source, yet the app saved that copy to the gallery and showed it as the result. Root cause: `verifyCompressedOutput` (in `src/features/compression/compression.service.ts`) validated only that the output existed and the original size was known; it never checked the output was actually smaller, so it returned `isValid: true` with a NEGATIVE `savedBytes`, and `compressMediaJob` proceeded to `saveToLibrary`. (The original was NOT lost — `deleteOriginal` already guards on `savedBytes <= 0` — but a bigger copy was still written and a negative-savings result was shown.)
- Why it happens: already-efficiently-encoded sources (low-bitrate or short videos) re-encoded by `react-native-compressor`'s `Video.compress` ("auto", `maxSize` per profile, `minimumFileSizeForCompress: 0`) to H.264 at the profile's `videoMaxSize` can come out the same size or larger. The `isCompressible` video gate (`>= 20 MB` OR `duration >= 20s`) admits such clips, so the encode is attempted and can grow the file.
- Fix (JS-only, both platforms): `verifyCompressedOutput` now returns `isValid: false` when `finalSizeBytes >= knownOriginalSize` (reason: "This file is already optimized…"). Because verification runs BEFORE `saveToLibrary`, a no-gain re-encode now fails the job and NO larger copy is written to the gallery; the original is untouched. Added an "already optimized" branch to `getFriendlyCompressionError` (in `compression.store.ts`) — placed FIRST so the generic `/space/` branch can't swallow it — surfacing a calm, non-retry message instead of "We could not compress this file. Please try again."
- Files: `src/features/compression/compression.service.ts`, `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; error strings are inline English (consistent with the existing `getFriendlyCompressionError`), so no new i18n keys (parity stays 425).
- Known limitation / possible follow-up: for Free users the daily video slot is consumed in the detail screen's `handleWatchAd` (after the rewarded ad, before the job runs), so a video that now fails as "already optimized" still costs the user an ad + one of their 2 daily slots. This is pre-existing (any video failure already costs the slot); a quota refund-on-failure would need wiring the failure path back to `free-video-quota.store.ts`. Also not changed: the `isCompressible` heuristic still attempts these clips (no bitrate signal available to pre-skip them).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (exit 0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm test` (24/24 pass), `npm run i18n:check` (425 keys). Live via Metro Fast-Refresh; on-device re-test of the exact clip pending (user-driven).

### 2026-06-14 - Compress All: ask up front whether to delete or keep originals
- Change (requested): the batch "Compress All" flow now asks the user UP FRONT what to do with the original files, instead of only prompting after the whole batch finished. Tapping "Compress All" opens a dialog with "Delete all originals" / "Keep originals" / Cancel; the chosen policy runs the batch and is applied automatically when it completes.
- Why: previously the decision was a post-batch sheet (`CompressionCompleteSheet` → `deleteAllOriginals`/`keepAllOriginals`). Moving it up front lets the user commit once before a long batch and walk away, and matches the request.
- How: added `BatchOriginalPolicy = "ask" | "delete" | "keep"` to `compression.types.ts`; `CompressionBatchInput` and the stored `CompressionBatch` now carry `originalPolicy` (default `"ask"`). `enqueueCompressionBatch` stores it. `refreshBatchSummary` (which finalizes the batch on the last job's completion/failure) now only sets `shouldAskDeleteOriginals` when policy is `"ask"`, and for `"delete"`/`"keep"` auto-calls `deleteAllOriginals`/`keepAllOriginals` once `isDone && completedCount > 0`. So the post-batch sheet is skipped for Compress All but still serves the legacy `"ask"` flow and single-item iOS completions. The existing safety guards are unchanged: `deleteAllOriginals` only deletes completed jobs that actually saved space (`savedBytes > 0`) and `deleteOriginal` still refuses without a verified, saved compressed copy — so the "already optimized" / no-shrink fix composes (a no-gain item is never deleted even under `"delete"`).
- UI: new `src/components/compress-all-original-dialog.tsx` (custom centered Modal matching `VideoCompressAdDialog`). `history-screen.tsx`'s `handleCompressAll` now opens the dialog; `runCompressAll(policy)` builds the jobs and calls `enqueueCompressionBatch({ ..., originalPolicy })`. Stop/Pause/Resume during the batch are unchanged (a stopped/cancelled batch does NOT auto-delete, since `cancelJob` doesn't finalize).
- Platform scope (refined same day): the upfront dialog is ANDROID ONLY — the iOS compress workflow is owned by a separate agent. `handleCompressAll` opens the dialog only when `Platform.OS === "android"`; otherwise it calls `runCompressAll("ask")`, which is byte-for-byte the pre-change iOS behavior (enqueue with default policy → legacy `CompressionCompleteSheet`). The dialog is also render-gated to Android. The store changes stay platform-agnostic and backward-compatible (default `"ask"`), so iOS is fully unchanged. Verified by an adversarial multi-agent isolation audit (iOS behavior unchanged; shared no-shrink/deletion-perf fixes correctly cross-platform; gating consistent with the existing `Platform.OS` compression conventions) — 0 confirmed issues.
- i18n: 2 new keys (`compression.compressAllTitle`, `compression.compressAllOriginalsPrompt`) translated across all 10 locales (parity 425 → 427); buttons reuse existing `deleteAllOriginalsButton`/`keepAllOriginalsButton`/`deleteWarning`/`common.cancel`.
- Files: `src/features/compression/compression.types.ts`, `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`, `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, new `src/components/compress-all-original-dialog.tsx`, all 10 `src/i18n/locales/*.json`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; JS-only (no native change).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run i18n:check` (427, all 9 in parity), `npm run lint` (0 errors; 2 PRE-EXISTING warnings in `history-screen.tsx` effects, unrelated), `npm test` (24/24). Live via Metro Fast-Refresh; on-device exercise pending (user-driven).

### 2026-06-14 - Delete-all-originals asks once for the whole batch (single native delete)
- Bug (reported from device): after a batch compression, deleting the originals popped Android's system delete-consent dialog ONCE PER IMAGE. Root cause: `deleteAllOriginals` looped over completed jobs calling `deleteOriginal(job.id)` one at a time, and each `deleteOriginal` → `deleteOriginalMedia` → `MediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync([oneId])` raises its own per-asset consent prompt on Android (scoped storage). N originals → N prompts.
- Fix (shared, both platforms): `deleteAllOriginals` now collects every deletable original and deletes them in ONE native call so Android shows a SINGLE consent dialog for the whole batch (iOS likewise asks once). Added `deleteOriginalMediaBatch(mediaIds[])` to `compression-deletion.service.ts` (one permission check + one `PhotoLibraryService.deletePhotos(ids)` → one `MediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync(ids)`); the store marks all targeted jobs in-progress, then on success marks each `markOriginalDeleted` / on failure `markOriginalDeleteFailed`. The per-item `deleteOriginal` is unchanged (still used by the single-item flow and per-item review).
- Safety: the bulk path applies the FULL per-item guard up front (only `completed` jobs that saved space AND have a verified, durably saved compressed copy — `outputUri`/`finalSizeBytes`/`libraryAssetId`), so it can never delete an original lacking a saved compressed copy. This composes with the no-shrink fix (no-gain items are excluded). This affects BOTH the Android upfront `"delete"` auto-apply and the iOS/legacy post-batch "Delete all originals" button — it is shared deletion logic, NOT a workflow/UX change, so it does not alter the iOS compress workflow flow (still the post-batch sheet → Delete all), it just makes the underlying deletion one prompt instead of N.
- Files: `src/features/compression/compression-deletion.service.ts`, `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; JS-only (no native change).
- Known limitation (FIXED — see the next entry): `PhotoLibraryService.deletePhotos` treated a resolved `deleteAssetsAsync` as success without inspecting its boolean return, so a user who DENIED the system dialog was still recorded as deleted.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm test` (24/24). Live via Metro Fast-Refresh; on-device re-test of the batch delete-consent pending (user-driven).

### 2026-06-14 - Deletion honors deleteAssetsAsync's boolean (denied consent no longer recorded as deleted)
- Bug (data-integrity): `MediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync` resolves `Promise<boolean>` (`false` when nothing was deleted — most often the user denying Android's system delete-consent dialog), but `PhotoLibraryService.deletePhotos` ignored the return and reported `success: true` whenever the call didn't throw. So denying the dialog still removed items from app state / recorded deletion history + stats while the files remained on the device — across review-delete, Smart Clean, AND compression original deletion (all route through `deletePhotos`).
- Fix: `deletePhotos` now inspects the boolean and returns `{ success: false, message: "The photos were not deleted." }` when `deleteAssetsAsync` resolves `false`. All consumers already treat `success: false` as a hard failure and abort: `permanentlyDeleteMarked` throws (so review-delete shows its failure Alert and Smart Clean keeps its sheet open + skips its ledger event), and the compression `deleteOriginalMedia`/`deleteOriginalMediaBatch` throw → the job is marked `delete_failed`. Also hardened `deleteCompressedMediaCopy` the same way (throws on `false`).
- Result: a denied/canceled system delete now correctly KEEPS the media in state (nothing is falsely reported deleted, no false history/stats), and the user sees an accurate "not deleted" message.
- Files: `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`, `src/features/compression/compression-deletion.service.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; JS-only. Shared/cross-platform (correct on iOS too).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm test` (24/24). Live via Metro Fast-Refresh; on-device test (deny the system dialog → items stay) pending (user-driven).

### 2026-06-14 - Video compression made effective (bitrate-targeted) + batch lag fix
- Bug (reported + reproduced on-device): "Compress All" on videos was laggy and every video "failed". Device diagnostic (`[CompressDiag]` instrumentation, since removed) showed the real cause: `react-native-compressor`'s `compressionMethod: "auto"` re-encodes already-low-bitrate clips at a resolution-based bitrate that is HIGHER than the source, producing files 1.5–4.5× LARGER than the original (e.g. 3.6 MB → 16.5 MB, 7.2 MB → 11 MB). The no-shrink guard then correctly rejected them → a wall of "Failed", and each wasted ~11 s encode = the lag.
- Fix (in `src/services/compression-service.ts`, shared / both platforms):
  1. **Manual bitrate targeting** — `compressVideo` now uses `compressionMethod: "manual"` with `bitrate = min(profile.videoTargetBitrate, sourceBitrate * profile.videoBitrateRatio)` (caps per profile: low 1.8 Mbps/0.5, medium 3.5 Mbps/0.6, high 6 Mbps/0.7). The target is always below the source bitrate, so output is genuinely smaller. Falls back to `"auto"` only when duration/bitrate is unknown.
  2. **Bitrate-aware `isCompressible`** — a video is offered only if its source bitrate ≥ `VIDEO_MIN_COMPRESSIBLE_BITRATE` (4 Mbps). Already-compressed low-bitrate clips (downloads/social) are treated as already-optimized and not attempted, so no wasted encodes (the main batch lag) and no "Failed" wall. Unknown-duration videos fall back to the old `VIDEO_MIN_BYTES` size gate.
  3. **Bitrate-aware `estimate`** — compressed size = `originalBytes * (targetBitrate / sourceBitrate)` (duration cancels), so the global "Estimated savings" is realistic instead of a flat ratio that over-promised (the old "8.4 GB" type figure).
  4. **Progress throttle** (`compression.store.ts`) — mid-encode progress updates to the store + notification are throttled to ~4/sec (`PROGRESS_THROTTLE_MS` 250, edges always pass), so a long video doesn't re-render the compress grid on every native tick.
- The no-shrink verification guard stays as the final safety net (a re-encode that still isn't smaller is rejected before save).
- Verified on-device (S24): after the fix a 63 MB camera video compressed to 41.6 MB (**saved 21 MB**, valid) and photos to ~150 KB; the auto-method "bigger output" failures are gone. Note: the bitrate gate re-classifies the compressible pool only after an **Estimate again** (the persisted media index keeps the prior classification until then).
- Files: `src/services/compression-service.ts`, `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; JS-only (no native rebuild). `VIDEO_MIN_COMPRESSIBLE_BITRATE` and the per-profile bitrate caps/ratios are the tuning knobs. Diagnostic confirmed `ACCESS_MEDIA_LOCATION` is NOT involved (no cache-copy fallback fired).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm test` (24/24), plus the on-device batch run above.

### 2026-06-14 - Compression modes redefined (Low/Medium/High) + mode picker un-gated for everyone
- Product change (requested): the `low/medium/high` quality selector was redefined around a clear size↔quality trade-off and made available to ALL users (was Pro-only). Applies to BOTH photos and videos (they share `compressionProfiles`).
  - **Low** ("Max savings") — files ~80% smaller, quality noticeably reduced (`imageQuality` 0.35 / `imageMaxSize` 1080; video `videoBitrateRatio` 0.2, cap 5 Mbps, `videoMaxSize` 720; `estimatedRatio` 0.2).
  - **Medium** ("Balanced", default) — ~50% smaller for ~half the quality (`imageQuality` 0.55 / 1600; video ratio 0.5, cap 10 Mbps, 1080; `estimatedRatio` 0.5).
  - **High** ("Best quality") — keeps original quality, compresses gently / saves less (`imageQuality` 0.9 / 4096; video ratio 0.8, cap 25 Mbps, 2160; `estimatedRatio` 0.8).
- Bitrate-target math: video target = `min(cap, sourceBitrate * ratio)`, so the ratio sets the savings (≈80/50/20%) and the cap protects very-high-bitrate sources from over-compression. The per-resolution caps are generous so the ratio dominates for typical footage.
- Un-gating: moved `advancedCompressionSettings` from PRO → FREE in `feature-flags.ts`. `compression-detail-screen.tsx` was NOT otherwise changed — `advancedUnlocked` (= `canUseFeature("advancedCompressionSettings")`) is now always true, so the picker is active for everyone and the Pro-badge / locked-note / paywall branches are inert (left in place, harmless). The `subscription.advancedCompressionSettings` paywall string is now unused (kept for parity). Decisions per user: picker for EVERYONE, kept PER-ITEM (Compress All batch still uses a fixed Medium).
- The profile `fidelity` field was repurposed from a "20/50/80%" number to a short tag ("Max savings"/"Balanced"/"Best quality"); `label`/`fidelity`/`description` are used ONLY in `compression-detail-screen.tsx`. These three display strings remain hardcoded English (pre-existing — they were never localized); localizing the mode UI is a possible follow-up.
- The 4 Mbps compressibility gate (`VIDEO_MIN_COMPRESSIBLE_BITRATE`) is unchanged and orthogonal: it decides WHICH videos are offered; the mode decides HOW hard an offered item is compressed.
- Files: `src/services/compression-service.ts` (profiles), `src/features/subscription/feature-flags.ts` (tier move), `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps; JS-only. NOTE: `advancedCompressionSettings` is now a FREE feature — the historical 2026-06-09 entry above (which lists it under Pro) is superseded by this entry.
- Verified on-device (S24, Free tier): the photo compression-detail picker shows Low/Max-savings · Medium/Balanced · High/Best-quality with NO Pro lock and the new descriptions; `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors; 1 pre-existing unrelated warning), `npm test` (24/24).

### 2026-06-15 - Compression redesign: foreground single-item flow (remove Compress All + background)
- Why: batch ("Compress All") + background-service compression was laggy/error-prone and let the user roam away mid-compress. Replaced with a single-item, fully-FOREGROUND flow per the user-approved plan (`~/.claude/plans/sorted-mixing-glacier.md`). For BOTH platforms in this branch (iOS code-only — no iOS native project here; verified on the Android S24). Done in 3 committed phases.
- **Phase 1 (commit `7ddc25f`)** — removed Compress All + Pause/Stop. `history-screen` is now browse-grid + filter; deleted `compress-all-original-dialog.tsx`; removed the batch engine from `compression.store` (`enqueueCompressionBatch`, pause/resume/stop, `paused`, `batches`, `deleteAllOriginals`/`keepAllOriginals`/`reviewBatchItems`/`dismissBatchPrompt`, `refreshBatchSummary`) and the batch types (`CompressionBatch*`, `BatchOriginalPolicy`); `compression-complete-sheet` rewritten single-item-only.
- **Phases 2 + 3 (this commit)** — NEW foreground screen `src/screens/compress-run-screen.tsx` (route `app/compress-run.tsx`, registered with `gestureEnabled:false`): the detail screen's "Compress now" `router.push("/compress-run?id=…&quality=…&origin=…")` (after the Free-video rewarded-ad gate). The screen runs the single compression via `enqueueCompression` (now INLINE — `runCompressionJob` no longer uses RNBA `runInForeground`, the Smart Clean `releaseForegroundService` handshake, or progress notifications), shows live % + bar (back/gestures blocked while running), then the compressed media (`CachedImage`/`VideoMediaPlayer`) + before/after/saved, then **Keep / Delete original**. Keep → `keepOriginal` → `dismissTo(origin)`. Delete → App Lock PIN modal (`PasscodePad` + `AppLockService.verifyPasscode`/biometric; skipped if no passcode) → `deleteOriginal` (its guards + the OS delete-consent prompt + `markOriginalDeleted` index purge) → `dismissTo(origin)`. Failure shows Retry/Close.
- Removed background machinery: `applyPostCompressionPolicy` (the screen always asks; `settings.afterCompressionOriginalPolicy` is now dead), `notifyCompletedJob`, and the `_layout` mounts `CompressionLifecycle`/`CompressionProgressNotification`/`CompressionCompleteSheet` + the compression notification deep-link listener. **Deleted files:** `compression-complete-sheet.tsx`, `compression-lifecycle.tsx`, `compression-progress-notification.tsx`, `compression-banner.tsx`, `compression.notifications.ts`, `services/background-compression-worker.ts`. Smart Clean's own background scan is UNTOUCHED (separate worker; `FOREGROUND_SERVICE*` perms kept).
- Reuses: `compressMediaJob` (runs standalone, callback-driven), `markCompleted` (records to `compressedMedia` + cleanup-events ledger), `deleteOriginal`/`markOriginalDeleted` (guards + index purge), `PasscodePad`/`AppLockService`. i18n: 8 new `compressRun.*` keys across all 10 locales (parity 427 → 435).
- Deferred cleanup (harmless dead code, not reached by the new flow): the detail screen's auto-open/result UI, `compression-media-viewer-screen` + `compression-result-sheet.tsx`, the vestigial `CompressionJob.batchId/queuePosition/queueTotal`, and dead batch i18n keys.
- This SUPERSEDES the platform-split Compress All (the earlier "Android-only / iOS owned by another agent" arrangement) — expect merge conflicts with the other agent's iOS compress work.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm run i18n:check` (435, all 9 in parity), `npm test` (24/24). On-device end-to-end (S24) pending (user-driven).

### 2026-06-15 - Compress-run screen: in-progress UX overhaul (media preview, shimmer bar, live size, Cancel)

- What changed: Rewrote the `isRunning` branch of the foreground compress flow into a full fit-to-screen layout (both platforms — shared React Native/TS screen, no platform branches; `react-native-reanimated`, `expo-linear-gradient`, and `react-native-svg` behave identically on iOS and Android).
  - Media preview while compressing: the ORIGINAL media now renders during compression filling a `flex:1` rounded bordered frame — image via `CachedImage` or video via `VideoMediaPlayer`, both `contentFit="cover"` (an `ActivityIndicator` fallback when no `uri`). Previously nothing was shown during compression.
  - Alive animations: new `ShimmerProgressBar` component — a determinate accent fill animated to `percent` over 400ms (`withTiming`) plus a continuous white `LinearGradient` shimmer sweep that repeats forever (1200ms, `withRepeat`) so the bar reads as "working" even when `%` is momentarily static (image compression reports coarse progress).
  - Live ticking real→compressed size: computes `realBytes` (`job.originalSizeBytes ?? asset.sizeBytes`), `targetBytes` (clamped `job.estimatedReducedSizeBytes`), and `liveCompressedBytes` interpolated from real toward target by `job.progress`; rendered as `formatBytes(realBytes)` → (ArrowRight) → `formatBytes(liveCompressedBytes)` in green, so the compressed figure visibly ticks down in real time.
  - Animated label: new `WorkingLabel` cycles 1–3 ellipsis dots every 450ms after the title. Percentage downsized 64→40 with `tabular-nums`.
- Cancel during compression: new bordered Cancel button (`common.cancel`) → `handleCancel` fires an `Alert` confirm (`compressRun.cancelTitle`/`cancelMessage`) with a destructive `cancelConfirm` action that calls `cancelJob(job.id)` then `goHome`, plus a `keepCompressing` cancel action — previously back was simply blocked while running with no escape.
- Why: the prior in-progress screen was a centered percentage + flat bar + filename/hint with no preview, no sign of motion when `%` stalled, and no way to abort an in-flight compress.
- Files: `src/screens/compress-run-screen.tsx` (new imports: `Alert`, `StyleSheet`, `Animated`/`Easing`/`useAnimatedStyle`/`useSharedValue`/`withRepeat`/`withTiming` from reanimated, `LinearGradient` from `expo-linear-gradient`, `ArrowRight`; `cancelJob`/`appLockEnabled` selectors added; the old `filename` var removed).
- New dependencies: None (`react-native-reanimated`, `expo-linear-gradient`, `lucide-react-native` already present).
- i18n: 6 new `compressRun.*` keys across all 10 locales — `cancelTitle`, `cancelMessage`, `keepCompressing`, `cancelConfirm` (cancel flow) and `original`, `compressed` (shared with the before/after entry below). en.json 435 → 443 (see i18n entry); all 9 locales in parity.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm run i18n:check` (443, all 9 in parity). On-device end-to-end pending (uncommitted working-tree, not pushed).

### 2026-06-15 - PIN-on-delete gate: require App Lock to be ENABLED, not just a stored passcode

- Problem: in `compress-run-screen.tsx` `handleDeletePress`, the passcode gate before deleting the original was `if (await AppLockService.hasPasscode())`. A passcode can linger in the Keychain (which survives app reinstalls) while App Lock is toggled OFF, so gating on `hasPasscode()` alone prompted for a PIN the user never configured. This is effectively an iOS concern (the lingering-Keychain-across-reinstall behavior is iOS Keychain), though the code has no platform branch.
- What changed: the gate is now `if (appLockEnabled && (await AppLockService.hasPasscode()))`, where `appLockEnabled` is read from `useAppStore` `settings.appLockEnabled`. The PIN prompt before deleting the original now appears only when App Lock is actually enabled AND a passcode is stored; otherwise we go straight to the OS delete-consent prompt.
- Files: `src/screens/compress-run-screen.tsx`.
- New dependencies: None. i18n: none.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors). Uncommitted working-tree, not pushed.

### 2026-06-15 - Large-gallery memory crash fix: capped thumbnail decode + bounded image cache (both platforms)
- Problem (root-caused on-device via logcat + a meminfo trace on the S24): fast-scrolling large image/video grids (gallery / Marked-for-Deletion / **Smart Clean review** = worst) janked then crashed. The crash was a **memory crash**: every grid thumbnail went through `expo-image` with NO decode-size cap, so a 50+ MP photo decoded at FULL resolution — a single 222 MB ARGB_8888 allocation. Against the 256 MB ART heap that threw `OutOfMemoryError`, ART went into a GC death-spiral evicting bitmaps, the UI thread blocked >10 s → **ANR** (the "crash" the user saw). 48 OOMs in one run (single allocations 193–815 MB). `android:largeHeap="true"` is in the manifest but NOT in the installed dev client (runtime growth limit was 256 MB) — and largeHeap is NOT the fix anyway (concurrent full-res decodes overflow any heap).
- Fix (the real fix is capping decode resolution, not raising the heap):
  - NEW `src/services/thumbnail-service.ts` — downscales each grid source ONCE to a ~512 px disk-cached JPEG (`<cacheDir>/sc-thumb-{id}-{px}.jpg`) via **react-native-compressor** `Image.compress` (manual mode → Android `BitmapFactory.inSampleSize`, iOS ImageIO — downscales DURING decode, so the full-res bitmap is never allocated). Deliberately NOT `expo-image-manipulator`, whose `resize` decodes the full bitmap first (a first attempt with it stopped the crash but still spiked native heap to ~1.3 GB and lagged the first scroll). Videos: `createVideoThumbnail` → compress. Bounded concurrency (3), in-flight dedup, persistent disk cache, **fails safe to the original uri** on any error. react-native-compressor is statically linked (no native rebuild needed).
  - NEW `src/components/thumbnail.tsx` — resolves the cached uri and renders `expo-image` with **`cachePolicy="disk"`** (not `memory-disk`: decoded bitmaps live on the native heap and the memory cache retained them after they scrolled off → the 700–800 MB native heap; "disk" bounds resident bitmaps to ~the visible window), `allowDownscaling`, `priority="low"`, `recyclingKey`.
  - `src/components/media-thumbnail.tsx` — both photo AND video branches now route through `<Thumbnail/>` (was `CachedImage`); `SAFE_MAX_DIMENSION` 8192→4096 so video frame extraction itself can't OOM (this propagates the cap to the Marked-for-Deletion grid and the swipe-card stack too).
  - `src/features/smart-clean/components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` — photo cells use `<Thumbnail/>`; added `removeClippedSubviews`, `windowSize` 7→5.
  - `src/screens/selected-photos-screen.tsx` — extracted a **memoized `MediaTile`** + stabilized callbacks/colors + `removeClippedSubviews`/`windowSize=5`. Fixes a separate "All Media → trash icon takes several seconds" bug: in the all-media scope the grid holds the whole library and marking one item re-rendered every mounted tile; now only the tapped tile re-renders.
  - `app/_layout.tsx` — NEW `ImageCacheManager` calls `ExpoImage.clearMemoryCache()` on AppState `background`/`inactive`.
- Result (S24 logcat trace, user-confirmed "0 lag"): GL textures 128→19 MB, native heap ~700–800→peak 515/steady ~280 MB, **0 OOM, 0 ANR, 0 frame skips**, app stable.
- Platform scope: all cross-platform JS (no `Platform.OS` gates); the libs (react-native-compressor, expo-image, expo-image-manipulator, expo-file-system) all ship iOS native code, so the fix applies to iOS — but it is **UNVERIFIED on iOS** (this repo has no generated `ios/` project). It works across the full Android range (minSdk 24 / Android 7.0) and helps OLDER Android most (pre-API-26 bitmaps sit on the Java heap, so the cap matters more there). When building iOS, sanity-check `removeClippedSubviews` (more Android-oriented; can blank cells on iOS).
- Deferred: the `imagesReady` 60 ms hack in the review sheet is now obsolete (small pre-capped sources make the SIZE_ORIGINAL race moot) and still trips 2 advisory lint warnings; `getItemLayout` not added to the grids; a native rebuild for `largeHeap` remains a safety-margin-only follow-up.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0 errors), `npm run lint` (0 errors; only pre-existing `set-state-in-effect` warnings), Metro bundle builds. Not yet committed.

### 2026-06-15 - Video compression eligibility: add a size floor (bitrate OR size)
- Change: a video is now compressible if **size ≥ 8 MB (`VIDEO_MIN_BYTES`, lowered from 20 MB) OR bitrate ≥ 4 Mbps (`VIDEO_MIN_COMPRESSIBLE_BITRATE`)**. Previously it was bitrate-only when duration was known (≥4 Mbps), with the 20 MB size check used ONLY as the unknown-bitrate fallback — which hid large lower-bitrate clips (e.g. a ~10 MB clip > ~20 s reads < 4 Mbps and was under the 20 MB fallback). User-requested (size floor chosen = 8 MB) to surface those; they DO shrink because the encoder always targets below the source bitrate (Medium = source×0.5), with a bit more visible quality loss on low-bitrate sources (High mode minimizes it). The no-shrink guard still rejects the rare non-shrinking output. This intentionally revises the earlier (2026-06-14) "bitrate-only 4 Mbps gate" decision.
- Files: `src/services/compression-service.ts` (`isCompressible` video branch + the two constants/comments). NOTE: the saved global estimate/pool does NOT auto-refresh — newly-eligible videos appear after the user runs "Estimate again" (or a fresh full scan).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors), `npm test` (24 passed).

### 2026-06-15 - Compress a custom (user-picked) file — Pro unlimited, Free 1/day via rewarded ad (NEEDS NATIVE REBUILD)
- Feature: a "Compress a custom file" button on the Compress screen (`history-screen.tsx`, in the list header under Filter) opens the OS image/video picker; the chosen file runs through the normal foreground compress flow. **Pro** users (new PRO feature key `customCompression` in `feature-flags.ts`) pick unlimited + ad-free. **Free** users are gated to **1/day** (`free-custom-compress-quota.store.ts`, `FREE_DAILY_CUSTOM_LIMIT = 1`, persisted `swipeclean-free-custom-compress-quota`) AND must watch a rewarded ad each time (`CustomCompressAdDialog` → `RewardedAdService.showForReward()` → record quota → pick). Once the daily 1 is used, the button routes to `/premium`. Mirrors the existing free-video rewarded pattern.
- NEW dependency: **`expo-image-picker` ~17.0.11** (native module `ExponentImagePicker`) + its config plugin in `app.json` (photo-library usage string). **This is a NATIVE module → the dev client MUST be rebuilt (`:app:assembleDebug` + reinstall) before the picker works.** Until then it degrades gracefully: `custom-media-picker.ts` capability-probes via `requireOptionalNativeModule` and the button shows a "needs an updated build" alert (`customCompress.needsUpdate`) — no crash on the current APK (the wrapper is only dynamically imported AFTER the probe passes, per the native-capabilities rule).
- Custom-file path: a picked file is NOT in the media index, so `custom-media-picker.ts` builds a synthetic `PhotoAsset` (id `custom:<uri>`, duration converted ms→s) and stashes it in the transient `custom-compress.store.ts`; `compress-run-screen.tsx` resolves the asset from that store when `custom=1` (fallback to the index otherwise). Custom files are **Keep-only** — the "original" is a picker cache copy, not a managed library asset, so the delete-original button + decision prompt are hidden and the Keep button reads "Done". Quality defaults to Medium (no detail screen in this path).
- Files: NEW `src/services/custom-media-picker.ts`, `src/features/compression/{free-custom-compress-quota,custom-compress}.store.ts`, `src/components/custom-compress-ad-dialog.tsx`; edited `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/compress-run-screen.tsx`, `src/features/subscription/feature-flags.ts`, `app.json`, all 10 `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (+5 `customCompress.*` keys, parity 437→442).
- Status: implemented + **device-verified on the S24 (2026-06-15)** — the dev client was rebuilt (`:app:assembleDebug`, 3m16s) with `expo-image-picker` autolinked and reinstalled (`adb install -r`, app data kept); the custom button opens the OS picker and the picked file compresses through the foreground flow (verified mid-compress on device). `typecheck` 0, `lint` 0 errors. iOS untested (no `ios/` project). Not committed.

### 2026-06-15 - Smart Clean scan redesign: single-decode pipeline + concurrent "parallel" pre-pass + lossless resume + iOS foreground model
- What changed: rebuilt the Smart Clean scan so categories surface together and far faster, with seamless pause/resume across platforms.
  - SINGLE DECODE: new `computeGrayFeatures(uri)` decodes a photo ONCE at the 64x64 blur grid and derives BOTH the dHash (average-pool 64x64→9x8 via new `downscaleGray`) and the blur variance. The similar + blurry detectors both route through it and cache BOTH features, so a photo feeding both is decoded once, not twice (~2x fewer photo decodes). Blur stays 64x64 → `BLUR_VARIANCE_THRESHOLD` unchanged; the dHash derivation changed, so the feature cache is version-bumped (v1) + migrated to DROP old direct-9x8 dHashes (recomputed next scan; blurVar/md5/vHash kept). Removed the now-dead `computeBlurVariance`. Video vHash still uses `computeDHash` on the thumbnail (unchanged).
  - CONCURRENT PRE-PASS ("parallel" scan): the runner is restructured into three phases — (A) cheap metadata/MD5 detectors surface in seconds; (B) ONE concurrent single-decode sweep (`prewarmPhotoFeatures`, bounded concurrency 4 via new `shared.mapWithConcurrency`) warms the photo feature cache with a per-photo progress count; (C) pixel detectors run as pure cache hits and surface together. JS is single-threaded, so the win is single-decode + NATIVE-decode overlap + decode-once-feed-all detectors, NOT N concurrent detector loops. Detector grouping + hard keeper-protection logic UNCHANGED.
  - LOSSLESS RESUME: the feature cache IS the per-asset checkpoint — the pre-pass skips already-cached photos, so a resumed scan finishes only the remaining photos instead of replaying a whole detector. New `flushAllDebouncedStorages()` force-writes every debounced store (feature cache + results) on `AppState background/inactive`, so an OS suspend (iOS) or aggressive OEM kill (Android S24) loses no computed hashes. This fixes the user-reported "stuck / too long" resume.
  - iOS FOREGROUND MODEL: on iOS the scan runs as plain foreground JS (no RNBA; iOS has no general background execution — faking it via audio/location modes risks App Store rejection). The user can roam the app while it scans; backgrounding suspends JS → the scan auto-pauses, and the existing `AppState active → resumeIfInterrupted` + the new flush resume it losslessly on return. Decisions (user): auto-pause+resume (over a 30s background grace); smoothness via bounded concurrency + yielding (no worklet).
  - PROGRESS UX: the Smart Clean screen shows a stage-aware label — "Analyzing photos N of M" during the pre-pass (new `stage`/`analyzed`/`analyzeTotal` store fields) — plus a "You can keep using the app while this runs." hint. Review sheet: removed the obsolete `imagesReady` 60ms flash (the OOM/thumbnail cap made it moot) and added `getItemLayout` for O(1) scroll math.
- Why: the old runner was a strict sequential per-detector loop that decoded every photo TWICE (similar 9x8, blurry 64x64) and checkpointed per-detector — minutes-long on large libraries, categories one-by-one, and a kill restarted a whole detector. User asked for a faster, parallel scan + clarity on the iOS execution model.
- Files: `detectors/{hash-utils (+downscaleGray, +tests), image-pipeline (+computeGrayFeatures, -computeBlurVariance), tier2-similar, tier2-blurry, shared (+mapWithConcurrency), pre-pass.ts (NEW)}`, `feature-cache-store.ts` (version 1 + migrate), `smart-clean-store.ts` (3-phase runner + stage/analyzed fields), `screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx` (progress UX), `components/smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` (drop imagesReady + getItemLayout), `src/utils/debounced-storage.ts` (+flushAllDebouncedStorages), `app/_layout.tsx` (flush on background), all 10 `i18n/locales/*.json` (+`smartClean.analyzingPhotos`/`scanKeepUsing`, parity 442→444).
- Platform scope: all shared JS — no Android native edits, iOS native untouched.
- Deferred: detector thresholds (`DHASH_SIMILAR_MAX`/`DHASH_DUP_MAX`, kept 10/6) may want on-device retuning after the dHash-derivation change — validate on a real library. 7 React-Compiler advisory warnings in `smart-clean-preview-overlay.tsx` (Reanimated) + 1 legitimate re-init effect in the review sheet are pre-existing and left as-is.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors, 58 warnings — down 1 from removing the flash), `npm test` (28 passed, +4 downscaleGray), `npm run i18n:check` (444 keys, parity). NOT device-verified yet: iOS deferred until the Xcode 26 update (the iPhone 13 runs iOS 26.5, which Xcode 16.4 cannot launch); Android verification pending on the S24. Not committed.

### 2026-06-16 - iOS device verification under Xcode 26.5 (previously-deferred 2026-06-15 work now confirmed on iOS)
- What changed: NO code changes — this is a verification + environment milestone. The dev Mac was upgraded to **Xcode 26.5** with an iOS 26.5 simulator runtime, unblocking the iOS build that the 2026-06-15 entries flagged as "deferred until the Xcode 26 update" (Xcode 16.4 could not launch iOS 26.5). Rebuilt the untracked `ios/` project via the documented `__UNSAFE_EXPO_HOME_DIRECTORY=/tmp/expo-home … npx expo run:ios` flow and launched on the **iPhone 17 Pro / iOS 26.5** simulator (udid `2DDFBC0F-F007-4020-B960-B0184E343BF2`).
- Result: app builds, installs, and runs with **no redbox**; RevenueCat Test Store configures correctly (Pro active in dev → 4th tab shows "Smart Clean"). Smoke-tested all four tabs on-device. The cross-platform JS changes from 2026-06-15 that were previously iOS-UNVERIFIED are now confirmed on iOS: **Swipe** (right-swipe advances the card 1/8 → 2/8 — Reanimated + gesture-handler OK); **Compress** (estimate pill "Estimated savings: 6.4 MB", per-item before→after, and the "Compress a custom file" entry point — `expo-image-picker` is linked into the iOS Pods, so the 2026-06-15 custom-file feature that "NEEDS NATIVE REBUILD" now renders on iOS); **Stats** (SVG swipe-distribution pie + 3-category legend, Largest Photos section gone); and most importantly **Smart Clean** — a full single-decode/concurrent pre-pass scan **completed and produced real detector results** on iOS (One-Tap "≈14 MB across 4 items", Similar photos "1 found", Memes "3 found"; Duplicate photos/videos, Blurry, Screenshots "Nothing found"), proving the pixel pipeline + iOS plain-foreground execution model work on-device.
- Build note (carry forward): the FIRST `expo run:ios` after the Xcode upgrade FAILS with `error: Build input file cannot be found: …/ios/build/generated/…/States.cpp (target 'ReactCodegen')` for rnscreens/rnsvg/safeareacontext. This is a known New-Architecture first-build ordering race — the "[CP-User] Generate Specs" script phase generates those files but ReactCodegen tries to compile them first (note the "Script has ambiguous dependencies" warning). Fix: just re-run the same `expo run:ios` command; the generated files are now on disk and the 2nd build succeeds (Pods stay cached → fast). Installed iOS bundle id observed: `com.cognitix.swipeclean`.
- Platform scope: iOS verification only; no source edits (shared JS, Android, and iOS native all untouched). Not committed. Still NOT verified on iOS: destructive deletion, an actual compression run, and video playback/codecs (only discovery/estimate UI was exercised). Detector thresholds still want real-library tuning (sample-library results above are from the 8 simulator stock photos).

### 2026-06-16 - Photo/video permission overhaul: denied + limited ("selected photos") access on iOS & Android
- What changed: fixed four defects in the media-permission flow found in device testing. (1) **Request is now pure** — `PermissionService.requestMediaPermission` no longer calls `openSettings()` as a hidden side effect when `canAskAgain === false`; it always attempts the in-app OS dialog (a no-op when permanently denied) and lets the UI decide when to offer Settings. Added `PermissionService.presentLimitedPicker()` wrapping `MediaLibrary.presentPermissionsPickerAsync(["photo","video"])` (iOS + Android 14+; falls back to `openSettings()` when unavailable) so a limited user can change their selected set in-app. (2) **Instant reload after granting from Settings** — `usePhotoLibrarySync` now ALWAYS attaches the `AppState` "active" listener (previously it early-returned while access wasn't readable, so granting in system Settings never refreshed); the `MediaLibrary.addListener` + 45s poll stay gated to readable status. (3) **Deterministic limited reconcile** — `startFullScan` gained a `restart?: boolean` option that PREEMPTS an in-flight scan (bumps `scanToken`, drops the stale promise) instead of joining it; `reconcileMediaIndex`'s limited branch now passes `{ force:true, restart:true }` so expanding the "selected photos" set always re-reads the CURRENT selection rather than awaiting a scan that captured the old one. (4) **Smart Clean works under limited** — removed the `if (limitedAccess) return;` scan block and changed the delete guard from `status !== "granted"` to "not readable" (`granted` OR `limited`), since the OS allows deleting accessible assets under limited; the old blocking limited card became a non-blocking "Scanning only your selected photos" strip + "Select more photos" button. Permission gate UI on Swipe/Compress now routes to Settings only when `permanentlyDenied` (`denied` + `canAskAgain === false`), else shows the in-app dialog. The existing **Settings → Permissions "Photos & Videos" (Media Access) row** is the SINGLE place that adapts to the live permission state: `limited` → `presentLimitedPicker` + refresh (change the selected set in-app), `granted`/permanently-denied → `openSettings`, askable → the in-app OS dialog; its subtitle already reflects Full/Limited/Not-granted. Under `limited`, the row's trailing checkbox is replaced by a **"Full Access"** button (`permissions.fullAccess`) that opens the app's system settings (so the user can upgrade selected→all), while tapping the row still opens the in-app picker. No separate "Select More Photos" row and nothing extra on the content screens (Swipe/Compress); Smart Clean shows an info-only "scanning your selected photos" note (no picker button).
- Why: on first denial the "Allow access" button jumped straight to Settings; granting from Settings didn't reload; expanding a limited selection still showed only the stale cached subset; and Smart Clean refused to run/delete under limited access. The intended flow is: first open → OS dialog; denied → in-app dialog while the OS still prompts, Settings only after it won't; limited OR full → load whatever is permitted (Swipe + Compress + Smart Clean), and keep it current.
- Files: `src/services/permission-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts` (`restart` preempt), `src/store/app-store.ts` (`reconcileMediaIndex`), `src/hooks/use-photo-library-sync.ts`, `src/screens/{swipe,history,settings,smart-clean}-screen.tsx` (the Settings "Photos & Videos" row gained the limited→`presentLimitedPicker` branch), all 10 `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (+`smartClean.limitedNotice`, `permissions.fullAccess`; parity 444→**446**). The now-orphaned `smartClean.permissionNeeded`/`grantAccess` keys were left in place (harmless; parity unaffected).
- Platform scope: all shared JS/TS — no Android or iOS native edits. Verified the API behavior against the installed `expo-media-library` v18.2 types + Android `MediaLibraryPermissionPromiseWrapper.kt` (both `get`/`requestPermissionsAsync` report `accessPrivileges:"limited"` + `granted:true` under Android 14 partial access).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors, 58 warnings — unchanged baseline), `npm test` (28 passed), `npm run i18n:check` (446 keys, parity). iOS sim (iPhone 17 Pro / 26.5): rebuilt + launched with the new JS — no redbox; the first-open OS permission dialog auto-fires (request path OK). The INTERACTIVE state tests (denied-gate button, limited-selection load, Smart Clean under limited, full-gallery load) are NOT yet device-confirmed: `simctl privacy grant/revoke photos` can't set granted/limited on the iOS 26 runtime (the dialog is authoritative and must be tapped) and the Mac screen locked mid-session, blocking computer-use taps. Pending: drive those flows once the Mac is unlocked (iOS sim + Android S24). Not committed.

- What changed: `handleNotificationAccessPress` in `src/screens/settings-screen.tsx` now opens system Settings (`PermissionService.openSettings`) whenever notifications are NOT in the never-asked state — i.e. `notifStatus` is `granted` (manage) OR `denied`/won't-prompt-again. Only an `undetermined` status still surfaces the in-app OS permission dialog (`ReminderNotificationService.requestPermission`). ### 2026-06-17 - Media grids: FlatList → @shopify/flash-list + downscaled cells (rendering perf only)
- What changed: replaced FlatList with **`@shopify/flash-list` v2.0.2** in the three FULL-SCREEN media grids and routed the one grid that bypassed the downscaled-thumbnail path through it. STRICTLY display-layer — no change to the media-index store, paginated fetching/cursors, navigation, state patterns, or business logic. Grids on FlashList: `src/screens/{review-delete-list,selected-photos,history}-screen.tsx`. The **Smart Clean review sheet stays on FlatList** (`smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx`) — FlashList v2's RecyclerView lays out at 0 size inside that animated, absolutely-positioned bottom sheet (renders blank), and it only shows a single bounded category so FlatList's virtualization + `getItemLayout` is sufficient. It still gained the `cellDp` decode optimization.
  - FLASHLIST v2 (New Arch, already enabled): dropped FlatList-only props (`windowSize`/`maxToRenderPerBatch`/`initialNumToRender`/`removeClippedSubviews`/`getItemLayout`/`columnWrapperStyle`); v2 auto-sizes (no `estimatedItemSize`). Existing in-component pagination (`visibleCount` + `onEndReached`/`loadMore`) and `keyExtractor` kept verbatim. `history-screen`'s `listRef` is now `FlashListRef`. review-delete uses `getItemType={(row) => row.type}` (header vs tile-row recycle pools). Since v2 has no `columnWrapperStyle`, grid gaps are done with a per-cell padding wrapper (`flex:1, padding: gap/2`) + an edge-padding strategy per screen (Compress uses per-column `index % 3` padding to avoid indenting its full-bleed scrolling header; selected-photos/smart-clean use contentContainer/negative-margin); cells fill the column (`width:'100%'` + `aspectRatio`) instead of a fixed px size.
  - COMPRESS GRID FIX (the real bug): `history-screen`'s `MediaImage` fed `CachedImage` the ORIGINAL full-res photo URI (`createThumbnail` returns `asset.uri` for photos) with `cachePolicy="memory-disk"` — decoding full-size bitmaps and retaining them in memory. Now it renders `<MediaThumbnail/>` like every other grid: a small, disk-cached, downscaled copy (react-native-compressor `inSampleSize` downsample-on-decode), `cachePolicy="disk"`, with the ≤4K video-frame OOM guard. This is the main Android memory/jank fix.
  - CELL-SIZED DECODE: added an optional `cellDp` passthrough to `MediaThumbnail` (→ existing `Thumbnail` `cellDp`) and every grid now passes its cell size, so thumbnails decode at the cell resolution instead of the 512 px default.
  - The heavy lifting (downsample-on-decode, disk-only caching, ≤4K video extraction guard, bounded concurrency, lazy/visible generation, asset-id caching, `React.memo` cells) was ALREADY in `ThumbnailService`/`Thumbnail`/`MediaThumbnail` — FlashList's view recycling now bounds resident cells to the visible window on top of it.
- Why: grids lagged on 1000+ items (esp. Android) and large files; the Compress grid additionally decoded full-res photos in cells. User scoped the work to rendering components only.
- Files: `package.json` (+`@shopify/flash-list`), the 4 grid files above, `src/components/media-thumbnail.tsx` (+`cellDp`), `src/components/photo-grid.tsx` (pass `cellDp`). NATIVE DEPENDENCY → requires a native rebuild (iOS `pod install`+build, Android gradle); hot-reload alone will redbox (missing native module).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` (0), `npm run lint` (0 errors, 57 warnings — down 1), `npm test` (28), `npm run i18n:check` (parity). Native rebuilt + launched on iOS sim AND the S24 (FlashList linked; bundle loads, no missing-native-module redbox). Not committed.
- FLASHLIST v2 LAYOUT FIXES (follow-up, after on-device testing): (1) Compress grid cells were uneven (middle column bigger) because per-column padding was asymmetric (edge columns padded `horizontalPadding`, middle `gap/2`); fixed to UNIFORM `padding: gap/2` on every cell + the edge margin moved to `contentContainerStyle.paddingHorizontal` with a compensating negative `marginHorizontal` on the scrolling header so it stays full-bleed. (2) Added `style={{ flex: 1 }}` to the selected-photos and review-delete FlashLists — FlashList v2 needs a bounded height and they lacked one (would render blank in their flex:1 parents). (3) The Smart Clean review sheet first tried FlashList with a computed `height`, but it STILL rendered blank on-device (RecyclerView lays out at 0 inside the animated absolute sheet, no error in logcat) — so it was **reverted to FlatList** (its prior known-good form). Takeaways: a FlashList ALWAYS needs a bounded height (flex:1 in a bounded parent, or explicit height); grid gaps must be uniform per cell (no asymmetric per-column padding); and FlashList v2 is unreliable inside an animated absolutely-positioned bottom sheet — keep such lists on FlatList.
- SMART CLEAN follow-up fixes (same session): (a) the review sheet's FlatList had `removeClippedSubviews` re-added during the revert — it detaches/reattaches off-screen cells on Android so slow scroll "snaps" row-by-row; REMOVED it (and bumped `windowSize` 5→7), matching the documented smooth config. (b) `smart-clean-card.tsx` showed "**Coming soon**" (`smartClean.comingSoon`) for `not_available` detectors — a stale "Stage 2" leftover (detectors are real now). Folded `not_available` into the same presentation as `idle` ("Not scanned" pill, no per-card button — the top "Scan now" drives it), so no card ever reads "Coming soon". The `smartClean.comingSoon` key is now orphaned (left in place; parity unaffected).
- SMART CLEAN REVIEW → FULL SCREEN (supersedes the "keep on FlatList" decision above): on-device the FlatList review sheet still **row-snapped** when scrolling slowly (the `getItemLayout` + `numColumns` combo over-reports content height — `length: rowHeight` summed per item = `columns×` too tall — so the scroll math fights the layout). Rather than keep fighting FlatList-in-a-sheet, the review was rebuilt as a real **screen** so it gets the SAME smooth FlashList setup as the Compress grid (FlashList in a `flex:1` bounded parent — recycling, no `getItemLayout`, no `removeClippedSubviews`, uniform cells). New `src/screens/smart-clean-review-screen.tsx` + route `app/smart-clean-review.tsx` (Stack `presentation: "card"`); **deleted** `smart-clean-review-sheet.tsx` and removed `<SmartCleanReviewSheet/>` from `app/_layout.tsx`. It still reads the target from `useSmartCleanReviewStore` (Smart Clean's `startReview` now `openReview(...)` + `router.push("/smart-clean-review")`); dismissal is centralized through the store's `close()` → a `visible` effect pops the screen (covers cancel, hardware back, and the post-delete close from `handleConfirmDelete`), plus an unmount cleanup resets the store on OS-back/swipe. Keeper protection, selection, pagination (60/page), the delete-confirm dialog, and long-press → the still-root-mounted `SmartCleanPreviewOverlay` are all preserved. Navigation IS touched here (a new route) — that was explicitly requested for this fix; the earlier "display-layer only" constraint was relaxed by the user for it. NOTE: a new route file may need a full dev reload (not just Fast Refresh) to register.

### 2026-06-18 - Compression result: dedicated full-screen comparison + safer result layout
- What changed: removed the inline Compressed/Original selector from the completed `compress-run` result screen. The result now shows one edge-to-edge (`cover`) compressed-media preview with an expand affordance; tapping it pushes the existing `/compression-media-viewer` route in a new `compare=1` mode. The full-screen comparison keeps the Compressed/Original selector, supports pinch zoom + panning for both photos and texture-backed video, keeps video play/pause, and swipes downward to return. Switching comparison sides resets zoom so the newly selected source starts framed correctly.
- Result layout: the completed state now uses a vertical `ScrollView` with explicit top/bottom content padding and safe-area padding, preventing the decision controls and irreversible-delete warning from colliding with system navigation on shorter screens or longer translations.
- Delete-denial UX: a denied/failed original deletion no longer prints the raw native `ExpoMediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync` error inside the result screen. Android shows the translated, friendly `compressRun.deleteFailed` message as a long toast; iOS uses a simple alert fallback. The user remains on the decision screen and the original remains untouched.
- Custom-file compatibility: comparison mode resolves picker-backed originals through `custom-compress.store` when the media is not in the persisted library index. Custom files remain Keep-only on the result screen.
- Video tap follow-up: the completed result card wraps its native `VideoView` preview in a `pointerEvents="none"` container. The video remains visible but display-only on the summary screen, preventing the native texture surface from consuming taps so the parent card reliably opens the same full-screen comparison flow as photos.

### 2026-06-18 - Delete-original PIN prompt opens immediately
- Problem: tapping **Delete Original** in the compression result awaited `AppLockService.hasPasscode()` on the interaction path. That call dynamically loaded SecureStore and read encrypted storage before changing modal state, producing a noticeable delay and sometimes making the tap appear unresponsive.
- What changed: `AppLockService` now caches passcode presence after `hasPasscode`, successful `setPasscode`, `verifyPasscode`, and successful `clearPasscode`. `compress-run-screen` also preloads passcode presence as soon as the screen mounts, while compression is still running. The Delete Original handler is now synchronous: App Lock enabled + PIN present opens the verification modal immediately; App Lock disabled or confirmed no-PIN continues directly to the OS deletion-consent flow.
- Safety: App Lock remains the master gate, so a stale Keychain/Keystore PIN cannot prompt while App Lock is disabled. While the initial preload is unresolved, an enabled App Lock uses its setup invariant (enabled implies a configured PIN) and opens verification rather than bypassing it.

### 2026-06-18 - Smart Clean automatically rescans after media-permission changes
- What changed: the global photo-library foreground sync now detects real permission transitions and changes to the selected-media set under limited access. It first lets the existing media-index reconciliation finish, cancels any Smart Clean run that captured the old asset snapshot, prunes inaccessible feature-cache entries, and starts a fresh non-resume Smart Clean scan when prior Smart Clean results exist and Pro is active. A limited grant scans only the currently authorized photos/videos. An upgrade to full access explicitly awaits a forced full-gallery media-index scan (not just the normal newest-page refresh) before Smart Clean begins. Losing readable access resets Smart Clean so stale recommendations for inaccessible assets are never shown.
- Change detection: the hook keeps its own last-reconciled permission ref so Settings' lightweight status refresh cannot hide an old→new transition; under limited access it also compares the media index's identity-stable `orderedIds` reference, which changes only when the authorized asset set actually changes. The 45-second no-op poll therefore does not repeatedly launch Smart Clean.
- Files: `src/hooks/use-photo-library-sync.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependencies, native configuration, routes, persisted schemas, or translations changed.

During that work it becomes a disabled busy action with a spinner and live phase copy: **Checking gallery…**, **Indexing gallery · N items** for full access, or **Updating selected media · N items** for limited access. When indexing completes, the existing Smart Clean detector progress panel takes over automatically.

The action cannot be tapped twice while indexing, exposes busy/disabled accessibility state, and allows longer translations to shrink/wrap safely within the existing button. Added equivalent copy to all 10 supported locales.

### 2026-06-18 - Smart Clean full-to-selected permission downgrade safety
- Problem: overlapping Settings, AppState, and media-library events could arrive while the global permission reconcile was already running; the later event was dropped. A full-access → selected-media downgrade could therefore leave the previous Smart Clean result groups available, and cancelling the detector run did not clear those old snapshots.
- Fix: photo-library sync is now a module-level queued pipeline. Concurrent triggers coalesce and always receive one trailing reconcile pass, so a permission value that settles late is not missed. The pipeline reads the live OS grant before rebuilding the index; when access changes it immediately closes any Smart Clean review/preview and resets all old detector results. After the media index has pruned itself to the currently authorized IDs, the feature cache is pruned and Smart Clean starts a fresh scan only over that reconciled set. The Settings limited-media picker uses the same pipeline and explicitly forces a fresh Smart Clean scan after dismissal.
- Defense in depth: while the media index is scanning or its stored access level does not match the current permission, Smart Clean derives no recommendations from old results and category review actions resolve to the non-actionable not-scanned state. The final delete-confirmation path also requires a settled permission-matching index and rejects the whole action if any requested ID is absent from the currently authorized set. Thus revoked images/videos cannot be reopened or deleted from a stale review while reconciliation is in progress, even before the new detector scan begins.
- Files: `src/hooks/use-photo-library-sync.ts`, `src/screens/settings-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, and `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependencies, native configuration, routes, persisted schemas, or translations changed.
- Verification: intentionally left for user device testing per request; no tests or screenshots were run.

### 2026-06-29 - All Media gallery: month sections, date scrubber, scroll-in-select, paint-deselect, hide-marked
- What changed: the dense "All Media" gallery (`selected-photos-screen.tsx`) was reworked from a uniform `FlashList numColumns` grid into a **month-sectioned** list (a text header per month — localized "June 2026" + "N items · X" subtitle — followed by that month's tiles), and several selection/scrubber behaviours were fixed.
- Sectioned layout: photos are flattened into header/photo rows with absolute tops by new PURE, unit-tested helpers in `src/utils/gallery-grid.ts` — `buildGalleryLayout()` (rows + `monthOffsets` + `contentHeight`), `hitTestSectionedIndex()` (binary-search a touch → photo index, variable-height aware), and `photoIndexAtOffset()` (scroll offset → photo, for the scrubber label). The FlashList renders the flattened rows with `getItemType` (`header`/`photos`); each photo row is a fixed-height (`listW/numColumns`) flex row of up to `numColumns` square tiles. The old uniform-grid math (`hitTestGridIndex`, `buildMonthSpans` for the screen) is superseded (those exports remain, still unit-tested).
- Scrubber (`gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`): the right-edge fast-scroll thumb's month bubble was **broken** (it never appeared while dragging) — two root causes fixed: (1) the pan lost the touch to the list's native scroll, fixed by `onScrubbingChange` disabling the list's scroll on touch-down (the scrubber is a sibling overlay above the list, so its pan receives the touch directly). NOTE: an earlier `Gesture.Native()` (`listGesture`) + `pan.blocksExternalGesture(listGesture)` attempt was ABANDONED — it broke native scrolling; see the later 2026-06-29 scroll-regression entry. (2) the bubble's text was squished to ~0 width because it was anchored inside the 30 px track — the scrubber root is now full-width (`box-none`) so the bubble extends left of the track. The bubble now shows the **date of the item at the top of the viewport**: full date "Dec 25, 2025" (`formatDate`) for the multi-month "All Media" scope, or weekday+day "Mon 24" (new `formatWeekdayDay` in `utils/date.ts`) when a single month is in scope. The scrubber now shows whenever the list scrolls (`maxScroll > 1`), not only when >1 month.
- Selection gestures redesigned: a single wrapper `Gesture.Exclusive(pan, tap)` over the list replaces the old select-mode full-screen overlay. A quick **tap** toggles a tile (select mode) or opens the preview (normal mode); a **long-press-drag** paints selection (`pan.activateAfterLongPress(180)` — no `blocksExternalGesture`), entering select mode if needed and edge-auto-scrolling. Because the pan only activates after a long press, **the list scrolls normally in select mode too** (flick = scroll, press-drag = select); a `painting` state flag turns off `scrollEnabled` only for the duration of a paint stroke. Each drag stroke snapshots the current selection and picks its direction from the anchor tile's state, so **re-dragging over already-selected tiles now deselects them**. `GalleryTile` is now purely presentational (no Pressable).
- Crash fix: entering select mode previously crashed with `Cannot read property 'forEach' of null` in RN's `_validateTransforms` — the tile's `transform` toggled between an array and `undefined`, and RN's prop diff resets that to `null`. Now `transform: [{ scale: isSelected ? 0.84 : 1 }]` is always a valid array. (This drag-select path had never been device-verified before.)
- Marked items: the gallery now **excludes anything already queued for deletion** (`selectedPhotos` filters out `markedIds`), so marking items removes them from the grid and the header/section counts reflect only un-marked media.
- Files: `src/screens/selected-photos-screen.tsx`, `src/components/gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`, `src/utils/gallery-grid.ts` (+`.test.ts`, now 23 cases), `src/utils/date.ts`, and `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependencies, native config, routes, persisted schemas, or translations changed.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck`, `npm test` (79 pass incl. new geometry cases), `npm run i18n:check`, and `npm run lint` (0 errors) all green. Device-verified on a connected Android (Galaxy S24, dev client over Metro): month section headers render, the scrubber bubble shows the date ("Jul 12, 2025") and the per-month grouping works, normal scrolling works, and the select-mode transform crash is gone. The reworked select-mode gesture (scroll-while-selecting, long-press-paint, paint-deselect) and the single-month "Mon 24" label are implemented + gate-green but the final gesture feel was not re-exercised on device (per user request to stop device testing); they need an on-device pass.

### 2026-06-29 - Bottom-sheet nested scroll + gallery scroll-regression fix + Compress filter as native sheet
- Gallery scroll regression: the prior entry's select-mode gesture used `pan.blocksExternalGesture(listGesture)` and composed a `Gesture.Native()` (`listGesture`) with the paint/tap gestures. Both BROKE the FlashList's native scroll entirely (no scroll in or out of select mode): `blocksExternalGesture` makes the native scroll wait for the pan to fail, and the pan (activateAfterLongPress) never fails cleanly; and FlashList v2's scroll isn't represented by `Gesture.Native()`, so any composition starved it. Fix: drop `listGesture`/`blocksExternalGesture` entirely; wrap the list in just `Gesture.Exclusive(pan.activateAfterLongPress(180), tap)`. The native scroll runs underneath and works because the pan only activates after a long press; a `painting` state flag then sets `scrollEnabled={false}` for the paint stroke. Device-verified scroll works (onScroll fires). The scrubber now drives scroll solely via `onScrubbingChange`→`scrollEnabled=false` (no `listGesture` to block).
- Month-selector sheet (Swipe) nested scroll: scrolling the month list up dragged the native form sheet closed mid-list instead of scrolling. Root cause: RN `ScrollView`'s `nestedScrollEnabled` defaults to FALSE on Android, so the form sheet's Material `BottomSheetBehavior.findScrollingChild()` didn't recognize the list and handled every downward drag as a dismiss. Fix: `nestedScrollEnabled` on the `ScrollView` in `month-selector-bottom-sheet.tsx`. (Device-verified: list now scrolls up; sheet dismisses only at the top.)
- Compress filter → native form sheet: the Compress screen's filter was a custom slide-up `Modal` (`compression-filter-dialog.tsx`, now DELETED) with no drag-to-dismiss. Converted it to a native `formSheet` route (`/compress-filter`) mirroring the month-selector exactly (draggable, dimmed, detents `[0.58,0.92]`, `nestedScrollEnabled` list) so both bottom sheets behave identically. New: `app/compress-filter.tsx`, `src/components/compress-filter-sheet.tsx`, the non-persisted `src/features/compression/compress-filter.store.ts` (monthKey + mediaType; changing type resets month), and the shared `src/features/compression/use-compressible-pool.ts` hook (returns `{ pool, doneSourceIds }`) so the grid and the sheet derive the compressible month list from one source. `history-screen.tsx` now reads the filter from the store, opens the sheet via `router.push("/compress-filter")`, and uses the shared pool hook (its local `filter`/`filterVisible` state, `doneSourceIds`/`compressiblePool`/`months` memos, and the dialog were removed).
- Files: `src/screens/selected-photos-screen.tsx`, `src/components/gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`, `src/components/month-selector-bottom-sheet.tsx`, `src/screens/history-screen.tsx`, `app/_layout.tsx`, `app/compress-filter.tsx` (new), `src/components/compress-filter-sheet.tsx` (new), `src/features/compression/{compress-filter.store.ts,use-compressible-pool.ts}` (new), `src/components/compression-filter-dialog.tsx` (DELETED), `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependency, persisted-schema, or translation changes (the Compress sheet reuses existing keys).
- Verification: typecheck / 79 tests / i18n parity / lint (0 errors) all green. Device-verified: the month-selector list scrolls up without dismissing, and general gallery scrolling works. The Compress filter sheet conversion and the gallery select-mode gesture feel are gate-green but pending the user's own on-device pass (per request).

### 2026-06-29 - Smart Clean scan: informative foreground notification (Android) + background-execution decision
- Decision (after deep research, documented here so it isn't re-litigated): for the Smart Clean scan we KEEP the `react-native-background-actions` foreground-service model (it keeps the JS scan thread alive on Android + shows an ongoing notification). Migrating to Android **WorkManager would be a downgrade** for our scan — a Worker runs native Kotlin, is short-lived (~10 min), can't host a continuous JS runtime, and a long-running worker just calls `setForeground()` (a foreground service) under the hood anyway; it would force rewriting all 8 detectors + perceptual hashing in Kotlin for no user-visible gain. `expo-background-task`/`expo-task-manager` are also unsuitable (deferred, ≥15-min, system-scheduled, no live UI). Because the FGS keeps the SAME JS thread alive, in-app progress updates flow through the existing Zustand store — **no native→JS bridge needed**.
- What changed (Android, shared JS/TS only — manifest already declared `dataSync` + `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC` + `POST_NOTIFICATIONS`, no native edits): the ongoing scan notification + the in-app progress label now show the same human-readable "what it's doing" line via one shared helper `scanStatusText({ stage, activeDetectorKey, analyzed, analyzeTotal })` in `smart-clean.service.ts` — "Analyzing photos 1,240 of 3,355" during the photo pre-pass, "Scanning {category}" (localized card title) while a detector runs, else "Scanning…". The notifier (`updateScanNotification`/`throttledNotify` in `smart-clean-store.ts`) and `SmartCleanScanNotifications.showProgress(body)` now take that text instead of a raw `current/total`. New i18n key `smartClean.scanningCategory` added to all 10 locales (529 keys); `smartClean.scanningProgress` is now an unused orphan key (left in place, harmless). The screen drops its `activeIndex`-based "X of Y" counter for the shared label; the determinate progress bar is unchanged.
- iOS / Phase 2 (lose-nothing checkpoint on background): NO new work — already implemented. `app/_layout.tsx` flushes `flushAllDebouncedStorages()` (scan-results store + feature cache, both `createDebouncedStorage(800)`) on AppState `background`/`inactive`, and `resumeIfInterrupted()` on `active`. iOS stays foreground-only by design (the scan pauses when backgrounded and resumes from the flushed checkpoint). True iOS background scanning would need iOS-26 `BGContinuedProcessingTask` via a custom native module — explicitly deferred.
- Files: `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean.service.ts` (+`scanStatusText`), `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts`, `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-notifications.ts`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, all 10 `src/i18n/locales/*.json`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No deps, native config, routes, or persisted-schema changes.
- Verification: typecheck / 79 tests / i18n parity (529) / lint (0 errors) green. On-device verification left to the user (per [[user-runs-device-testing]]). Not done (out of scope per decision): WorkManager migration, native Kotlin detectors, Notifee Stop-button on the service-path notification, iOS BGContinuedProcessingTask.
