### 2026-07-03 - Gallery recycling, image-loading, and scrubber production fixes
- Problem: the month-sectioned gallery could visually repeat previously rendered images near the bottom and the right-edge scrubber bubble could appear briefly then stop. Root causes were recycled image components rendering a previous URI for one commit while a new thumbnail resolved, photo rows reading assets indirectly by external index, FlashList's default visible-position retention on a non-chat gallery, and the scrubber's pan gesture being recreated during active scrub/bubble state updates.
- Fix: `Thumbnail` now gates its displayed URI by a request key (`cacheKey`, `sourceUri`, cell size, media type), and `CachedImage` remounts the native image per URI while keeping loading state tied to the active URI. `buildGalleryLayout()` now stores `itemIds` on every photo row; `selected-photos-screen.tsx` renders rows through a `photoById` map, includes layout rows in `extraData`, disables FlashList `maintainVisibleContentPosition`, and mounts the scrubber only after the list height is known. `GalleryMonthScrubber` now keeps a stable pan gesture across bubble/parent re-renders and bridges current callbacks through refs, so active scrubs are not cancelled by state updates.
- Files: `src/components/thumbnail.tsx`, `src/components/cached-image.tsx`, `src/components/gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`, `src/screens/selected-photos-screen.tsx`, `src/utils/gallery-grid.ts`, `src/utils/gallery-grid.test.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependencies, native config, routes, translations, persisted schemas, or business rules changed.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (91 tests, gallery rows now assert `itemIds`); `npm run i18n:check` green (545 keys); `npm run lint` green with 0 errors and 88 advisory warnings. No device/emulator runtime pass was run in this change; native converter/compression/Smart Clean execution still needs device QA before a production release claim.

### 2026-07-05 - Security + cold-launch-lag audit pass (multi-agent) and fixes
- Context: an 8-dimension multi-agent audit (4 security, 3 performance/iOS-cold-launch, 1 broken-parts/gates) with adversarial verification, prompted by a reported big iOS cold-launch lag. Confirmed on the S24 dev client that the persisted `swipeclean-media-index-store` row is ~1.5 MB (device library = 3,339 assets), so the full AsyncStorage DB is ~2.3 MB and is `JSON.parse`d on the JS thread every cold launch — approaching Android's 2 MB CursorWindow per-row cliff on bigger libraries.
- Fixes applied (all JS/config; no native code changed, so they Fast-Refresh into the running dev client and need no rebuild):
  - **iOS iCloud stall (biggest launch win):** `photo-library-service.ts` `mapAsset` now calls `getAssetInfoAsync(asset, { shouldDownloadFromNetwork: false })`. The default (`true`) makes iOS pull each not-locally-cached asset down from iCloud — done per asset on every page fetch AND across the whole library during a full scan. Local size/creationTime still resolve (verified on device: "6.3 MB" still shows).
  - **Redundant prefetch on idle:** `media-index-store.ts` `refreshNewestPage` only calls `ImageCacheService.prefetchPhotos` when the newest page actually changed (`mergeUnchanged` flag), so the 45 s poll / foreground reconcile no longer re-prefetches 36 full-res assets when nothing changed.
  - **Ads off the critical path + UMP compliance:** `app/_layout.tsx` wraps the consent-gather + `mobileAds().initialize()` + interstitial/rewarded preloads in `InteractionManager.runAfterInteractions(...)` (cancels on unmount) and now honors `AdsConsentService.gather()`'s `canRequestAds` — preloads are skipped when consent is required-but-not-granted (previously the result was ignored and ads always preloaded).
  - **Per-swipe full-store write:** `app-store.ts` (`swipeclean-free-store`) switched from plain `AsyncStorage` to `createJSONStorage(() => createDebouncedStorage(400))` (the shared debounced storage already used by feature-cache/compression). zustand-persist wrote the whole partialized blob (settings, stats, history, marked queue, ever-growing `reviewedPhotoIds`) on every `set()`; the debounce collapses bursts. `flushAllDebouncedStorages()` (already wired on app background in `_layout`) keeps the marked-deletion queue durable before suspend.
  - **Deletion write-back race:** `permanentlyDeleteMarked` now uses the functional `set((current) => …)` form so state written after the (seconds-long) OS delete-consent dialog reads fresh values instead of a pre-await snapshot that could clobber a concurrent swipe/restore/refresh.
  - **Android hardening:** `android:allowBackup="false"` (set in `app.json` `android.allowBackup` AND the generated `AndroidManifest.xml`) so ADB/cloud backup no longer exfiltrates the plaintext AsyncStorage (media index, deletion history, App-Lock flag, Pro status); `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` capped with `android:maxSdkVersion="32"` in the manifest.
  - **Repo hygiene:** `.gitignore` now ignores the ~791 MB local `outputs/` scratch tree and the dev-server `qr-*.svg` files (they encode this machine's Metro LAN/tunnel URL); the two tracked QR SVGs were `git rm --cached`'d (kept on disk). `eslint.config.js` ignores `outputs/**` — those scratch scripts were the source of the only 2 lint errors (`'Buffer' is not defined`), so the lint gate is green again.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (91 tests); `npm run i18n:check` green (545 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 88 advisory warnings — matches the prior baseline). App relaunched + reloaded on the S24 dev client (Metro bundle re-transformed, HTTP 200, no red box); Swipe screen renders a full-res photo on a 3,339-asset library.
- Documented-but-NOT-fixed (need a native rebuild and/or are larger changes; left as recommendations): no `expo-splash-screen` gating (blank window during iOS JS startup); Android MP3/WAV convert buffers the whole PCM stream (OOM on long videos — already in §10); iOS WAV/`SCLameEncoder` unbounded/`CMBlockBufferGetDataPointer` buffering; `lame_jni.c`/encoder loops ignore `fwrite` failures (truncated-on-disk-full); WebM `MediaMuxer` leak on early failure; App-Lock 4-digit PIN has no attempt lockout and the gate starts unlocked (content can flash during hydration); `lucide-react-native` barrel import; iOS AdMob App ID is still Google's sample id; Share button shares the URI as text on Android. See the audit findings list in the scratchpad for the full set.
- Files: `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/store/app-store.ts`, `app/_layout.tsx`, `app.json`, `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`, `.gitignore`, `eslint.config.js`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No dependency, i18n, persisted-schema, or business-rule changes.

### 2026-07-05 - Convert: audio opens in an external OS player; MP3 target removed
- Two product changes to the Convert feature:
  - **Converted audio opens in an external player instead of a Share sheet.** The audio result action (single run + batch row tap) now hands the file to the OS: on Android it launches the system "Open with…" chooser via `expo-intent-launcher` (`ACTION_VIEW` on a `expo-file-system` FileProvider `content://` uri with `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`); iOS has no such chooser so it falls back to the share sheet, as does any build where the native intent module isn't compiled in yet. Logic centralized in the new `src/features/convert/open-media-file.ts` (capability-probed by native-module NAME `ExpoIntentLauncher` via `requireOptionalNativeModule`, then dynamic-`import`, same red-box-safe pattern as `app-lock-service`). The result button changed from "Share"/`Share2` to "Open"/`ExternalLink`; new i18n key `convert.open` added to all 10 locales (546 keys).
  - **MP3 removed as a convert target for now** (product decision): `"mp3"` dropped from `AUDIO_TARGETS` in `convert-targets.ts` so the chip is never offered. The `ConvertTarget` type, the output-kind/mime/label maps, `isTargetAvailable("mp3")`, the LAME `audio-engine` mp3 path, and the `audioMp3` capability all remain — re-add `"mp3"` to `AUDIO_TARGETS` to restore it. `convert-targets.test.ts` expectations updated.
- **New dependency:** `expo-intent-launcher ~13.0.8` (a NATIVE module) → requires a native rebuild/reinstall to activate. Until then the audio "Open" action degrades to the share sheet (probe returns false, no crash). Rebuilt `:app:assembleDebug` and reinstalled on the S24 this session (the rebuild also bakes in the 2026-07-05 `allowBackup="false"` + `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE maxSdkVersion` manifest hardening). The manifest needed `tools:replace="android:allowBackup"` because a transitive dep (`TAndroidLame`) declares `allowBackup="true"`.
- **On-device fix (audio uri must be FileProvider-serveable):** first device run of "Open" fell back to the share sheet. Root cause: `convert.service.ts` set the audio result `outputUri` to the media-store copy returned by `saveAudioToLibrary` (a public `/storage/emulated/0/Music/...` path), but the OS "Open with…" flow needs `expo-file-system.getContentUriAsync`, whose FileProvider only serves the app's own dirs (`file_system_provider_paths.xml` = `files-path "."` + `cache-path "."`). A public path throws `Failed to find configured root`, so the opener caught it and shared. Fix: keep the **sandbox** (`documentDirectory`) uri as `outputUri` (createAssetAsync copies rather than moves, so the file stays in `documentDirectory` AND the media-store copy still exists via `libraryAssetId`). `getContentUriAsync` then resolves to `content://com.cognitix.swipeclean.FileSystem.fileprovider/expo_files/…` and the audio-player chooser opens.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (91 tests, convert-targets updated); `npm run i18n:check` green (546 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 88 warnings). **On device (S24, rebuilt+installed) — both VERIFIED:** (1) MP3 chip is gone from the format sheet (AUDIO shows only M4A + WAV). (2) Tapping a freshly-converted WAV launched the Android **"Open with"** chooser (`android/com.android.internal.app.ResolverActivity` in logcat) listing real audio players (Spotify, YouTube Music, Files by Google, WPS Office) with Just once / Always — i.e. the OS ACTION_VIEW open flow, not a share sheet. (Older jobs converted before the fix still hold the public uri and fall back to share — expected; only post-fix conversions get the sandbox uri.)
- Files: `src/features/convert/open-media-file.ts` (new), `src/features/convert/convert-targets.ts`, `src/features/convert/convert-targets.test.ts`, `src/features/convert/convert.types.ts`, `src/features/convert/convert.service.ts`, `src/screens/convert-run-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/convert-batch-run-screen.tsx`, `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (10), `package.json`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`.

### 2026-07-06 - Bug sweep: Smart Clean results wiped on rescan/refresh; Convert output filename extension; M4A open-with
- Three user-reported bugs fixed (all JS, Fast-Refresh into the running dev client):
  - **Smart Clean lost all results when a rescan started or was stopped.** `runScan` blanked `resultsByKey` to the (empty for non-resume) `seeded` set at scan start, so tapping "Scan again" instantly cleared the whole recommendation set and a manual Stop then left nothing (no "Review all / N items"). Fix (`smart-clean-store.ts`): seed both the visible `resultsByKey` and the working `acc` from the PREVIOUS results (`previousResults`) instead of the empty skip-set; each detector overwrites its own entry as it recomputes, so at worst a Stop leaves a mix of prior + freshly-recomputed results, never an empty set. `seeded` is still the resume skip-set (unchanged).
  - **Smart Clean cards flashed to "Not scanned" during a routine background gallery check.** `smart-clean-screen.tsx` gated the detector cards + One-Tap Recommendations on `mediaScopeReconciling`, which folded in plain `indexing` (`mediaIndexStatus === "scanning" | "refreshing"`) — so every 45s newest-page refresh / background reindex blanked valid results to placeholders. Renamed the results gate to `resultsUntrustworthy = !canReadMedia || mediaIndexAccessLevel !== expectedIndexAccess` (drops `indexing`): results are only hidden when media truly can't be read or the index is reconciling to a DIFFERENT access level (full↔limited, which can leave groups pointing at inaccessible assets). The scan button still uses `indexing`/`scanning` directly, so it keeps showing "Checking gallery…". Results now stay visible during a background check.
  - **Converted output showed the SOURCE extension (e.g. a WebM labeled `1000011248.mp4`).** The on-disk artifact was always correct (verified in the MediaStore: `convert-<ts>.webm`, `video/webm`), but the Convert UI rendered `job.fileName` (the source name). Added pure `convertedFileName(sourceName, target)` (`convert-targets.ts`, unit-tested) = source basename + target extension (`1000011248.mp4` → `1000011248.webm`); used it in the Recent-converted list (`convert-screen.tsx`) and the batch rows (`convert-batch-run-screen.tsx`).
- **M4A "Open with" confirmed working** (user had reported it failing): it was the same pre-fix public-uri fallback as WAV — a freshly-converted M4A opens the Android "Open with" chooser (audio players) correctly; only jobs converted before the 2026-07-05 sandbox-uri fix fall back to share.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (94 tests, +3 for `convertedFileName`); `npm run i18n:check` green (546 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 88 warnings). On-device: Convert Recent list now shows correct extensions (`.webm`/`.wav`/`.m4a`/`.webp`/`.png`), and a fresh M4A opened the OS audio-player chooser (`ResolverActivity`). Smart Clean fixes were code-verified (device testing owned by the user this session).
- Files: `src/features/smart-clean/smart-clean-store.ts`, `src/screens/smart-clean-screen.tsx`, `src/features/convert/convert-targets.ts`, `src/features/convert/convert-targets.test.ts`, `src/screens/convert-screen.tsx`, `src/screens/convert-batch-run-screen.tsx`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No deps, native config, persisted-schema, or business-rule changes.

### 2026-07-06 - All Media gallery: duplicate month headers ("June June"), scrubber date bubble
- Two user-reported gallery bugs:
  - **Duplicate / non-contiguous month sections + out-of-order media (root cause: an unsorted persisted index).** `buildGalleryLayout` groups by CONTIGUOUS `monthKey` runs (the index is meant to be creationTime-desc). But the media index is persisted, and its `orderedIds` hydrates AS-SAVED; a list saved by an older build (or mid-scan) can be out of order, and `startFullScan`'s `canSkip` may never re-scan to fix it — so the gallery rendered the same month twice ("June 2026 … June 2026"), Swipe showed cards out of chronological order, and old media landed at wrong positions. Fix (`media-index-store.ts`): new `normalizeOrderedIds(assetsById, orderedIds)` runs in the persist `merge` (once per launch) — dedupes, drops ids with no asset, folds in any asset missing from the list, and re-sorts newest-first (the same invariant `mergeIndexedAssets` maintains). Benefits every screen, not just the gallery. Note: it only reorders EXISTING entries — if the persisted count looks low (index incomplete), a fresh full scan is still needed to add missing assets.
  - **Scrubber date bubble disappeared mid-scroll.** `GalleryMonthScrubber` set the bubble to `labelForOffset(offset)`, which returns `""` when the top-of-viewport item briefly has no resolvable date (missing `creationTime`, or an index/layout gap while scrolling) — an empty string is falsy, so `{bubble ? … : null}` hid the whole bubble. Fix (`gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`): only update the bubble on a NON-empty label, keeping the last real date instead of flickering to nothing.
- The recycling / stale-image / itemIds work from 2026-07-03 was already in place (verified sound); the remaining gallery symptoms trace to the unsorted-index root cause above.
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (94 tests); `npm run i18n:check` green (546 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 88 warnings). Device testing owned by the user this session.
- Files: `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `src/components/gallery-month-scrubber.tsx`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No deps, native config, i18n, persisted-schema (shape unchanged — only the id ORDER is normalized), or business-rule changes.

### 2026-07-06 - Gallery: undated assets bucketed correctly (fixes "unknown date" section + duplicate current-month at bottom)
- Deeper root cause behind the gallery date bugs: an asset's `monthKey` and its sort key could DISAGREE. `getMonthKey(falsy)` returns the CURRENT month, but the index sorts a missing `creationTime` to the bottom (`creationTime ?? 0` → 0). So an asset with no creation date got a bogus "current-month" (`2026-07`) monthKey yet sorted last — producing a SECOND "July 2026" section at the very bottom full of "unknown date" items, and (after the hydration re-sort surfaced them) recent photos whose creation metadata was missing landed there instead of at the top.
- Fix — resolve the date + month TOGETHER so they can never disagree (`src/utils/date.ts` `resolveMediaDate(creationTime, modificationTime)`): prefer `creationTime`; fall back to `modificationTime` (recovers assets whose creation time was lost — recent photos rejoin their real month at the top); else it's genuinely undated → new `UNKNOWN_MONTH_KEY` sentinel, which sorts to the end and renders as "Unknown date" (`monthLabel` maps it to `common.unknownDate`; also added a NaN guard). Applied in `mapAsset` (both branches — no more `Date.now()` fallback that faked a current-month date) and, for already-persisted bad data, in a new `repairIndexedAssets` run in the media-index hydration `merge` (recomputes each asset's `creationTime`+`monthKey` once per launch, so a stale index self-heals without waiting for a re-scan).
- Net: months are contiguous and correctly labeled; undated media collects in one "Unknown date" section at the bottom instead of a duplicate current month; recent photos with recoverable modification dates sort into their real month. The scrubber keeps its last real date when passing undated items (from the earlier fix).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (94 tests); `npm run i18n:check` green (546 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 88 warnings). `resolveMediaDate` is not unit-tested because `date.ts` imports `@/i18n` (native/expo — out of vitest scope per `vitest.config.ts`). Device testing owned by the user.
- Files: `src/utils/date.ts`, `src/services/photo-library-service.ts`, `src/store/media-index-store.ts`, `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. `creationTime` was already optional in the model, so producing `undefined` for truly-undated assets is not a schema change.

### 2026-07-06 - Stats screen redesign + Convert stats + count-up animations
- User ask: add Convert statistics, make the Stats screen more appealing/animated, cleaner and friendlier with more at-a-glance detail.
- **Durable Convert stats (new data):** `convert.store.ts` gained a persisted `lifetime` tally (`ConvertLifetimeStats`: total + per-output-kind image/video/audio counts + input/output bytes), incremented once per completed conversion in `markCompleted` (guarded against double-count) and included in `partialize`. Because it lives outside the transient `jobs` map, it SURVIVES clearing the "Recent converted" list. A one-time `onRehydrateStorage` backfill seeds it from existing completed jobs so current users don't see 0 (`EMPTY_CONVERT_LIFETIME` sentinel; runs only while total is 0).
- **New reusable animated components:** `src/components/animated-stat-value.tsx` (a number/bytes value that counts up from its previous value via a JS-driven `Animated.Value` + a formatter ref so inline formatters don't re-trigger the run) and `src/components/animated-bar.tsx` (a reanimated proportion bar that fills from 0, staggerable via `delay`).
- **`convert-stats-section.tsx` (new):** lifetime total + media processed (animated), and an images/videos/audio breakdown with animated proportion bars; friendly empty state when nothing has been converted. Everyone sees it (Free users just see the empty state).
- **Stats screen redesign (`stats-screen.tsx`):** a gradient **hero** (`expo-linear-gradient`, accent→`shade()`-darkened accent) with the big animated "Space reclaimed" figure + items-reviewed caption; a 2×2 grid of **icon stat tiles** (library size, scanned, cleared, marked) with count-up values; the existing swipe-distribution pie; the new Convert section; and Advanced Stats (Pro) / locked card. Every block enters with a staggered reanimated `FadeInDown`. `StatsCard` (old plain card) is still used by the Advanced Stats section.
- **i18n:** +9 keys under `stats` (`reclaimed`, `itemsReviewed` with `{{count}}`, and a `convert` sub-object: `title/empty/total/processed/images/videos/audio`) across all 10 locales (555 keys).
- Verification: `npm run typecheck` green; `npm test` green (94 tests); `npm run i18n:check` green (555 keys); `npm run lint` green (0 errors, 93 warnings — +5 advisory react-compiler warnings on the new animated components, same class as the accepted baseline). Bundle compiles (HTTP 200). On-device screenshot pending (S24 dropped off USB); the redesign Fast-Refreshes in.
- Files: `src/features/convert/convert.store.ts`, `src/features/convert/components/convert-stats-section.tsx` (new), `src/components/animated-stat-value.tsx` (new), `src/components/animated-bar.tsx` (new), `src/screens/stats-screen.tsx`, `src/i18n/locales/*.json` (10), `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`. No new deps (reanimated + expo-linear-gradient already present); `lifetime` is an additive persisted field (missing → seeded/empty, no migration).

## 12. AI Agent Rules
- Always read `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md` before making changes.
- After every implemented feature, update `PROJECT_CONTEXT.md`.
- When updating the context file, add a new entry under `Feature History`.
- Do not remove previous history unless it is clearly wrong.
- Keep the context file accurate and concise.
- Document new files, changed architecture, new dependencies, new commands, and new business rules.
- If a feature is partially implemented, clearly mark it as partial.
- If assumptions are made, document them.
- Follow the existing project architecture unless there is a strong reason to refactor.
- Do not introduce unnecessary dependencies.
- Prefer small, maintainable changes over large rewrites.
- Preserve user/unrelated work in the git tree. Do not revert dirty files unless explicitly asked.
- Keep native deletion and compression changes conservative, test them carefully, and maintain clear user confirmation for destructive actions.

## Release Prep & Security Hardening — 2026-07-12 (current state)

Snapshot for a fresh agent picking up the Play release. Grounded in the actual tree; nothing here is aspirational unless marked PENDING/DEFERRED.

### Security scan
- Full repo audit performed → results in **`SECURITY_SCAN.md`** (repo root). Verdict: **0 Critical · 2 High · 12 Medium · ~18 Low**, no committed secrets, no data-loss path in delete/compress flows. See that file for per-finding detail and file:line refs.

### Security fixes APPLIED (in the tree now)
- **R8/ProGuard on for release** — `android/app/build.gradle` release buildType has `minifyEnabled true` + `shrinkResources true`; keep rules in `android/app/proguard-rules.pro` (RN/Hermes/JNI, RevenueCat, google-mobile-ads, react-native-compressor, expo modules, vendored LAME/webm/audio modules).
- **RevenueCat Test-Store flag gated behind `__DEV__`** — `src/features/subscription/revenuecat.service.ts` `getApiKey()` (`__DEV__ && EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_USE_TEST_STORE === "1"`), so a stale `.env.local` flag can't ship free Pro in release.
- **Ads/GDPR consent fails CLOSED** — `src/features/ads/ads-consent-store.ts` defaults `canRequestAds:false`; `consent.service.ts` returns `false` on any UMP error; `interstitial.service.ts` + `rewarded.service.ts` each check `canRequestAds` before requesting.
- **Cancelled compression no longer persists output** — `src/features/compression/compression.store.ts` `markCompleted` guards the `useAppStore.setState(compressedMedia…)` on post-set job status (no orphan gallery entry / no source exclusion after cancel).
- **Release build fails loudly if `android/keystore.properties` missing** — no silent debug-signing fallback (`android/app/build.gradle`).
- **App Lock PIN hardened** — `src/services/app-lock-service.ts`: PIN stored as **salted SHA-256** (pure-JS `src/utils/sha256.ts`, no native dep) with lazy migration of any legacy plaintext; attempt **rate-limit/backoff** (4 free tries → exponential lockout). `src/components/app-lock-gate.tsx`: **re-locks on return to foreground** after a 15s grace.
- **Open-Source Licenses screen** — new `src/screens/licenses-screen.tsx` (route `app/licenses.tsx`, registered in `app/_layout.tsx`, linked from Settings → Support), credits **LAME (LGPL v2.1)** with a written source offer; i18n keys added across **all 11 locales** (`i18n:check` green).
- Verification: `tsc --noEmit` green; `npm run i18n:check` green.

Removed via `app.json` expo-image-picker `cameraPermission:false` + `AndroidManifest.xml` `<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.CAMERA" tools:node="remove"/>` + iOS plist camera key removed. Confirmed **CAMERA absent** from the built APK/AAB manifest (aapt). Play "Photo/camera" declaration for camera is N/A.

  - **FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC — DEMO VIDEO NOW RECORDED (2026-07-16), declaration still to be submitted.** The blocker (couldn't film it) is gone: `swipeclean-foreground-service-datasync-demo.mp4` (26 s) shows Scan → immediate ongoing notification → app backgrounded → scan continues. Filmed on a REAL Pro entitlement (release build, versionCode 3) — no debug/Test-Store build was needed after all. Intended answers below still apply. Intended answers to finish it later: category = **"Local processing → Other"**; used by **exactly one** thing — the **Smart Clean background library scan** (`src/services/background-smart-clean-scan-worker.ts`, taskName `SwipeCleanScan`; service `com.asterinet.react.bgactions.RNBackgroundActionsTask`, type `dataSync`, from **react-native-background-actions**), which keeps the on-device scan alive while backgrounded/screen-off = **local processing of on-device media, NOT transcoding and NOT network**. Compression runs inline (no FGS) — `compression.store.ts:503`. **Still REQUIRES a demo video** (a real screen recording of the app showing the scan running with its ongoing notification) — currently **blocked** because Smart Clean is Pro-gated (see PENDING/DEFERRED below: needs a debug/Test-Store build or the S24 dev build to film).

### PENDING / DEFERRED (follow-up needed)
- **FGS demo video for the Play declaration — STILL NEEDED.** Smart Clean (the only FGS trigger) is **Pro-gated** (Premium tab renders Studio/Smart Clean only when `isPro`; free users get the paywall — `src/screens/premium-screen.tsx:40`). Can't be filmed on a free/prod build: emulator has no Play Billing, `adb root` refused (Play system image), release APK non-debuggable. To record: use a **debug build** (debuggable, same package `com.cognitix.swipeclean`, needs Metro) and grant Pro via the **RevenueCat Test Store** (`EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_USE_TEST_STORE=1` + `EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_TEST_API_KEY` in `.env.local`) **or** inject `subscriptionStatus:"active"` into AsyncStorage via `adb run-as`. The scan button itself is NOT gated — only the screen hosting it. (`isPro` = `subscriptionStatus === "active"`, `feature-access.service.ts`.)
- **Media-pipeline hardening — DEFERRED** (needs on-device testing; touches Convert/WebM/MP3 + iOS native): stream/chunk audio decode to avoid whole-track PCM OOM (M6), drain-loop timeouts/cancellation on corrupt media (M7, `modules/swipe-clean-webm/.../VideoTranscoder.kt`, `swipe-clean-audio-encode/.../AudioEncodeModule.kt`), iOS non-contiguous `CMBlockBuffer` bounds check (M8). See SECURITY_SCAN.md M6–M8.
- **Native libs 16 KB-page alignment — ✅ RESOLVED & VERIFIED (2026-07-16).** Adding `-Wl,-z,max-page-size=16384` (CMake `target_link_options`) to the one custom-compiled `.so` (`libswipecleanlame.so`) was the ONLY change needed — NDK 27 (both 27.0.12077973 and 27.1.12297006 are installed; RN 0.81 default) does not 16 KB-align app-compiled libs without it. VERIFIED on a fresh signed `:app:assembleRelease` (versionCode 3): `llvm-readelf -l` reports LOAD align `0x4000` for **all 26 arm64-v8a `.so`** — `libswipecleanlame.so` (our fix), RN core (`libhermes`/`libreactnative`/`libjsi`), AND every third-party prebuilt (react-native-compressor, google-mobile-ads, react-native-purchases, fresco, etc.). **No dependency bumps needed.** Zip-level `zipalign -c -P 16 4 app-release.apk` also passes (thanks to `expo.useLegacyPackaging=false`). The earlier emulator "page size compatible mode" warning is cleared; no longer an Android-15 release blocker.
- **LAME iOS static-link LGPL compliance** — unresolved architectural item for any iOS release (LGPL §6 relinkable-objects problem for a signed App Store binary); Android ships LAME as a separate `.so` (defensible). NOTE (2026-08-06): the in-app attribution screen referenced here was subsequently REMOVED — see §10 and §11 entry 2026-08-06 (c). See SECURITY_SCAN.md H1.

## iOS ↔ Android Parity Audit (2026-07-17)

Full read-only, evidence-backed audit of every platform-conditional path. **Android is the reference platform and is fine as-is** — nothing below asks for Android changes (the two "both-platform" bugs are noted for completeness but are not iOS-release blockers). This section exists so a Mac-side agent can make the iOS build shippable. Every claim is verified against code with `file:line`. Where a claim is a well-founded inference rather than something provable from code alone, it says so.

**Headline:** the iOS build is NOT feature-equivalent to Android. Three items are outright non-functional on iOS (paywall, ads, app icon). The rest is graceful degradation. The pattern across the whole codebase: **every `Platform.OS` branch that EXISTS is correct** — the author understood the differences they wrote. The failures are all *absences* — keys never added, icons never generated, plists never regenerated, TODOs never closed. `src/features/ads/ad-config.ts:12` still says "iOS has no native project yet", which is now stale (`ios/` exists and is committed) in a way that hides how far iOS is from shippable.

### HEIC / HEIF — the specific question, answered

**There is NO HEIC special-casing anywhere in the app.** A precise grep (`heic|heif|kUTType|AVFileType|UTType`) returns hits in exactly two spots, both in Convert, neither platform-conditional: `src/features/convert/convert-targets.ts:82` (`heif`→`heic` extension normalize) and `:72` (`heic` as a source token). **Zero** HEIC references in the compression pipeline.

- **Compression of a HEIC photo → JPEG, on BOTH platforms, always.** Output is hardcoded `output: "jpg"` at `src/services/compression-service.ts:316` — unconditional. iOS decodes via `UIImage(contentsOfFile:)` and encodes `jpegData(...)`; the compressor library's `OutputType` enum has only `jpg`/`png` (`node_modules/react-native-compressor/ios/Image/ImageCompressorOptions.swift:4-7`) — **HEIC output is not expressible in this library on either platform**. Android decodes via `BitmapFactory.decodeFile` (HEIF decode requires API 28+) and encodes `CompressFormat.JPEG`.
- **HEIC compression can GROW the file — guard exists and is correct.** A 12 MP iPhone HEIC (~2–3 MB) clears eligibility on PIXELS not size (`compression-service.ts:97-100`, `LARGE_PHOTO_MIN_PIXELS = 8_000_000`), so it's OFFERED. Re-encoding to JPEG at the High profile (`quality 0.9`, `imageMaxSize 4096` — no downscale for a 4032-wide photo) frequently produces a file LARGER than the source HEIC (HEIC ≈ 2× JPEG efficiency). The no-shrink guard catches it — `src/features/compression/compression.service.ts:111-119` fails verification BEFORE `saveToLibrary` and reads the REAL original size (`:84-94`) so a bad estimate can't authorize deleting a smaller original. Correct. **User-visible iOS impact:** iPhone users on High quality will frequently see "This file is already optimized" and get nothing — because the app offered work it can't complete. Android (JPEG camera default) rarely hits this. *(The "usually grows" claim is a well-founded inference about encoder behavior, NOT verified on-device — see NEEDS-DEVICE below.)*
- **HEIC conversion: supported SOURCE on both, NOT a target on either.** `heic` is deliberately kept outside the `ConvertTarget` union (`convert.types.ts:17-22`) so it can't be a target; a HEIC source gets all three image targets — verified by test `convert-targets.test.ts:55-56`. Decode delegated to `expo-image-manipulator`, no branch. Latent asymmetry: HEIC decode is universal on iOS but Android-version-dependent; nothing in the repo tests Android HEIC decode.

### Compression matrix

| | iOS | Android |
|---|---|---|
| Image in → out | any `UIImage` format → **JPEG** | any `BitmapFactory` format (HEIF API 28+) → **JPEG** |
| Video in → out | HEVC/H.264 `.mov` → **H.264/MP4** (`react-native-compressor/ios/Video/VideoMain.swift:313,323`) | H.264/HEVC → H.264/MP4 |
| Profiles / eligibility / no-shrink guard | Identical (`compression-service.ts:22-102`, `compression.service.ts:111`) | Identical |
| Save location / delete-original | Photos via `MediaLibrary.createAssetAsync`; delete-original ✅ | MediaStore, same call; ✅ |

**iPhone HEVC video transcodes correctly to H.264/MP4** — the compression engine is genuinely at parity, zero platform branches. **iOS result-flow gap (verified):** `src/screens/compression-detail-screen.tsx:96` (`if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;`) disables the auto-open of the result viewer, and `src/screens/compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx:39` (`isResultMode = … Platform.OS === "android" …`) means the `CompressionResultSheet` (`:137`) NEVER renders on iOS. So tapping "View Compressed" (`compression-detail-screen.tsx:278`, passes `result=1`) silently ignores the param → plain drag-to-dismiss view, no savings figures / no Keep-Delete on that surface. **BUT degraded, not broken:** the primary flow (`startCompression` → `/compress-run`, `compression-detail-screen.tsx:113`) is platform-neutral, and `CompletedDecisionPanel` (`:271`) renders unconditionally — iOS users DO get results + Keep/Delete, just never the sheet-over-media presentation. Likely just untested-on-iOS caution; needs an iOS back-stack pass because `router.replace` semantics differ.

### Convert (Studio) matrix

Capability probe is platform-BLIND by design (`convert-targets.ts:113-133`; each flag = `requireOptionalNativeModule(...) != null`). Platform behavior falls out of each module's `expo-module.config.json` `platforms` array.

| Target | iOS | Android | Engine |
|---|---|---|---|
| jpg/png/webp/mp4 | ✅ | ✅ | expo-image-manipulator / react-native-compressor |
| m4a / wav | ✅ | ✅ | `SwipeCleanAudioEncode` (`["apple","android"]`) |
| **webm** | ❌ | ✅ | `SwipeCleanWebm` — config `["android"]` only, no `ios/` dir |
| **gif** | ✅ | ❌ | `SwipeCleanGif` — config `["apple"]` only |
| mp3 | ❌ | ❌ | works on both (vendored LAME) but delisted by product decision (`convert-targets.ts:17-21`) |

**Disjoint video-target split:** for a `.mp4` source, iOS offers only **GIF**, Android only **WebM** — neither offers both. Cuts both ways (iOS has GIF Android lacks). Audio output lands in Photos-rejected → sandbox on iOS ("saved to Files") vs MediaStore on Android ("saved to Music"); handled gracefully via `libraryAssetId`, not a platform branch (`convert.service.ts:70-79`, UI keys off it at `convert-run-screen.tsx:163`). Only convert platform branch: `open-media-file.ts:64` (Android "Open with…" chooser vs iOS share sheet). Convert bugs (mostly cross-platform, non-iOS-specific): Android WebM silently drops audio (`VideoTranscoder.kt:11-15`); WAV output differs (iOS hardcodes 44100/stereo `AudioEncodeModule.swift:66-67`, Android preserves source); iOS M4A re-encodes vs Android lossless remux; `convert.store.ts:432` error-classifier misses `webm/gif/audio-*-unavailable`; `gif-engine.ts:7-9` docstring is inverted.

### iOS BLOCKERS — must fix before an iOS release

**Cannot ship (build/submit fails):**
1. **App icon MISSING.** `app.json` has no `icon` key at all; `ios/SwipeClean/Images.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/` contains only `Contents.json` pointing at `App-Icon-1024x1024@1x.png` which **does not exist on disk**. Archive validation fails (ITMS-90713). Because there's no source `icon`, `expo prebuild` can't regenerate it either — a source asset must be added first.
2. **Version mismatch (three-way).** `package.json:3` = `0.1.0`, `app.json:6` = `1.0.0`, `ios/SwipeClean/Info.plist CFBundleShortVersionString` = **`0.1.0`** (stale committed plist; `ios/` is NOT gitignored, only `ios/Pods|build`, so it's authoritative and NOT managed by EAS `autoIncrement`). Android is correct (`versionName 1.0.0`, `versionCode 4`).

**Ships but broken:**
3. **RevenueCat iOS = whole paywall throws.** `EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_IOS_API_KEY` is ABSENT from `.env.local` (declared empty in `.env.example:18`, not in `eas.json`/`app.json extra`). Chain: `getApiKey()`→undefined (`revenuecat.service.ts:166`) → `isUsableKey` false → `configure()` false → **`ensureConfigured()` throws** (`:151-154`); every entry point (`getBillingPlans` `:74`, `purchasePlan` `:89`, `restorePurchases` `:101`, `presentCodeRedemption` `:132`) awaits it first. **No Pro purchase, no restore, no redemption on iOS.**
4. **AdMob iOS = $0, silently.** All three iOS unit IDs are `TestIds` (`ad-config.ts:19-26`, with TODO); `app.json:62-68` `iosAppId` is **Google's public SAMPLE id** `ca-app-pub-3940256099942544~1458002511` (materialized into `Info.plist` `GADApplicationIdentifier`). No TestIds release-assertion exists anywhere.

**App Review rejections:**
5. **No Terms/EULA link on the paywall** — `premium-screen.tsx` has no `Linking` import / no URLs at all. Guideline 3.1.2 (auto-renewing subs). `settings-screen.tsx:338` links only a privacy policy (unbranded Netlify subdomain, `:31`).
6. **`NSMicrophoneUsageDescription`** present with generic boilerplate for an UNUSED permission (Guideline 5.1.1). The author already did the right thing for camera (`app.json:70-75` `cameraPermission:false`) — mic needs the same.
7. **`UIBackgroundModes: audio`** with no background playback (Guideline 2.5.4). Injected by `expo-video`'s `supportsPictureInPicture:true` (`app.json:48-53`) — reconsider that flag.
8. **AdMob privacy manifest contradiction** — no `NSUserTrackingUsageDescription`, no `SKAdNetworkItems`, while `ios/SwipeClean/PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` declares `NSPrivacyTracking:false` + empty collected-data. Contradicts linking the Google Mobile Ads SDK.
9. **`aps-environment: development`** in `SwipeClean.entitlements` (likely vestigial — local notifications don't need it).

### iOS DEGRADATIONS (functional but worse than Android)

- **Smart Clean scan FREEZES when backgrounded.** `smart-clean-store.ts:437` (`useService = Platform.OS === "android" && …`) means iOS runs the scan FOREGROUND-ONLY. The RNBA pod IS installed on iOS (`ios/Podfile.lock:1583`) — it just never invokes the API, so no crash — but `Info.plist` declares only `UIBackgroundModes:[audio]`, no `processing`/`fetch`, so iOS suspends the JS thread within seconds of backgrounding. Android's FGS (`AndroidManifest.xml:44`, `foregroundServiceType="dataSync"`) scans screen-off. Mitigation: interrupted scans persist as `"interrupted"` (`:528`) and auto-resume via `resumeIfInterrupted` (`:239-255`) on next Smart Clean screen mount — but the user must reopen the app AND that screen.
- **Scan notification stuck at "Scanning…"** for ~75% of the run + no progress bar on iOS. `smart-clean-store.ts:200` (`if (Platform.OS !== "android" || !serviceHeld) return;`) drops all intra-detector progress; the progress bar exists only on the RNBA path (`background-smart-clean-scan-worker.ts:50-54`). `sticky:true`/`autoDismiss:false` (`smart-clean-notifications.ts:159-160`) are Android-only semantics — on iOS the Stop-button notification is swipeable, can orphan a running scan.
- **Detectors: all 8 run on both, zero platform branches**, but two are functionally Android-biased WITHOUT a branch: `memes` (`tier3-memes.ts:28-30`) keys on `whatsapp`/`fb_img`/`img-\d{8}-wa\d+`/`Download` album (Android conventions — 3 signals never fire on iOS `IMG_1234.JPG`); `screenshots` (`tier1-screenshots.ts:15`) filename regex never matches iOS but is rescued by the "Screenshots" smart album (`:20-21`, +3) — works on iOS via ONE signal instead of three (RISK: a non-English iOS device may return a localized album title that `/screenshot/i` misses → detection collapses).
- **No compression result sheet on iOS** (item in Compression matrix above).
- **Converted audio only reachable via Files/Share on iOS** (Photos rejects audio), vs Music on Android.

### iOS/Android correctness bugs (both-platform; NOT iOS-release blockers, listed for the Mac agent)
- `app-lock-gate.tsx:242` — the `face` case returns "Use Face ID" UNCONDITIONALLY, so an **Android** face-unlock device shows an Apple trademark. (Hurts Android.) `NSFaceIDUsageDescription` IS present on iOS (`app.json:56-61` + Info.plist) — biometrics otherwise at parity.
- `photo-preview-screen.tsx:59` — `Share.share({ url })` is iOS-only; **Android** shares a URI string not the image. Fix: use `Sharing.shareAsync` (already correct at `open-media-file.ts:41`).
- `compression-deletion.service.ts:12-18` — calls `MediaLibrary.getPermissionsAsync` directly, bypassing `PermissionService.getMediaPermission`, so the Android-14 grant refinement never applies to delete-original.
- Convert error-classifier + inverted gif docstring (above).
- Dead code: `releaseForegroundService` (`smart-clean-store.ts:471-478`) has no caller — the documented "scan yields FGS to compression" was never wired (Android-only impact).

### AT PARITY — need NO iOS work
Swipe/review; stats (`stats-service.ts` pure arithmetic); history (`history-service.ts` pure mapper); selected-photos gallery; review-delete-list; deletion flow (unbranched, `false`-means-denied handling correct on both — `photo-library-service.ts:94-116`); **iOS limited-library handled correctly** via `accessPrivileges` mapping (`permission-service.ts:78-91`) + `presentLimitedPicker` (`:55-61`), no branch needed and none missing; Android-14 granular refinement is correctly iOS-early-returned (`:65`); reminders (DAILY/WEEKLY both); notification permission flow + channels (correctly Android-gated); UMP/GDPR consent (fail-closed, platform-neutral). Photo-library usage strings present + specific (`app.json:12-15,41-47`).

### iOS FIX LIST (for the Mac-side agent; no code changed in this pass)

**(a) Quick wins**
- Add `EXPO_PUBLIC_REVENUECAT_IOS_API_KEY` to `.env.local` / EAS secrets → unblocks the entire iOS paywall (blocker #3).
- Add an `icon` to `app.json` (source asset first) → then `expo prebuild -p ios` regenerates `AppIcon.appiconset` (blocker #1).
- Fix the three-way version mismatch → set `CFBundleShortVersionString` to `1.0.0` (or add `ios.buildNumber` + regenerate) (blocker #2).
- Remove `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` (unused) (reject #6).
- Reconsider `supportsPictureInPicture:true` → drops the unjustified `UIBackgroundModes:audio` (reject #7).
- Add Terms/EULA link to `premium-screen.tsx` (reject #5).
- Add a TestIds release-assertion in `ad-config.ts` so a misconfigured release build fails loudly (blocker #4 guard).
- Fix `app-lock-gate.tsx:242` (Face-ID-on-Android) and `photo-preview-screen.tsx:59` (Android share).
- Fix inverted `gif-engine.ts:7-9` docstring + stale `ad-config.ts:12` comment.
- Extend `convert.store.ts:432` to match `webm-unavailable|gif-unavailable|audio-(mp3|wav)-unavailable`.
- `aps-environment` → production or remove (reject #9).

**(b) Real work**
- Drop the two Android-only compression result-flow gates (`compression-detail-screen.tsx:96`, `compression-media-viewer-screen.tsx:39`) after an iOS back-stack pass.
- Real AdMob iOS: real App ID + unit IDs, `NSUserTrackingUsageDescription`, `SKAdNetworkItems`, reconcile `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` (blocker #4 + reject #8).
- Format-aware image output — the always-JPEG hardcode (`compression-service.ts:316`) is the root of HEIC-grows AND a known TODO; NOTE `react-native-compressor` cannot emit HEIC, so this needs a different lib or a native module. Cheaper interim: HEIC-aware eligibility (exclude efficient formats from High, or lower `imageMaxSize` for HEIC).
- iOS background scan: add `UIBackgroundModes:processing` + `BGProcessingTask`, or accept foreground-only + improve messaging (no like-for-like FGS on iOS).
- Route iOS Smart Clean progress through expo-notifications instead of dropping it (`smart-clean-store.ts:200`).
- Stream the WAV encode / add a duration cap (`AudioEncodeModule.swift:81-93`, see SECURITY_SCAN.md M6); fix CMBlockBuffer non-contiguous read (M8).
- iOS-aware meme/screenshot heuristics (`IMG_\d+`, localization-proof screenshot album).
- iOS WebM or Android GIF to close the disjoint video-target split (Android GIF blocked on vendoring approval).

**(c) Needs a Mac / real device to verify**
- Whether HEIC@0.9 actually grows (premise of the format-aware-output work) — test real iPhone HEIC across all three profiles.
- Whether `AVAssetReaderTrackOutput` ever yields a non-contiguous `CMBlockBuffer` (is M8 latent or live).
- WAV memory ceiling on an older iPhone (is the ~210 MB transient a jetsam kill).
- iOS memory behavior of the thumbnail path (Android OOM was measured, iOS never was).
- Whether the iOS "Screenshots" smart-album title localizes on a non-English device.
- The result-flow change's iOS back-stack behavior with `router.replace`.
